export type Battle = {
  foes: Foe[];
  log: string[];
  turn: "you" | "ally" | "them" | "act";
  /** The action currently playing. Damage lands at hit, and the next actor waits until until. */
  act: { who: "you" | "ally" | "foe"; kind: string; foe: number; aimAlly: boolean; hit: number; until: number; done: boolean } | null;
  foeQueue: number[];
  follow: "" | "ally" | "foes";
  guard: boolean;
  goblin: boolean;
  hunter: boolean;
  poison: number;
  scrambleFails: number;
  phylactery: "none" | "whole" | "broken" | "spent";
  playerStun: boolean;
  skipEnemy: boolean;
  cry: number;
  stance: number;
  taunt: number;
  allyGuard: number;
  round: number;
  fromRole: string;
};

export type Roamer = {
  id: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  family: string;
  tint: string;
  boss?: boolean;
  mini?: boolean;
  def: string;
  level: number;
  ang: number;
  stuck?: number;

  max?: number;
  atk?: number;
  ac?: number;
  xp?: number;
  silver?: number;
  name?: string;
  aggro?: boolean;
  cool?: number;
  tell?: number;
  flash?: number;
  helper?: boolean;
  goblin?: boolean;
  hunter?: boolean;
  age?: number;
  wave?: number;

  spamTag?: string;
  mid?: string;
  midTag?: string;
  big?: string;
  bigTag?: string;
  personality?: string;
  affix?: string;
  rare?: boolean;

  escortSwings?: number;
  /** On its turn at the captive: walking over to swing at them. */
  atEscort?: boolean;
  link?: number;
  casting?: "" | "mid" | "big" | "spam";
  /** fade2 (OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-01 21:20 ET): world ms a festival foe appeared at. Drawing only; never saved, never read by play. */
  spawnAt?: number;
  /** Swing or cast. Drawn on every family, including bosses. */
  act?: "" | "swing" | "cast";
  actFor?: number;
  markX?: number;
  markY?: number;
  markR?: number;
  /** playtest1j [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03: telegraphed attacks]: the whole tell this cast started with (the mark fills over it), the boss phase (2 at or under
   * half HP), how many big moves it has cast, and the pattern of the cast in hand ("nova", "echo", "echoed"). Never saved. */
  tellMax?: number;
  phase?: number;
  bigs?: number;
  pattern?: string;
  /** playtest1k [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03: elemental combos, companion commands]: elemental statuses (poison, wet, chill, the combo
   * cooldown; seconds left) and the world ms a companion's taunt holds it until. Never saved. */
  st?: Status;
  taunt?: number;
  /** playtest1n [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03 15:41 ET: use-the-room combat]: the shove a slash or whirl gives (px/s, s left), a stun (s), the oil soak (s left) and the
   * world s a spike last bit it. Never saved. */
  shoveX?: number;
  shoveY?: number;
  shoveT?: number;
  stun?: number;
  oilT?: number;
  spikeAt?: number;
  homeX?: number;
  homeY?: number;
  hits?: number;
  add?: boolean;
  /** Item 4: a woken chest. The one solo rare (owner-approved exception). */
  mimic?: boolean;
  /** Item 7: tonight's posted stalker, or the pack's rare once the fight starts. */
  bounty?: boolean;
  /** Items 12/14: a festival boss ("harvest" | "krampus"), only on its own night. */
  festival?: string;
  /** Item 14: a naughty-list name (its list id), or the pack's rare once the fight starts. */
  naughty?: string;
  /** Map writer: this roamer opens a Stalker pack (the boss-room guard, or a rift's possible Stalker). */
  pack?: "guard" | "stalker";
  /** Map writer: the boss-room guard's slain key (opened list), carried by the pack's rare once the fight starts. */
  guard?: string;
  /** Map writer: a rift's possible Loot Goblin, waiting to be touched. */
  riftGoblin?: boolean;
  /** True only while the feet actually moved. Idle bodies do not march. */
  moving?: boolean;
};
export type Act = "up" | "down" | "left" | "right" | "use" | "area" | "far" | "drink" | "pause" | "map" | Order;

function defaultKeys(): Record<Act, string> {
  return { up: "KeyW", down: "KeyS", left: "KeyA", right: "KeyD", use: "KeyE", area: "KeyQ", far: "KeyF", drink: "KeyP", pause: "Escape", map: "KeyM", taunt: "KeyZ", guard: "KeyX", focus: "KeyR", stay: "KeyV" };
}

function defaultPad(): Record<Act, number> {
  return { up: 12, down: 13, left: 14, right: 15, use: 0, area: 2, far: 3, drink: 4, pause: 9, map: 8, taunt: 7, guard: 6, focus: 11, stay: 10 };
}

type SaveBlob = {
  cls: ClassId;
  path: StatPath;
  name: string;
  level: number;
  xp: number;
  str: number;
  dex: number;
  int: number;
  sta: number;
  agi: number;
  fort: number;
  hp: number;
  energy: number;
  coin: number;
  bank: number;
  points: number;
  potHp: number;
  potMana: number;
  specials: string[];
  ranks: Record<string, number>;
  worldMs: number;
  mapId: string;
  px: number;
  py: number;
  quest: number;
  bossDead: Record<string, number>;
  dungeon: string;
  floor: number;
  inv: Item[];
  equip: Partial<Record<string, Item>>;
  visited: string[];
  bestiary: string[];
  portal: Portal | null;
  rentedUntil: number;
  hunterBanUntil: number;
  hunterGoneUntil: number;
  barred: Record<string, number>;
  blessing: number;
  fed: Record<string, number>;
  opened: string[];
  entryLevel: number;
  companion: Companion | null;
  favor: Record<string, number>;
  trickDay: Record<string, number>;
  fishPoints?: number;
  inside?: string;
  vault?: Item[];
  ownedHome?: boolean;
  zones?: string[];
  paidZones?: string[];
  fishStep?: number;
  remnantId?: string;
  pagePaid?: boolean;
  campX?: number;
  campY?: number;
  /** playtest1e: 2 once world places are on the twice-size vale. A save without it is migrated on load (migrateWorldSave). */
  worldV?: number;
  drops?: {
    x: number;
    y: number;
    mapId: string;
    dungeon: string;
    floor: number;
    inside: string;
    age: number;
    silver: number;
    item: Item | null;
    told: boolean;
  }[];
};

/** Rank 4 armor belongs to the wearer's class set. */
const SET_NAME: Record<string, string> = {
  plate: "Gallows Plate",

  leather: "Nightleather",
  chain: "Bloodmail",
};

function emptySaves(): Array<SaveBlob | null> {
  return [null, null, null];
}

function nextDay(ms: number) {
  return (Math.floor(ms / CYCLE_MS) + 1) * CYCLE_MS;
}

function nextNight(ms: number) {
  const cyc = Math.floor(ms / CYCLE_MS) * CYCLE_MS;
  const night = cyc + DAY_MS;
  return ms < night ? night : night + CYCLE_MS;
}

/**
 * One run. Pixel position is px/py. The tile under those feet decides water,
 * ice, doors, and whether the weather is allowed to fall.
 */
export class Game {
  mode: Mode = "title";
  cls: ClassId = "warrior";
  /** playtest1: the hero shows the writer's climb frames until this world ms (a stair or ladder just taken). Picture only, not saved. */
  climbUntil = 0;
  /** playtest1 (owner-reported 2026-10-01): "Leave camp?" is up. The world waits while it is. Not saved. */
  askLeave = false;
  path: StatPath = "str";
  name = "Wanderer";
  level = 1;
  xp = 0;
  str = 6;
  dex = 4;
  int = 3;
  sta = 6;
  agi = 4;
  fort = 4;
  hp = 20;
  energy = 10;
  coin = 40;
  bank = 0;
  points = 0;
  fishPoints = 0;
  inside = "";
  vault: Item[] = [];
  ownedHome = false;
  zones: string[] = [];
  paidZones: string[] = [];
  fishStep = 0;
  remnantId = "";
  /** Item 12: the plaza lantern game in progress (not saved: a reload mid-run simply ends it unpaid). */
  lanternRun: { day: number; lit: number[]; left: number } | null = null;
  /** Item 12: a pumpkin bought and part-cut at Hessa's stall (not saved). */
  carving: { day: number; eyes?: string } | null = null;
  /** Ashen Fair: an ember-dance turn in progress (not saved: a reload mid-turn ends it, the turn spent).
   * show: seconds of the barker's flares left; step: braziers walked right; on: the brazier underfoot, or -1. */
  private festCache = new Map<string, unknown>();
  danceRun: { day: number; turn: number; order: number[]; show: number; step: number; left: number; on: number } | null = null;
  pagePaid = false;
  campX = 0;
  campY = 0;
  campWalk = 0;
  campGoal: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
  /** Loot on the ground. Walk onto it. A full pack leaves the piece where it fell. */
  drops: {
    x: number;
    y: number;
    mapId: string;
    dungeon: string;
    floor: number;
    inside: string;
    age: number;
    silver: number;
    item: Item | null;
    told: boolean;
  }[] = [];
  private doorCool = 0;
  private roomKey = "";
  inv: Item[] = [];
  equip: Partial<Record<string, Item>> = {};
  potHp = 2;
  potMana = 1;
  /** Whirl spends this. The outer stick ring sets running. */
  stam = 40;
  running = false;
  /** Vampire blood. Whirl and Smite spend it. Everyone else spends stamina and mana. */
  blood = 20;
  /** Smite cannot break another big move until this world time. */
  smiteLock = 0;
  /** Hit recovery. The portrait flickers while this is up. */
  iframe = 0;
  /** Swing or cast. The body stays on the ground. Only the arm moves. */
  actPose: "" | "swing" | "cast" = "";
  actFor = 0;
  spellCool = 0;
  cryUntil = 0;
  cryMul = 1;
  wardUntil = 0;
  /** Short visual bursts: chips, dust, ash, ticks. Fixed pool, never saved. See particles.ts. */
  readonly fx = new ParticlePool();
  /** A bolt or a ring. It fades. It does not linger. */
  /** A drawn spell. Picture only: nothing in play reads it. playtest1: max is its whole life, so draw.ts can run the
   * flight, then the impact flash. */
  spells: { x: number; y: number; tx: number; ty: number; life: number; max?: number; kind: "bolt" | "ring" | "nova" | "mend" | "cone"; color: string }[] = [];
  /** Death toast. Then slot 1 reloads, or town if that slot is empty. */
  fallen = 0;
  darkUntil = 0;
  nums: { x: number; y: number; text: string; life: number }[] = [];
  specials: string[] = [];
  ranks: Record<string, number> = {};
  blessing = 0;
  worldMs = 0;
  mapId = "town";
  px = 200;
  py = 200;
  facing: Facing = "s";
  w = 10;
  h = 10;
  tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike> = new Uint8Array(100);
  fog: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike> | null = null;
  theme = "town";
  npcs: Npc[] = [];
  roamers: Roamer[] = [];
  critters: Critter[] = [];
  frame = 0;
  shake = 0;
  shakeMul = 0.65;
  /** Short battle poses. Damage is already applied. This only plays the swing or the cast. */
  battleFx: { who: "you" | "ally" | "foe"; kind: "swing" | "spell" | "shot" | "heal" | "buff"; foe: number; aim: "you" | "ally" | "foes"; at: number }[] = [];
  /** Whole-number zoom only. 4× is the default. Never a fractional scale. */
  zoom = 4;
  logLine = "";
  combatLog = true;
  talk: { who: string; text: string; role: string; npcId: string } | null = null;
  shopRole = "";
  stock: Item[] = [];
  battle: Battle | null = null;
  bossDead: Record<string, number> = {};
  floor = 1;
  dungeon = "";
  floors: Record<string, { tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>; w: number; h: number; fog: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>; feats?: FloorFeats; gen?: MapResult }> = {};
  /** Rune door and secret wall on this floor, if it has them. Read by draw.ts. */
  feats: FloorFeats | null = null;
  /** Glyphs lit so far on this floor's brazier door, in the order lit. */
  runeLit: number[] = [];
  /** Secret-room tiles not yet opened. draw.ts paints them as plain rock until they open. */
  hidden = new Set<number>();
  /** Pressure plates on this floor, by index into feats.traps. tell > 0 is a live mark. Read by draw.ts. */
  plates: { tell: number; cool: number }[] = [];
  private trapIndex = new Map<number, number>();
  /** Index into feats.traps for the trap on tile i, or -1. */
  trapAt(i: number) {
    return this.trapIndex.get(i) ?? -1;
  }
  /** Floor curse drawn for this run, or "". It holds on every floor but the boss floor. See runs.ts. */
  curse: CurseId | "" = "";
  /** Ashen curse: fallen foes about to burst. tell > 0 is a live mark. Read by draw.ts. */
  ashes: { x: number; y: number; tell: number }[] = [];
  /** Rescue: the captive on this floor, bound to the stake or freed and at your back. Read by draw.ts. */
  escort: { id: string; x: number; y: number; state: "bound" | "follow" | "down"; moving?: boolean; hp?: number; max?: number; flash?: number } | null = null;
  quest = 0;
  held = new Set<string>();
  stickX = 0;
  stickY = 0;
  goal: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
  audio: AudioBus | null = null;
  entrances: Record<string, string> = {};
  visited: string[] = [];
  bestiary: string[] = [];
  portal: Portal | null = null;
  /**
   * OWNER-APPROVED EXCEPTION 2026-10-01: MAP WRITER. Where a rift took you from: the Portal record, kept apart from
   * the town portal so one never overwrites the other. Not saved: a save inside a rift is written at this spot.
   */
  riftReturn: Portal | null = null;
  /** The day-night cycle the rift you stand in was opened on (its pocket rerolls each cycle). */
  riftCycle = 0;
  /** A rift mouth you were set down on: it does not take you again until you step off it. */
  riftHold = "";
  /** playtest1f [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1f portals]: a wayrift journey under way (the swirl closes for
   * WAYRIFT.out s, you move to the far rift's front, it opens for WAYRIFT.back s). Never saved: it is over in a second. */
  travel: { t: number; from: string; to: string; moved: boolean } | null = null;
  /** The rift you just stepped out of: it will not take you back until your feet are off its mouth and front. */
  wayHold = "";
  private swirlCool = 0;
  private bossRoomNoted = "";
  /** Map writer: the generated floor you stand on (rooms, boss spot), or null on a hand-made floor. Read by checks and the headless run. */
  gen: MapResult | null = null;
  rentedUntil = 0;
  hunterBanUntil = 0;
  hunterGoneUntil = 0;
  barred: Record<string, number> = {};
  fed: Record<string, number> = {};
  entryLevel = 1;
  companion: Companion | null = null;
  /** playtest1k [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03: elemental combos, companion commands]: combo name pops (drawn in the pixel font), the companion's
   * orders (stay, the focus target, the guard, each order's cooldown, world ms) and the foe you last hit. Never saved. */
  pops: { x: number; y: number; text: string; row: "red" | "blue" | "gold" | "violet"; life: number }[] = [];
  allyStay = false;
  allyStayAt = "";
  allyFocus: Roamer | null = null;
  focusUntil = 0;
  guardUntil = 0;
  orderCool: Record<Order, number> = { taunt: 0, guard: 0, focus: 0, stay: 0 };
  private lastHit: Roamer | null = null;
  private castCombos = 0;
  /** playtest1n [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03 15:41 ET: use-the-room combat]: this floor's props, fire and oil patches, falling pillars and lanterns (room.ts). Placed from the
   * floor's seed on every load and never saved. */
  room: RoomState = emptyRoom();
  /** The combo names this cast set off (an area spell's log names them). */
  private castNames: string[] = [];
  favor: Record<string, number> = {};
  trickDay: Record<string, number> = {};
  opener: "strike" | "cleave" | "ranged" = "strike";
  private opened = new Set<string>();
  private linger = 0;
  private calm = 0;
  private walk = 0;
  private mood = "";
  private hunterPhase = "";
  private pending: Roamer | null = null;
  private goalStuck = 0;
  private warnedBridge = false;
  private seenBiome = "";
  private folkKey = "";
  private lastBar = "";
  private weatherMark = "";
  private thunderMark = -1;
  private pausedFrom: Mode | null = null;
  private stuckT = 0;
  private padWas = new Array<boolean>(16).fill(false);
  private padStick = false;
  private padHeld = new Set<string>();
  moving = false;
  wading = false;
  sliding = false;
  /** Item 11 Watchful: traps already called out on this floor (not saved; a new floor starts fresh). */
  private trapsCalled = new Set<string>();
  /** Seconds to the next derby settle check (item 9). */
  private derbyClock = 0;
  fishMark = 0;
  fishDir = 1;
  fishZone = 28;
  fishBait = "";
  fishLine = "";
  private slideX = 0;
  private slideY = 0;
  private notedWade = false;
  private notedSlide = false;
  keyBind: Record<Act, string> = defaultKeys();
  padBind: Record<Act, number> = defaultPad();
  captureAct: Act | null = null;
  padName = "";

  constructor() {
    this.loadBinds();
  }

  loadBinds() {
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BIND_KEY) || "{}") as { keys?: Partial<Record<Act, string>>; pad?: Partial<Record<Act, number>> };
      this.keyBind = { ...defaultKeys(), ...(raw.keys ?? {}) };
      this.padBind = { ...defaultPad(), ...(raw.pad ?? {}) };
    } catch {
      this.keyBind = defaultKeys();
      this.padBind = defaultPad();
    }
  }

  private storeBinds() {
    localStorage.setItem(BIND_KEY, JSON.stringify({ keys: this.keyBind, pad: this.padBind }));
  }

  bindKey(act: Act, code: string) {
    if (!code) return;
    this.keyBind[act] = code;
    this.captureAct = null;
    this.storeBinds();
  }

  bindPad(act: Act, button: number) {
    this.padBind[act] = button;
    this.captureAct = null;
    this.storeBinds();
  }

  saveBrief(slot: number) {
    const raw = this.readSaves()[slot];
    if (!raw) return "Empty";
    return `${raw.name} · ${CLASSES[raw.cls]?.label ?? raw.cls} · Lv ${raw.level}`;
  }

  pollPad() {
    const pads = navigator.getGamepads?.() ?? [];
    let pad: Gamepad | null = null;
    for (const p of pads) if (p && p.connected) pad = p;
    this.padName = pad ? pad.id.replace(/\(.*/, "").trim() : "";
    if (!pad) {
      if (this.padHeld.size) {
        for (const code of this.padHeld) this.held.delete(code);
        this.padHeld.clear();
      }
      if (this.padStick) {
        this.stickX = 0;
        this.stickY = 0;
        this.padStick = false;
      }
      return;
    }
    const pressed = pad.buttons.map((b) => !!(b && (b.pressed || b.value > 0.5)));
    if (this.captureAct) {
      const found = pressed.findIndex((on, i) => on && !this.padWas[i]);
      this.padWas = pressed;
      if (found >= 0) this.bindPad(this.captureAct, found);
      return;
    }
    const lx = pad.axes[0] ?? 0;
    const ly = pad.axes[1] ?? 0;
    if (Math.hypot(lx, ly) > 0.28) {
      this.stickX = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, lx));
      this.stickY = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ly));
      this.padStick = true;
    } else if (this.padStick) {
      this.stickX = 0;
      this.stickY = 0;
      this.padStick = false;
    }
    const edge = (act: Act) => {
      const b = this.padBind[act];
      return b >= 0 && pressed[b] && !this.padWas[b];
    };
    if (edge("use")) this.fireAct("use");
    if (edge("area")) this.fireAct("area");
    if (edge("far")) this.fireAct("far");
    if (edge("drink")) this.fireAct("drink");
    if (edge("pause")) this.fireAct("pause");
    if (edge("map")) this.fireAct("map");
    for (const o of ORDER_ACTS) if (edge(o)) this.fireAct(o);
    const hold = (act: Act, code: string) => {
      const b = this.padBind[act];
      const on = b >= 0 && !!pressed[b];
      if (on) {
        this.held.add(code);
        this.padHeld.add(code);
      } else if (this.padHeld.has(code)) {
        this.padHeld.delete(code);
        this.held.delete(code);
      }
    };
    hold("up", this.keyBind.up);
    hold("down", this.keyBind.down);
    hold("left", this.keyBind.left);
    hold("right", this.keyBind.right);
    this.padWas = pressed.slice();
  }

  /** Freeze the current mode, including a fight or a cast, and come back to it. */
  togglePause() {
    if (this.mode === "pause") {
      this.mode = this.pausedFrom ?? "play";
      this.pausedFrom = null;
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "dead" || this.mode === "map" || this.mode === "level") return;
    this.pausedFrom = this.mode;
    this.mode = "pause";
  }

  private fireAct(act: Act) {
    if (this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "dead") return;
    // playtest1: while "Leave camp?" is up the pad drives its two buttons (the UI pad layer), not the hero.
    if (this.askLeave) return;
    if (this.mode === "fish") {
      if (act === "use") this.reel();
      else if (act === "pause") this.togglePause();
      else if (act === "map") this.mode = "play";
      return;
    }
    if (act === "pause") {
      if (this.mode === "map") this.mode = "play";
      else this.togglePause();
      return;
    }
    if (act === "map") {
      if (this.mode === "play") this.mode = "map";
      else if (this.mode === "map") this.mode = "play";
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "battle") {
      if (this.battle?.turn === "ally") {
        const opts = this.allyOptions();
        if (act === "use") this.command("strike");
        if (act === "area") this.command("guard");
        if (act === "far") this.command(opts[0] ?? "strike");
        if (act === "drink") this.command(opts[1] ?? "guard");
        return;
      }
      if (act === "use") this.command("strike");
      if (act === "area") this.command("cleave");
      if (act === "far") this.command("ranged");
      if (act === "drink") this.command("potion");
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    if (act === "taunt" || act === "guard" || act === "focus" || act === "stay") this.order(act);
    if (act === "use") this.interact();
    if (act === "area") this.engage("cleave");
    if (act === "far") this.engage("ranged");
    if (act === "drink") this.usePotion();
  }

  get phase(): Phase {
    return this.worldMs % CYCLE_MS < DAY_MS ? "day" : "night";
  }

  get maxHp() {
    return 16 + this.sta * 4 + this.level * 2;
  }

  /**
   * [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02 Bill] (playtest 01:05 ET, "mana runs out after ~2 spells"): the pool is the old formula
   * times MANA.pool, so a fresh wizard holds 83 MP (was 33): 6 Smites (12 MP) from full and most of a 7th, 13 arts (6 MP).
   * Spell costs and damage are unchanged.
   */
  get maxEnergy() {
    if (this.cls === "vampire") return 0;
    const base = this.cls === "wizard" ? 8 + this.int * 3 + this.level : this.cls === "assassin" ? 8 + this.dex * 2 + this.level : 8 + this.str * 2 + this.level;
    return Math.round(base * MANA.pool);
  }

  get atk() {
    const weapon = this.equip.main?.atk ?? 2;
    const primary =
      this.cls === "wizard"
        ? this.int
        : this.cls === "assassin"
          ? this.dex
          : this.cls === "vampire"
            ? this.path === "int"
              ? this.int
              : this.path === "dex"
                ? this.dex
                : this.str
            : this.str;
    let gear = 0;
    for (const it of Object.values(this.equip)) {
      if (!it || it.kind === "armor") continue;
      gear += (it.bonus ?? 0) + (it.gemBonus ?? 0);
    }
    return weapon + primary + Math.floor(this.level / 2) + gear + (this.battle?.cry ?? 0) + this.setAtk();
  }

  get ac() {
    let worn = Math.floor(this.fort / 4) + (this.battle?.stance ?? 0);
    for (const it of Object.values(this.equip)) {
      if (!it) continue;
      worn += it.ac ?? 0;
      if (it.kind === "armor") worn += (it.bonus ?? 0) + (it.gemBonus ?? 0);
    }
    return worn + this.wardAura() + this.setAc();
  }

  xpNeed() {
    return 20 + this.level * 12;
  }

  /** Rank 4 sets. Two worn pieces add armor. Four add attack. */
  private setAc() {
    let n = 0;
    for (const c of Object.values(this.setCounts())) if (c >= 2) n += 2;
    return n;
  }

  private setAtk() {
    let n = 0;
    for (const c of Object.values(this.setCounts())) if (c >= 4) n += 2;
    return n;
  }

  private setCounts() {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const it of Object.values(this.equip)) {
      if (it?.set) counts[it.set] = (counts[it.set] ?? 0) + 1;
    }
    return counts;
  }

  purse() {
    let s = this.coin;
    const plat = Math.floor(s / 100);
    s -= plat * 100;
    const gold = Math.floor(s / 10);
    s -= gold * 10;
    return `${plat}p ${gold}g ${s}s`;
  }

  clockLabel() {
    const into = this.worldMs % CYCLE_MS;
    const t = into < DAY_MS ? into : into - DAY_MS;
    const m = Math.floor(t / 60000);
    const sec = Math.floor((t % 60000) / 1000);
    return `${this.phase} ${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(sec).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }

  seasonName() {
    const day = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
    return SEASONS[Math.floor(day / 6) % SEASONS.length];
  }

  get weather(): WeatherId {
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") return "still";
    return seasonWeather(this.worldMs);
  }

  /** Item 13: the season of the world clock (autumn, winter, spring, summer of the dead; six days each). */
  season(): SeasonId {
    return seasonAt(this.worldMs);
  }

  /** HUD line: the season and the day inside it. */
  seasonLine() {
    return `${SEASON.names[this.season()]} · day ${dayInSeason(this.worldMs) + 1} of ${SEASON.days}`;
  }

  /** The season's festival if one is live tonight (Harvest Moon and Krampusnacht are built; spring and summer are hooks only). */
  festivalTonight() {
    if (this.phase !== "night") return null;
    const hook = festivalHook(Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS));
    return hook && LIVE_FESTIVALS.includes(hook.id) ? hook : null;
  }

  // ---- Festivals (items 12 and 14), on the season hooks. State rides in opened:
  // fest:<harvest|krampus>:<day>:down, fest:harvest:<day>:carved, fest:harvest:<day>:lanterns, fest:harvest:<day>:lit:<i>,
  // fest:krampus:<day>:caught:<list id>, fest:krampus:<day>:warned. Old saves simply have none.

  private festDay() {
    return Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
  }

  /** Tonight's live festival id ("harvest" | "krampus"), or "" (by day, or on an unbuilt hook). */
  festivalId(): string {
    return this.festivalTonight()?.id ?? "";
  }

  /** The live festival whose night falls on today's cycle (by day this is the coming night). */
  festivalToday(): string {
    const h = festivalHook(this.festDay());
    return h && LIVE_FESTIVALS.includes(h.id) ? h.id : "";
  }

  festivalBossDown(id: string) {
    return this.opened.has(`fest:${id}:${this.festDay()}:down`);
  }

  /** Tonight's naughty list (Krampusnacht only). */
  naughtyTonight(): Naughty[] {
    if (this.festivalId() !== "krampus") return [];
    const { lairs, w } = this.lairs();
    return naughtyList(this.festDay(), lairs, w, (x, y) => familiesFor(x, y, "world"), WORLD_BOSSES.map((b) => ({ x: b.tx, y: b.ty })));
  }

  naughtyCaught(id: string) {
    return this.opened.has(`fest:krampus:${this.festDay()}:caught:${id}`);
  }

  /** Tonight's Drowned Court (Drowned Bloom only): named Stalker packs on the flood. The Tzar is not among them. */
  courtTonight(): Naughty[] {
    if (this.festivalId() !== "bloom") return [];
    const { lairs, w } = this.lairs();
    return this.festMemo(`court:${this.festDay()}`, () => courtList(this.festDay(), lairs, w, (x, y) => familiesFor(x, y, "world"), WORLD_BOSSES.map((b) => ({ x: b.tx, y: b.ty }))));
  }

  /** Tonight's sideshow (Ashen Fair only): named Stalker packs in the Cinder. */
  sideshowTonight(): Naughty[] {
    if (this.festivalId() !== "ashen") return [];
    const { lairs, w } = this.lairs();
    return this.festMemo(`sideshow:${this.festDay()}`, () => sideshowList(this.festDay(), lairs, w, (x, y) => familiesFor(x, y, "world"), WORLD_BOSSES.map((b) => ({ x: b.tx, y: b.ty }))));
  }

  /** Tonight's named festival packs, whichever festival it is (the naughty list, the court, the sideshow). */
  festivalPacks(): Naughty[] {
    const id = this.festivalId();
    return id === "krampus" ? this.naughtyTonight() : id === "bloom" ? this.courtTonight() : id === "ashen" ? this.sideshowTonight() : [];
  }

  /** A named festival pack struck tonight (fest:<festival>:<day>:caught:<list id>). */
  packCaught(id: string) {
    const fest = id.startsWith("court-") ? "bloom" : id.startsWith("sideshow-") ? "ashen" : "krampus";
    return this.opened.has(`fest:${fest}:${this.festDay()}:caught:${id}`);
  }

  /** Tonight's blooms on the flood (Drowned Bloom only), off the court's lairs. */
  bloomsTonight() {
    if (this.festivalId() !== "bloom") return [];
    const { lairs, w } = this.lairs();
    return this.festMemo(`bloom:${this.festDay()}`, () => bloomSpots(this.festDay(), lairs, w, this.courtTonight(), WORLD_BOSSES.map((b) => ({ x: b.tx, y: b.ty }))));
  }

  /** Tonight's picks are pure (seeded by the day over fixed lairs), so each is worked out once per night. */
  private festMemo<T>(key: string, make: () => T): T {
    if (!this.festCache.has(key)) {
      if (this.festCache.size > 12) this.festCache.clear();
      this.festCache.set(key, make());
    }
    return this.festCache.get(key) as T;
  }

  bloomPicked(i: number) {
    return this.opened.has(`fest:bloom:${this.festDay()}:picked:${i}`);
  }

  /** Drawn by draw.ts: is this world tile under tonight's flood? Ground only; roads, water and mouths stay as they are. */
  floodAt(x: number, y: number) {
    if (this.mapId !== "world" || this.festivalId() !== "bloom" || !inFlood(x, y)) return false;
    const t = this.tiles[y * this.w + x];
    return t === T.grass || t === T.dirt || t === T.swamp || t === T.ash || t === T.bone;
  }

  /** Ember-dance turns spent tonight. */
  danceTurns() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    let n = 0;
    while (n < ASHEN_FAIR.turns && this.opened.has(`fest:ashen:${day}:turn:${n + 1}`)) n++;
    return n;
  }

  /** Drawn by draw.ts: the brazier the barker is flaring now (watch phase), or -1, and the braziers walked right. */
  danceFlare(): { now: number; done: number[] } {
    const run = this.danceRun;
    if (!run) return { now: -1, done: [] };
    if (run.show > 0) {
      const k = Math.min(run.order.length - 1, Math.floor((ASHEN_FAIR.steps * ASHEN_FAIR.showSeconds - run.show) / ASHEN_FAIR.showSeconds));
      return { now: run.order[k], done: [] };
    }
    return { now: -1, done: run.order.slice(0, run.step) };
  }

  /** Lanterns lit tonight, by index into HARVEST.lanterns. */
  lanternsLit(): number[] {
    const day = this.festDay();
    const run = this.lanternRun?.day === day ? this.lanternRun.lit : [];
    return HARVEST.lanterns.map((_, i) => i).filter((i) => run.includes(i) || this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${day}:lit:${i}`));
  }

  /** The festival boss and the naughty list walk the world only on their own night. */
  private spawnFestival() {
    const id = this.festivalId();
    if (id !== "harvest" && id !== "krampus" && id !== "bloom" && id !== "ashen") return;
    if ((id === "harvest" || id === "krampus") && !this.festivalBossDown(id) && !this.roamers.some((r) => r.festival === id) && this.pending?.festival !== id) {
      const def = FESTIVAL_BOSSES.find((b) => b.id === (id === "harvest" ? "pumpkinlord" : "krampus"))!;
      const at = id === "harvest" ? HARVEST.lord : KRAMPUSNACHT.spot;
      const spot = this.openNear(at.x * TILE + 8, at.y * TILE + 8);
      this.roamers.push({ id: def.id, x: spot.x, y: spot.y, family: def.family, tint: def.tint, boss: true, def: def.id, level: Math.max(at.lv, zoneLevel(at.x, at.y, this.level)), ang: 0, festival: id, name: def.name });
      this.roamers[this.roamers.length - 1].spawnAt = this.worldMs; // fade2 (OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-01 21:20 ET): spawn-time tag, read only by draw.ts
    }
    // The named packs: Krampus's naughty list, the Drowned Court, the Ashen Fair sideshow. No boss among them.
    for (const n of this.festivalPacks()) {
      if (this.packCaught(n.id) || this.roamers.some((r) => r.naughty === n.id)) continue;
      const def = monsterById(n.fam);
      this.roamers.push({ id: n.id, x: n.x * TILE + 8, y: n.y * TILE + 8, family: def.family, tint: def.tint, def: def.id, level: zoneLevel(n.x, n.y, this.level), ang: 0, naughty: n.id, name: n.name, affix: n.affix });
      this.roamers[this.roamers.length - 1].spawnAt = this.worldMs; // fade2 (OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-01 21:20 ET): spawn-time tag, read only by draw.ts
    }
  }

  /** Festival loot on a kill: coal and gift sacks roll the existing junk row and chest table. */
  private festivalFell(r: Roamer) {
    const day = this.festDay();
    if (r.naughty && r.rare && !r.naughty.startsWith("naughty-")) {
      // The court and the sideshow: a junk-row keepsake, and half the time one roll of the chest table.
      const court = r.naughty.startsWith("court-");
      this.opened.add(`fest:${court ? "bloom" : "ashen"}:${day}:caught:${r.naughty}`);
      this.dropItem(r.x - 4, r.y + 10, makeItem({ ...(court ? DROWNED_BLOOM.seal : ASHEN_FAIR.ticket), stack: 1 }));
      const box = Math.random() < (court ? DROWNED_BLOOM.cofferChance : ASHEN_FAIR.prizeChance);
      if (box) this.dropItem(r.x + 12, r.y + 10, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
      const left = this.festivalPacks().filter((n) => !this.packCaught(n.id)).length;
      this.logLine = court
        ? `${this.logLine} The court is one short: a court seal${box ? " and a drowned coffer" : ""}. ${left} left.`
        : `${this.logLine} The sideshow is one act short: a fair ticket${box ? " and a prize box" : ""}. ${left} left.`;
    } else if (r.naughty && r.rare) {
      this.opened.add(`fest:krampus:${day}:caught:${r.naughty}`);
      this.dropItem(r.x - 4, r.y + 10, makeItem({ ...KRAMPUSNACHT.coal, stack: 1 }));
      const sack = Math.random() < KRAMPUSNACHT.sackChance;
      if (sack) this.dropItem(r.x + 12, r.y + 10, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
      const left = this.naughtyTonight().filter((n) => !this.naughtyCaught(n.id)).length;
      this.logLine = `${this.logLine} Struck from the list: coal${sack ? " and a gift sack" : ""}. ${left} left.`;
    }
    if (r.festival && r.boss) {
      this.opened.add(`fest:${r.festival}:${day}:down`);
      if (r.festival === "krampus") {
        this.dropItem(r.x - 10, r.y + 12, makeItem({ ...KRAMPUSNACHT.coal, stack: KRAMPUSNACHT.krampusCoal }));
        this.dropItem(r.x + 14, r.y + 12, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
        this.logLine = `${this.logLine} The basket spills: coal, and a gift sack.`;
      } else this.logLine = `${this.logLine} The Harvest Moon dims over the patch.`;
    }
  }

  /** Hessa's stall stands in the plaza on Harvest Moon night; the lantern game runs; Krampus is heard first. */
  private tickFestival(dt: number) {
    if (this.mapId === "town") {
      // One festival keeper at the plaza stall: Hessa (Harvest Moon), Ottla (Drowned Bloom), Sallow (Ashen Fair).
      const fid = this.festivalId();
      const keeper = fid === "harvest" ? HARVEST.npc : fid === "bloom" ? DROWNED_BLOOM.npc : fid === "ashen" ? ASHEN_FAIR.npc : null;
      if (this.npcs.some((n) => n.role === "festival" && n.id !== keeper?.id)) this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => n.role !== "festival");
      if (keeper && !this.npcs.some((n) => n.role === "festival")) this.npcs.push({ id: keeper.id, name: keeper.name, x: HARVEST.stall.x * TILE + 8, y: HARVEST.stall.y * TILE + 8, coat: "#888888", role: "festival", look: keeper.look });
    }
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.festivalId() === "bloom" && this.mode === "play") this.pickBlooms();
    if (this.danceRun) this.tickDance(dt);
    const run = this.lanternRun;
    if (run) {
      if (this.mapId !== "town" || this.festDay() !== run.day) {
        this.endLanternRun();
        return;
      }
      run.left -= dt;
      HARVEST.lanterns.forEach(([x, y], i) => {
        if (run.lit.includes(i) || Math.hypot(this.px - (x * TILE + 8), this.py - (y * TILE + 8)) > HARVEST.touch) return;
        run.lit.push(i);
        this.opened.add(`fest:harvest:${run.day}:lit:${i}`);
        this.logLine = `Lantern ${run.lit.length} of ${HARVEST.lanterns.length} catches.`;
        this.audio?.coin();
      });
      if (run.lit.length === HARVEST.lanterns.length || run.left <= 0) this.endLanternRun();
    }
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.festivalId() === "krampus") {
      const k = this.roamers.find((r) => r.festival === "krampus" && r.boss);
      const key = `fest:krampus:${this.festDay()}:warned`;
      if (k && !this.opened.has(key) && Math.hypot(k.x - this.px, k.y - this.py) < KRAMPUSNACHT.warn) {
        this.opened.add(key);
        this.logLine = "A birch switch hisses in the snow. Something with a basket is counting names.";
      }
    }
  }

  /** Drowned Bloom: walk onto a bloom on the flood to pick it (a junk-row bloom for Ottla). */
  private pickBlooms() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    this.bloomsTonight().forEach((b, i) => {
      if (this.bloomPicked(i) || Math.hypot(this.px - (b.x * TILE + 8), this.py - (b.y * TILE + 8)) > DROWNED_BLOOM.touch) return;
      this.opened.add(`fest:bloom:${day}:picked:${i}`);
      this.gainItem(makeItem({ ...DROWNED_BLOOM.bloom, stack: 1 }));
      const got = this.bloomsTonight().filter((_, k) => this.bloomPicked(k)).length;
      this.logLine = `Drowned bloom ${got} of ${DROWNED_BLOOM.blooms}. Weir-wife Ottla buys them in the plaza.`;
      this.audio?.coin();
    });
  }

  /** Ottla takes every drowned bloom you carry; all six of tonight's handed in earns the garland. */
  private handBlooms() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    const stack = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "junk" && i.name === DROWNED_BLOOM.bloom.name);
    if (this.festivalId() !== "bloom" || !stack) return;
    const n = stack.stack ?? 1;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i !== stack);
    const pay = n * DROWNED_BLOOM.bloomPay;
    this.coin += pay;
    let handed = 0;
    while (this.opened.has(`fest:bloom:${day}:handed:${handed + 1}`)) handed++;
    for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) this.opened.add(`fest:bloom:${day}:handed:${handed + k}`);
    const picked = this.bloomsTonight().filter((_, k) => this.bloomPicked(k)).length;
    let line = `Ottla takes ${n} bloom${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}: ${pay} silver.`;
    if (picked === DROWNED_BLOOM.blooms && handed + n >= DROWNED_BLOOM.blooms && !this.opened.has(`fest:bloom:${day}:garland`)) {
      this.opened.add(`fest:bloom:${day}:garland`);
      this.dropItem(this.px + 12, this.py + 6, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
      line = `${line} All six: she braids a garland and presses a gift on you.`;
    }
    if (this.talk) this.talk.text = line;
    this.logLine = line;
  }

  /** Ashen Fair: pay for a turn; the barker flares the night's braziers in this turn's order. */
  private startDance() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    const turn = this.danceTurns() + 1;
    if (this.festivalId() !== "ashen" || this.danceRun || turn > ASHEN_FAIR.turns) return;
    if (this.coin < ASHEN_FAIR.turnPrice) {
      this.logLine = `A turn is ${ASHEN_FAIR.turnPrice} silver.`;
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= ASHEN_FAIR.turnPrice;
    this.opened.add(`fest:ashen:${day}:turn:${turn}`);
    this.danceRun = { day, turn, order: danceOrder(day, turn), show: ASHEN_FAIR.steps * ASHEN_FAIR.showSeconds, step: 0, left: ASHEN_FAIR.runSeconds, on: -1 };
    this.talk = null;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = `Watch the braziers. ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps} flares, then walk them in order.`;
  }

  private tickDance(dt: number) {
    const run = this.danceRun;
    if (!run) return;
    if (this.mapId !== "town" || this.festDay() !== run.day) {
      this.endDance(false);
      return;
    }
    if (run.show > 0) {
      run.show -= dt;
      if (run.show <= 0) {
        // Standing on a brazier when the flares stop is not a step: only stepping onto one counts.
        run.on = this.brazierUnder();
        this.logLine = `Now walk them: ${ASHEN_FAIR.runSeconds} seconds.`;
      }
      return;
    }
    run.left -= dt;
    const under = this.brazierUnder();
    if (under !== run.on) {
      run.on = under;
      if (under >= 0 && under === run.order[run.step]) {
        run.step += 1;
        this.logLine = `Step ${run.step} of ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps}.`;
        this.audio?.coin();
      } else if (under >= 0 && !run.order.slice(0, run.step).includes(under)) {
        this.endDance(true);
        return;
      }
    }
    if (run.step >= ASHEN_FAIR.steps || run.left <= 0) this.endDance(false);
  }

  /** The plaza post (brazier) under the hero's feet, or -1. */
  private brazierUnder() {
    return HARVEST.lanterns.findIndex(([x, y]) => Math.hypot(this.px - (x * TILE + 8), this.py - (y * TILE + 8)) <= ASHEN_FAIR.touch);
  }

  private endDance(wrong: boolean) {
    const run = this.danceRun;
    if (!run) return;
    this.danceRun = null;
    const perfect = run.step >= ASHEN_FAIR.steps;
    const pay = run.step * ASHEN_FAIR.stepPay + (perfect ? ASHEN_FAIR.perfectBonus : 0);
    this.coin += pay;
    const prizeKey = `fest:ashen:${run.day}:prize`;
    const prize = perfect && !this.opened.has(prizeKey);
    if (prize) {
      this.opened.add(prizeKey);
      this.dropItem(this.px + 12, this.py + 6, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
    }
    this.logLine = perfect
      ? `All four, in order. Sallow pays ${pay} silver${prize ? " and hands down a fair prize" : ""}.`
      : wrong
        ? `Wrong brazier. ${run.step} of ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps}: Sallow pays ${pay} silver.`
        : `The embers settle. ${run.step} of ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps}: Sallow pays ${pay} silver.`;
  }

  private endLanternRun() {
    const run = this.lanternRun;
    if (!run) return;
    this.lanternRun = null;
    const all = run.lit.length === HARVEST.lanterns.length;
    const pay = run.lit.length * HARVEST.lanternPay + (all ? HARVEST.allLitBonus : 0);
    this.coin += pay;
    this.logLine = all
      ? `Every lantern burns. Hessa pays ${pay} silver.`
      : `The wick-glass runs dry: ${run.lit.length} of ${HARVEST.lanterns.length} lit. Hessa pays ${pay} silver.`;
  }

  private talkFestival(n: Npc) {
    if (n.id === DROWNED_BLOOM.npc.id || n.id === ASHEN_FAIR.npc.id) {
      const turns = ASHEN_FAIR.turns - this.danceTurns();
      const text =
        n.id === DROWNED_BLOOM.npc.id
          ? `Drowned Bloom! The swamp is over its banks and the Tzar's court is out on the water. Bring me blooms off the flood: ${DROWNED_BLOOM.bloomPay} silver each, and a garland for all ${DROWNED_BLOOM.blooms}.`
          : turns > 0
            ? `Ashen Fair, under a burning moon! Watch the braziers flare, then walk them in order. ${ASHEN_FAIR.turnPrice} silver a turn, ${ASHEN_FAIR.stepPay} a step, ${ASHEN_FAIR.perfectBonus} more for all ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps}. ${turns} turn${turns === 1 ? "" : "s"} left tonight.`
            : "The embers are spent for you tonight. The sideshow still walks the Cinder.";
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text };
      this.mode = "talk";
      return;
    }
    const j = harvestJudge(this.festDay());
    const done = this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${this.festDay()}:carved`);
    const text = done
      ? "Your pumpkin is on the judge's table. Go light the plaza while the moon is fat."
      : `Harvest Moon! A pumpkin is ${HARVEST.pumpkinPrice} silver. Tonight's judge is soft on ${j.eyes} eyes; the mouth, they keep to themselves.`;
    this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text };
    this.mode = "talk";
  }

  private buyPumpkin() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    if (this.festivalId() !== "harvest" || this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${day}:carved`)) return;
    if (this.coin < HARVEST.pumpkinPrice) {
      this.logLine = `A pumpkin is ${HARVEST.pumpkinPrice} silver.`;
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= HARVEST.pumpkinPrice;
    this.carving = { day };
    if (this.talk) this.talk.text = "A fat one. Cut the eyes first.";
    this.logLine = `You pay ${HARVEST.pumpkinPrice} silver for a pumpkin.`;
  }

  private cutEyes(eyes: string) {
    if (!this.carving || this.carving.day !== this.festDay() || !(HARVEST.eyes as readonly string[]).includes(eyes)) return;
    this.carving.eyes = eyes;
    if (this.talk) this.talk.text = `${eyes[0].toUpperCase()}${eyes.slice(1)} eyes. Now the mouth.`;
  }

  private cutMouth(mouth: string) {
    const c = this.carving;
    const day = this.festDay();
    if (!c || c.day !== day || !c.eyes || !(HARVEST.mouths as readonly string[]).includes(mouth)) return;
    const score = carveScore(day, c.eyes, mouth);
    const prize = HARVEST.prize[score];
    this.coin += prize;
    this.opened.add(`fest:harvest:${day}:carved`);
    this.gainItem(makeItem({ name: `Carved pumpkin (${c.eyes} eyes, ${mouth})`, kind: "junk", rank: 1, stack: 1 }));
    this.carving = null;
    const line = score === 2 ? `The judge beams: both cuts. ${prize} silver.` : score === 1 ? `The judge nods at one cut. ${prize} silver.` : "The judge walks on. No prize, but it is yours to keep.";
    if (this.talk) this.talk.text = line;
    this.logLine = line;
  }

  private startLanternRun() {
    const day = this.festDay();
    if (this.festivalId() !== "harvest" || this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${day}:lanterns`) || this.lanternRun) return;
    this.opened.add(`fest:harvest:${day}:lanterns`);
    this.lanternRun = { day, lit: [], left: HARVEST.runSeconds };
    this.talk = null;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = `Six lanterns, ${HARVEST.runSeconds} seconds of wick-glass. Walk into each to light it.`;
  }

  /** The festival hook for a season, built or not (for the HUD and checks). */
  seasonFestival(season: SeasonId = this.season()) {
    return FESTIVALS[season];
  }

  weatherLabel() {
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") return "Still, under stone";
    const name = weatherName(this.weather);
    return this.underRoof() ? `${name}, under cover` : name;
  }

  private underRoof() {
    if (this.mapId === "inside") return true;
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return false;
    const t = this.tileUnder(this.px, this.py);
    return t === T.floor || t === T.wall || t === T.door || t === T.nets || t === T.hearth || t === T.cauldron || t === T.shelf || t === T.moon;
  }

  boltFlash() {
    if (this.weather !== "storm") return false;
    const bucket = Math.floor(this.worldMs / 900);
    if (hash(bucket, 4) < 0.55) return false;
    return this.worldMs % 900 < 220;
  }

  questLine() {
    const led = this.escortName();
    if (led) return `Lead ${led} up the stairs and out. A town gate works too.`;
    if (this.remnantId && MINI_NAME[this.remnantId]) return `Chapel notice: ${MINI_NAME[this.remnantId]} still stands. Bring them down for 20 silver.`;
    if (this.quest <= 0) return "Vale: win a fight on the road, then sell what drops.";
    if (this.quest === 1) return "Vale: sell junk at the general store.";
    if (this.quest === 2) return "Vale: the guild can teach you. Stay off the east bridge until you are stronger.";
    const bounty = this.bountyLine();
    if (bounty) return bounty;
    const errand = this.errandLine();
    if (errand) return errand;
    if (this.fishStep === 1) return "The Drowned Hook: trade the catch for points.";
    if (this.fishStep === 2) return "The Drowned Hook: the Bog-step boots cost 40 points.";
    if (this.bestiary.length >= 20 && !this.pagePaid) return "The guild will pay 40 silver for a page of 20 kinds.";
    if (this.level >= 10 && !this.companion) return "Level 10. The chapel, the cauldron, or the moon shop will walk with you.";
    const next = ["Decayed vale", "Winter hollow", "Dry waste", "Cinder", "Scourge swamp"].find((z) => !this.paidZones.includes(z));
    if (next) return `Zone: win a fight in the ${next}. The board pays when it is done.`;
    if (this.fishStep <= 0) return "The Drowned Hook, south street: catch a fish.";
    return `${this.seasonName()}. The wild still keeps its own lords.`;
  }

  /** New life. Wakes in town on the cobble just east of the pond. */
  start(cls: ClassId, path: StatPath, name: string) {
    this.cls = cls;
    this.path = cls === "vampire" ? path : cls === "wizard" ? "int" : cls === "assassin" ? "dex" : "str";
    this.name = name.trim().slice(0, 16) || "Wanderer";
    this.level = 1;
    this.xp = 0;
    this.str = cls === "warrior" || (cls === "vampire" && path === "str") ? 8 : 4;
    this.dex = cls === "assassin" || (cls === "vampire" && path === "dex") ? 8 : 4;
    this.int = cls === "wizard" || (cls === "vampire" && path === "int") ? 8 : 3;
    this.sta = cls === "warrior" || cls === "vampire" ? 8 : 5;
    this.agi = cls === "assassin" ? 7 : 4;
    this.fort = cls === "wizard" ? 6 : 4;
    this.hp = 1;
    this.energy = 1;
    this.hp = this.maxHp;
    this.energy = this.maxEnergy;
    this.coin = 35;
    this.bank = 0;
    this.points = 0;
    this.fishPoints = 0;
    this.vault = [];
    this.ownedHome = false;
    this.zones = [];
    this.paidZones = [];
    this.fishStep = 0;
    this.remnantId = "";
    this.pagePaid = false;
    this.inv = [makeItem({ name: "Bone chip", kind: "junk", rank: 1, stack: 2 })];
    this.equip = { main: starterWeapon(cls, this.path), chest: starterArmor(cls) };
    this.potHp = 2;
    this.potMana = 1;
    this.specials = [];
    this.ranks = {};
    this.blessing = 0;
    this.worldMs = 2 * 60 * 1000;
    this.bossDead = {};
    this.floors = {};
    this.quest = 0;
    this.visited = [];
    this.bestiary = [];
    this.portal = null;
    this.rentedUntil = 0;
    this.hunterBanUntil = 0;
    this.hunterGoneUntil = 0;
    this.barred = {};
    this.fed = {};
    this.opened = new Set();
    this.curse = "";
    this.ashes = [];
    this.escort = null;
    this.companion = null;
    this.favor = {};
    this.trickDay = {};
    this.battle = null;
    this.talk = null;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = "Twilight holds the town. A road gate at any edge leads to the vale. Walk into a door or a stair. E speaks and fights.";
    this.enterTown(20 * TILE + 8, 14 * TILE + 8);
  }

  /** Rebuild the town from scratch and drop the hero at a pixel. */
  enterTown(x = 20 * TILE + 8, y = 14 * TILE + 8) {
    this.mapId = "town";
    this.inside = "";
    this.theme = "town";
    this.fog = null;
    const built = buildTown();
    this.w = built.w;
    this.h = built.h;
    this.tiles = built.tiles;
    this.npcs = built.npcs;
    this.px = x;
    this.py = y;
    this.unstick();
    this.roamers = [];
    this.entrances = {};
    this.critters = [
      { x: 120, y: 220, kind: "cat", vx: 14 },
      { x: 16 * TILE + 8, y: 15 * TILE + 4, kind: "bat", vx: -12 },
    ];
    this.hunterPhase = "";
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.syncHunter();
    this.syncPortal();
    this.syncFolk();
    this.syncRescued();
  }

  /**
   * playtest1: a save, a revive or a portal that lands the hero's feet in a wall (an old save from before the town's
   * doors moved south, say) steps them to the nearest open tile centre instead, nearest first, a fixed search order.
   */
  private unstick() {
    if (!this.solidFeet()) return;
    const ox = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const oy = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    for (let r = 1; r < 8; r++) {
      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
          if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) !== r) continue;
          const tx = ox + dx;
          const ty = oy + dy;
          if (tx < 1 || ty < 1 || tx >= this.w - 1 || ty >= this.h - 1) continue;
          const t = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
          if (t === T.door || t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.stairD || t === T.stairU) continue;
          if (this.mapId === "world" && wayMouthAt(tx, ty)) continue; // playtest1f: never set down in a wayrift's mouth
          const keep = [this.px, this.py];
          this.px = tx * TILE + 8;
          this.py = ty * TILE + 8;
          if (!this.solidFeet()) return;
          [this.px, this.py] = keep;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * playtest1e: a migrated (pre-1e) vale save lands where its doubled spot falls on the new grid; if that is a tree, a
   * rock, water or any wall, step to the nearest open tile centre (nearest first, a fixed order, as unstick does).
   */
  private landMigrated() {
    const open = () => !this.solidFeet() && !this.blockedProp(this.px, this.py) && !this.blockedProp(this.px - 5, this.py + 3) && !this.blockedProp(this.px + 5, this.py + 3);
    if (open()) return;
    const ox = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const oy = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const keep = [this.px, this.py];
    for (let r = 1; r < 16; r++) {
      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
          if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) !== r) continue;
          const tx = ox + dx;
          const ty = oy + dy;
          if (tx < 1 || ty < 1 || tx >= this.w - 1 || ty >= this.h - 1) continue;
          const t = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
          if (t === T.door || t === T.stairD || t === T.stairU) continue;
          if (this.mapId === "world" && wayMouthAt(tx, ty)) continue; // playtest1f: never set down in a wayrift's mouth
          this.px = tx * TILE + 8;
          this.py = ty * TILE + 8;
          if (open()) return;
        }
      }
    }
    [this.px, this.py] = keep;
  }

  /** The vale. Walking off a town edge lands here. */
  enterWorld(x: number, y: number) {
    this.mapId = "world";
    this.inside = "";
    this.theme = "over";
    this.fog = null;
    const built = buildWorld();
    this.w = built.w;
    this.h = built.h;
    this.tiles = built.tiles;
    this.entrances = built.entrances;
    this.npcs = built.npcs;
    this.px = x;
    this.py = y;
    // playtest1f: a spot from before the wayrifts (a save, a camp's return spot) may sit in one's standing stones now
    this.unstick();
    this.placeBosses();
    this.critters = [{ x: x + 30, y: y + 16, kind: "rat", vx: 10 }];
    this.seenBiome = "";
  }

  /** Open a site at floor 1 and remember it as the portal home. */
  enterDungeon(id: string) {
    const def = dungeonById(id);
    if (!def) return;
    if (id === "grave" && this.cls !== "vampire") {
      this.logLine = "The ground will not break for you.";
      return;
    }
    if (def.rift) {
      // The Portal record, taken on the mouth itself: the exact spot you come back to.
      this.riftReturn = { mapId: this.mapId, px: this.px, py: this.py, dungeon: this.dungeon, floor: this.floor, theme: this.theme, inside: this.inside || undefined };
      this.riftCycle = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
      this.fx.portal(this.px, this.py - 4);
    }
    this.dungeon = id;
    this.floor = 1;
    if (def.scale) this.entryLevel = this.level;
    if (!def.town && !def.rift && !this.visited.includes(id)) this.visited.push(id);
    this.audio?.door();
    if (!def.rift) this.beginRun(id);
    this.loadFloor("start");
    this.logLine = def.rift ? `${def.name}. The ash folds shut behind you. It opens somewhere else tomorrow.` : def.pocket ? `${def.name}. A short ruin. The stair rule does not bind here.` : `${def.name}.`;
    if (!def.rift) this.noteCurse(true);
  }

  /** Carve one seeded floor and stand the hero off the stair they arrived on. */
  private loadFloor(from: "start" | "down" | "up") {
    this.trapsCalled.clear();
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    const key = def?.rift ? `${this.dungeon}:c${this.riftCycle}:${this.floor}` : `${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}`;
    if (!this.floors[key]) {
      if (def?.rift) this.floors[key] = carveRift(def, this.riftCycle);
      else if (def?.gen) this.floors[key] = carveGen(def, this.floor);
      else this.floors[key] = carveFloor(this.dungeon, this.floor, def?.floors ?? 5, !!def?.pocket);
    }
    const f = this.floors[key];
    this.gen = f.gen ?? null;
    this.bossRoomNoted = "";
    this.mapId = "dungeon";
    this.inside = "";
    this.theme = def?.theme ?? "cave";
    this.w = f.w;
    this.h = f.h;
    this.tiles = f.tiles;
    this.fog = f.fog;
    this.applyFeats(f.feats ?? null);
    this.npcs = [];
    this.roamers = [];
    this.entrances = {};
    const stair = from === "up" ? T.stairD : T.stairU;
    const spot = findTile(this.tiles, this.w, stair) ?? findTile(this.tiles, this.w, T.floor) ?? { x: 2, y: 2 };
    const stand = stepOff(this.tiles, this.w, this.h, spot.x, spot.y);
    this.px = stand.x * TILE + 8;
    this.py = stand.y * TILE + 10;
    this.placeFloorRoom(def?.rift ? `${this.dungeon}:c${this.riftCycle}` : this.dungeon); // playtest1n: the room's props
    this.reveal();
    this.seedDungeonFoes();
    this.syncMimic();
    for (const r of this.roamers) this.freeBody(r); // playtest1n: no foe starts inside a prop
    this.critters = [
      { x: this.px + 24, y: this.py, kind: "rat", vx: 8 },
      { x: this.px - 20, y: this.py + 10, kind: "bat", vx: -8 },
    ];
    this.syncRun();
  }

  // ---- Mimic chests (item 4). Placement lives in mimic.ts. ----
  // State rides in the existing opened list: mimic:<site>:<floor> once it wakes; the chest's own
  // opened key once it is slain. No new save field.

  private mimicKey() {
    return `mimic:${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}`;
  }

  private mimicSlainKey(m: Spot) {
    return `dungeon:${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}:${m.x}:${m.y}`;
  }

  /** The chest wakes. Its tile becomes floor; the mimic stands where it sat and starts its 0.35 s bite tell. */
  private wakeMimic(m: Spot) {
    this.opened.add(this.mimicKey());
    this.tiles[m.y * this.w + m.x] = T.floor;
    this.openBattle([this.mimicFoe()], { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
    const r = this.roamers[this.roamers.length - 1];
    if (r?.mimic) {
      r.x = m.x * TILE + 8;
      r.y = m.y * TILE + 8;
      r.homeX = r.x;
      r.homeY = r.y;
    }
    this.logLine = "The lid shudders. The chest has teeth. A mimic wakes.";
  }

  /** A floor loaded again: a woken chest stays floor; a mimic not yet slain waits where it sat. */
  private syncMimic() {
    const m = this.feats?.mimic;
    if (!m || !this.opened.has(this.mimicKey())) return;
    this.tiles[m.y * this.w + m.x] = T.floor;
    if (this.opened.has(this.mimicSlainKey(m))) return;
    this.roamers.push({ id: "mimic", x: m.x * TILE + 8, y: m.y * TILE + 8, family: "mimic", tint: MIMIC_DEF.tint, def: "mimic", level: this.dungeonLevel(), ang: 0, rare: true, mimic: true });
  }

  /** A slain mimic pays what the chest held (chest table) and a rare's share (rare table). Then it is spent. */
  private mimicLoot(r: Roamer) {
    const m = this.feats?.mimic;
    if (m) this.opened.add(this.mimicSlainKey(m));
    for (let i = 0; i < MIMICS.chestDrops; i++) this.dropItem(r.x - 6 + i * 12, r.y + 4, this.makeDrop("chest", false));
    for (let i = 0; i < MIMICS.rareDrops; i++) this.dropItem(r.x + 4, r.y + 12 + i * 8, this.makeDrop("rare", false));
  }

  // ---- Night bounty board (item 7). The picks live in bounty.ts. ----
  // State rides in the existing opened list: bounty:<night>:<kind>:<id> once posted (frozen for the
  // night), bounty:<night>:paid once the guild pays. Older nights' keys are dropped at the next posting.

  private lairCache: { tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>; w: number; lairs: number[] } | null = null;

  /** World remnants standing now that the chapel is not already paying for. */
  private standingRemnants() {
    return WORLD_BOSSES.filter((s) => this.bossDead[s.id] && this.worldMs < this.bossDead[s.id] && s.id !== this.remnantId && MINI_NAME[s.id]).map((s) => ({
      id: s.id,
      name: MINI_NAME[s.id],
      x: s.tx,
      y: s.ty,
    }));
  }

  private lairs() {
    if (!this.lairCache) {
      const world = buildWorld();
      const avoid: Spot[] = [
        ...WORLD_BOSSES.map((s) => ({ x: s.tx, y: s.ty })),
        ...Object.keys(world.entrances).map((k) => ({ x: Number(k.split(",")[0]), y: Number(k.split(",")[1]) })),
        ...RIFTS.map((r) => ({ x: r.tx, y: r.ty })),
        ...world.npcs.map((n) => ({ x: Math.floor(n.x / TILE), y: Math.floor(n.y / TILE) })),
        ...WAYRIFTS.map((w) => wayMouth(w)), // playtest1f: no lair on or by a wayrift's stones
      ];
      this.lairCache = { tiles: world.tiles, w: world.w, lairs: lairTiles(world.tiles, world.w, world.h, avoid) };
    }
    return this.lairCache;
  }

  /** Tonight's posting, or null by day. Frozen once posted, so a kill does not reshuffle the night. */
  bountyPost(): Posting | null {
    if (this.phase !== "night") return null;
    const night = nightOf(this.worldMs);
    const tag = `bounty:${night}:`;
    let frozen = [...this.opened].find((k) => k.startsWith(tag) && k !== `${tag}paid`);
    if (!frozen) {
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("bounty:")) this.opened.delete(k);
      const fresh = this.pickBounty(night, this.standingRemnants());
      if (!fresh) return null;
      frozen = `${tag}${fresh.kind}:${fresh.id}`;
      this.opened.add(frozen);
      return fresh;
    }
    const [, , kind, id] = frozen.split(":");
    if (kind === "remnant") {
      const s = WORLD_BOSSES.find((b) => b.id === id);
      if (!s) return null;
      return this.pickBounty(night, [{ id, name: MINI_NAME[id] ?? id, x: s.tx, y: s.ty }], true);
    }
    return this.pickBounty(night, []);
  }

  private pickBounty(night: number, standing: { id: string; name: string; x: number; y: number }[], forceRemnant = false) {
    const { lairs, w } = this.lairs();
    const post = postBounty(night, standing, lairs, w, biomeName, (x, y) => familiesFor(x, y, "world"));
    if (forceRemnant && post && post.kind !== "remnant") {
      const r = standing[0];
      const zone = biomeName(r.x, r.y);
      return { ...post, kind: "remnant" as const, id: r.id, name: r.name, x: r.x, y: r.y, zone, pay: BOUNTY.pay[zone] ?? 16 };
    }
    return post;
  }

  bountyPaid() {
    return this.opened.has(`bounty:${nightOf(this.worldMs)}:paid`);
  }

  /** The posted stalker walks its lair at night until it falls. The day clears it with the other roamers. */
  private spawnBounty() {
    const post = this.bountyPost();
    if (!post || post.kind !== "stalker" || this.bountyPaid() || this.roamers.some((r) => r.bounty)) return;
    const def = monsterById(post.id);
    this.roamers.push({
      id: `bounty-${post.night}`,
      x: post.x * TILE + 8,
      y: post.y * TILE + 8,
      family: def.family,
      tint: def.tint,
      def: def.id,
      level: zoneLevel(post.x, post.y, this.level),
      ang: 0,
      rare: true,
      bounty: true,
      name: post.name,
    });
  }

  /** The guild pays for tonight's name, once, at night. */
  private payBounty(r: Roamer) {
    const post = this.bountyPost();
    if (!post || this.bountyPaid()) return;
    const hit = post.kind === "stalker" ? !!r.bounty && r.rare : !!r.mini && r.def === post.id;
    if (!hit) return;
    this.opened.add(`bounty:${post.night}:paid`);
    this.coin += post.pay;
    this.logLine = `${this.logLine} The guild pays ${post.pay} silver for ${post.name}.`;
  }

  /** Hold Main under the board in the Guildhall. */
  private readBoard() {
    this.readBoardBounty();
    if (this.festivalId() !== "krampus") return;
    const open = this.naughtyTonight().filter((n) => !this.naughtyCaught(n.id));
    const list = open.length
      ? `Nailed over it, a naughty list: ${open.map((n) => `${n.name.split(",")[0]} (${fromGate(n.x, n.y)})`).join(", ")}. Krampus walks the Winter hollow.`
      : "Every name on the naughty list is struck through. Krampus still walks the Winter hollow.";
    this.logLine = `${list} ${this.logLine}`;
  }

  private readBoardBounty() {
    const post = this.bountyPost();
    if (!post) this.logLine = "The bounty board is bare by day. The guild posts a name at dusk.";
    else if (this.bountyPaid()) this.logLine = `Tonight's name is struck through: ${post.name}. Paid. The next goes up at dusk.`;
    else
      this.logLine =
        post.kind === "stalker"
          ? `Bounty: ${post.name}, in the ${post.zone}, ${fromGate(post.x, post.y)} of the gate. It runs with a pack. ${post.pay} silver, tonight only.`
          : `Bounty: ${post.name}, the remnant in the ${post.zone}, ${fromGate(post.x, post.y)} of the gate. ${post.pay} silver, tonight only.`;
  }

  /** Quest line at night while tonight's name is unpaid. */
  private bountyLine() {
    const post = this.bountyPost();
    if (!post || this.bountyPaid()) return "";
    return `Bounty: ${post.name}, ${post.zone}, ${fromGate(post.x, post.y)} of the gate. The guild board pays ${post.pay} silver.`;
  }

  // ---- Rescue follow-up (owner-approved 2026-09-30). Numbers and lines live in content.ts RESCUE_QUESTS. ----
  // State rides in the opened list: rq:<id>:taken, rq:<id>:kills:<n>, rq:<id>:met (a delivery landed),
  // rq:<id>:done (their stall is open). Old saves simply have none of these keys.

  /** Freed captives stand in town on their own grass tile, day and night. */
  private syncRescued() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return;
    this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => n.role !== "rescued");
    for (const q of RESCUE_QUESTS) {
      const res = RESCUES.find((r) => r.id === q.id);
      if (!res || !this.opened.has(`rescued:${res.id}`)) continue;
      this.npcs.push({ id: res.seed, name: res.name, x: q.x * TILE + 8, y: q.y * TILE + 8, coat: "#888888", role: "rescued", look: res.body });
    }
  }

  private errandOf(npcId: string): { q: RescueQuest; name: string } | null {
    const res = RESCUES.find((r) => r.seed === npcId);
    const q = res && RESCUE_QUESTS.find((x) => x.id === res.id);
    return res && q ? { q, name: res.name } : null;
  }

  errandKills(id: string) {
    const k = [...this.opened].find((x) => x.startsWith(`rq:${id}:kills:`));
    return k ? Number(k.split(":")[3]) || 0 : 0;
  }

  /** "none" | "taken" | "met" | "done" for one rescued captive's errand. */
  errandState(id: string): "none" | "taken" | "met" | "done" {
    if (this.opened.has(`rq:${id}:done`)) return "done";
    if (!this.opened.has(`rq:${id}:taken`)) return "none";
    const q = RESCUE_QUESTS.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!q) return "none";
    if (q.kind === "kill" && this.errandKills(id) >= (q.need ?? 1)) return "met";
    if (q.kind === "fetch" && this.inv.some((i) => i.kind === "gem")) return "met";
    if (q.kind === "deliver" && this.opened.has(`rq:${id}:met`)) return "met";
    return "taken";
  }

  private talkRescued(n: Npc) {
    const e = this.errandOf(n.id);
    if (!e) return;
    const st = this.errandState(e.q.id);
    let text = e.q.ask;
    if (st === "done") text = e.q.open;
    else if (st === "met") text = e.q.kind === "fetch" ? `${e.q.thanks.split(".")[0]}. Hand over the ${this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "gem").sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank)[0]?.name ?? "stone"}?` : e.q.thanks;
    else if (st === "taken")
      text =
        e.q.kind === "kill"
          ? `${e.q.nag} ${this.errandKills(e.q.id)} of ${e.q.need} on any Harrow floor.`
          : e.q.kind === "deliver" && !this.inv.some((i) => i.name === e.q.token)
            ? `${e.q.nag} Here, another seal. Do not lose this one.`
            : e.q.nag;
    this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text };
    this.mode = "talk";
  }

  /** Continue on a rescued captive: take the errand, turn it in, or browse the stall. */
  private acceptErrand() {
    const e = this.talk ? this.errandOf(this.talk.npcId ?? "") : null;
    if (!e) return;
    const id = e.q.id;
    const st = this.errandState(id);
    if (st === "done") {
      this.shopRole = `stall:${id}`;
      this.stock = this.buildStock(this.shopRole);
      this.mode = "shop";
      return;
    }
    if (st === "met") {
      if (e.q.kind === "fetch") {
        // The plainest stone goes: lowest rank first.
        const gem = this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "gem").sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank)[0];
        if (gem) this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i !== gem);
      }
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith(`rq:${id}:`)) this.opened.delete(k);
      this.opened.add(`rq:${id}:done`);
      this.audio?.coin();
      this.logLine = `${e.q.thanks} ${e.q.stall} is open.`;
      this.shopRole = `stall:${id}`;
      this.stock = this.buildStock(this.shopRole);
      this.mode = "shop";
      return;
    }
    if (st === "none") {
      this.opened.add(`rq:${id}:taken`);
      this.logLine = `${e.name}'s errand: ${this.errandLine(id)}`;
    }
    if (e.q.kind === "deliver" && !this.inv.some((i) => i.name === e.q.token) && !this.opened.has(`rq:${id}:met`)) {
      this.gainItem(makeItem({ name: e.q.token ?? "Sealed tithe", kind: "relic", rank: 1 }));
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
  }

  /** The undertaker takes Corin's seal. Returns the line, or "" if there is nothing to hand over. */
  private takeDelivery() {
    for (const q of RESCUE_QUESTS) {
      if (q.kind !== "deliver" || q.to !== "undertaker" || this.errandState(q.id) !== "taken") continue;
      const seal = this.inv.find((i) => i.name === q.token);
      if (!seal) continue;
      this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i !== seal);
      this.opened.add(`rq:${q.id}:met`);
      this.audio?.coin();
      return `A seal from the wraps. ${RESCUES.find((r) => r.id === q.id)?.name ?? "They"} is counted among the living again. Tell them so.`;
    }
    return "";
  }

  private countErrandKill() {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon") return;
    for (const q of RESCUE_QUESTS) {
      if (q.kind !== "kill" || q.site !== this.dungeon || this.errandState(q.id) !== "taken") continue;
      const n = this.errandKills(q.id);
      this.opened.delete(`rq:${q.id}:kills:${n}`);
      this.opened.add(`rq:${q.id}:kills:${n + 1}`);
    }
  }

  /** One errand line, for the quest line or the log. With no id, the first open errand. */
  errandLine(id?: string) {
    for (const q of RESCUE_QUESTS) {
      if (id ? q.id !== id : this.errandState(q.id) === "none" || this.errandState(q.id) === "done") continue;
      const who = RESCUES.find((r) => r.id === q.id)?.name ?? q.id;
      if (q.kind === "kill") return `${who}: put down ${q.need} on Harrow Manor floors (${this.errandKills(q.id)} of ${q.need}).`;
      if (q.kind === "fetch") return `${who}: bring any gem to the cauldron corner.`;
      return this.opened.has(`rq:${q.id}:met`) ? `${who}: the undertaker has the seal. Tell ${who}.` : `${who}: carry the sealed tithe to the undertaker.`;
    }
    return "";
  }

  /** A rescued captive's stall. Stat lines are copies of stock already sold in town; priceOf is unchanged. */
  private stallStock(id: string, lv: number, rng: () => number, potion: (name: string, bonus: number) => Item): Item[] {
    if (id === "wren")
      return [
        potion("Health draught", 1),
        potion("Spirit draught", 2),
        makeItem({ name: "Hooded lamp", kind: "weapon", rank: 2, slot: "off", hands: 1, atk: 2, active: "Lantern Flare", bonus: 1 }),
        makeItem({ name: "Road sword", kind: "weapon", rank: 1, slot: "main", atk: 4 + Math.floor(lv / 2) }),
      ];
    if (id === "tansy")
      return [potion("Health draught", 1), potion("Spirit draught", 2), makeItem({ name: "Tansy's ring", kind: "jewel", rank: 2, slot: "ring", bonus: 2 }), this.makeGem(rng, "rare", lv)];
    if (id === "corin") {
      const armor = this.cls === "vampire" ? "chain" : CLASSES[this.cls].armor;
      return [
        potion("Health draught", 1),
        makeItem({ name: "Cabinet edge", kind: "weapon", rank: 3, slot: "main", atk: 4 + 3 * 2 + Math.floor(lv / 4) }),
        makeItem({ name: "Mended armor", kind: "armor", rank: 2, slot: "chest", armor, ac: 2 + 1 }),
      ];
    }
    return [];
  }

  /** The counter title for a rescued captive's stall. */
  stallName() {
    return RESCUE_QUESTS.find((q) => `stall:${q.id}` === this.shopRole)?.stall ?? "Counter";
  }

  // ---- Floor curses and the rescue quest. Placement and seeds live in runs.ts. ----
  // Run state rides in the existing opened list, so saves need no new field:
  //   run:<site>:<day>  the run in progress and the day-night cycle it began on (picks the curse)
  //   escort:<id>       a freed captive is walking with you
  //   rescued:<id>      they made it home; their shop keeps the item

  /** A new run: forget the last run and any captive you did not bring out. */
  private beginRun(id: string) {
    for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("run:") || k.startsWith("escort:")) this.opened.delete(k);
    this.opened.add(`run:${id}:${Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS)}`);
  }

  /** Curse, ash marks, and captive for the floor just loaded. A gate back in resumes the same run. */
  private syncRun() {
    if (dungeonById(this.dungeon)?.rift) {
      // A rift zone is not a dungeon run: no curse, no ash marks, no captive.
      this.curse = "";
      this.ashes = [];
      this.escort = null;
      return;
    }
    const mine = [...this.opened].find((k) => k.startsWith(`run:${this.dungeon}:`));
    if (!mine) this.beginRun(this.dungeon);
    const key = mine ?? [...this.opened].find((k) => k.startsWith(`run:${this.dungeon}:`)) ?? "";
    this.curse = pickCurse(this.dungeon, Number(key.split(":")[2]) || 0);
    this.ashes = [];
    const res = rescueFor(this.dungeon);
    const spot = this.feats?.captive;
    if (res && this.opened.has(`escort:${res.id}`)) {
      const at = this.openNear(this.px + 14, this.py);
      this.escort = { id: res.id, x: at.x, y: at.y, state: "follow", hp: this.escortMax(), max: this.escortMax() };
    } else if (res && spot && !this.opened.has(`rescued:${res.id}`)) {
      this.escort = { id: res.id, x: spot.x * TILE + 8, y: spot.y * TILE + 10, state: "bound" };
    } else this.escort = null;
  }

  /** True on a floor the run's curse holds. */
  curseOn() {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !this.curse) return false;
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    return curseFloor(this.floor, def?.floors ?? 5, !!def?.pocket);
  }

  /** One HUD line while a curse holds, or "". */
  curseLine() {
    if (!this.curseOn() || !this.curse) return "";
    return `Curse: ${CURSES[this.curse].name} · silver +${RUNS.bonus[this.curse]}%`;
  }

  /** Corked: the draught pills are grey and a tap is ignored. */
  potionsCorked() {
    return this.curseOn() && this.curse === "dry";
  }

  /** Name of the captive walking with you, or "". */
  escortName() {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || this.escort?.state !== "follow") return "";
    return rescueFor(this.dungeon)?.name ?? "";
  }

  /** Say the curse once, on the way in and on each new floor. */
  private noteCurse(entering: boolean) {
    if (!this.curse || this.mapId !== "dungeon") return;
    const c = CURSES[this.curse];
    if (this.curseOn()) this.logLine = `${entering ? `${this.logLine} ` : ""}Curse: ${c.name}. ${c.line} Silver +${RUNS.bonus[this.curse]}%.`;
    else this.logLine = `${entering ? `${this.logLine} ` : ""}The ${c.name.toLowerCase()} curse thins here. ${dungeonById(this.dungeon)?.gen ? "Its keepers wait." : "The boss waits."}`;
  }

  /** A freed captive's HP on this site (owner-approved 2026-09-30): sturdy, and never killed. */
  escortMax() {
    return ESCORT.hpBase + ESCORT.hpPerLevel * this.dungeonLevel();
  }

  /** A knocked-down captive holds the stairs and the gate until helped up. */
  private escortHolds() {
    const e = this.escort;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || e?.state !== "down") return false;
    this.logLine = `${rescueFor(this.dungeon)?.name ?? "They"} is down. Hold Main beside them first.`;
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * A foe near the captive turns on them one swing in ESCORT.escortEvery: it swings at them if they are in
   * reach, or walks over first (they trail behind you). Returns true when the beat went to the captive.
   */
  private swingAtEscort(r: Roamer, heroDist: number) {
    const e = this.escort;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !e || e.state !== "follow") return (r.atEscort = false);
    const d = Math.hypot(e.x - r.x, e.y - r.y);
    if (r.atEscort) {
      if (d > ESCORT.notice * 1.5) return (r.atEscort = false);
      if (!this.inReach(r, d)) return true;
    } else {
      // Only a real beat counts: the foe is in reach of you or of them, and they are close.
      if (d > ESCORT.notice || (!this.inReach(r, heroDist) && !this.inReach(r, d))) return false;
      r.escortSwings = (r.escortSwings ?? 0) + 1;
      if (r.escortSwings % ESCORT.escortEvery !== 0) return false;
      if (!this.inReach(r, d)) {
        r.atEscort = true;
        r.cool = 0.2;
        return true;
      }
    }
    r.atEscort = false;
    const dealt = Math.max(1, r.atk ?? 2);
    r.cool = r.boss ? 1.15 : 0.85;
    r.act = r.spamTag === "bolt" ? "cast" : "swing";
    r.actFor = 0.28;
    if (r.spamTag === "bolt") this.bolt(r.x, r.y - 12, e.x, e.y - 10, "bolt");
    e.hp = Math.max(0, (e.hp ?? this.escortMax()) - dealt);
    e.flash = 0.08;
    const who = rescueFor(this.dungeon)?.name ?? "The captive";
    this.nums.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y - 16, text: String(dealt), life: 0.55 });
    if (e.hp <= 0) {
      e.state = "down";
      e.moving = false;
      this.audio?.hurt();
      this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} knocks ${who} down. Hold Main beside them to help them up.`;
    } else this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} turns on ${who} for ${dealt}.`;
    return true;
  }

  /** Main beside a bound captive breaks the chain; beside a knocked-down one, helps them up. */
  private freeCaptive() {
    const e = this.escort;
    if (e && e.state === "down" && Math.hypot(e.x - this.px, e.y - this.py) <= RUNS.captiveReach) {
      e.state = "follow";
      e.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round((e.max ?? this.escortMax()) * ESCORT.upShare));
      this.audio?.door();
      this.logLine = `You help ${rescueFor(this.dungeon)?.name ?? "them"} up. They can walk.`;
      return true;
    }
    if (!e || e.state !== "bound") return false;
    if (Math.hypot(e.x - this.px, e.y - this.py) > RUNS.captiveReach) return false;
    const res = rescueFor(this.dungeon);
    if (!res) return false;
    e.state = "follow";
    e.hp = this.escortMax();
    e.max = e.hp;
    this.opened.add(`escort:${res.id}`);
    this.fx.death(e.x, e.y - 2, "trash", true);
    this.audio?.door();
    this.logLine = `The chain snaps. ${res.plea}`;
    return true;
  }

  /** Leaving the site. Out by stair or gate, a captive at your back is home and their shop keeps the item. A fall loses them. */
  private bringOut(home = true): string {
    const res = rescueFor(this.dungeon);
    const led = home && res && this.mapId === "dungeon" && this.escort?.state === "follow";
    this.escort = null;
    this.ashes = [];
    for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("escort:")) this.opened.delete(k);
    if (!led || !res) return "";
    this.opened.add(`rescued:${res.id}`);
    this.audio?.coin();
    return res.home;
  }

  /** A shop's rescue item, once its captive is home. Stat lines are copies of stock already sold. */
  private rescueStock(role: string, lv: number): Item[] {
    const out: Item[] = [];
    for (const r of RESCUES) {
      if (r.shop !== role || !this.opened.has(`rescued:${r.id}`)) continue;
      const it = { ...r.item };
      if (it.kind === "weapon" && it.slot === "main") it.atk = 4 + it.rank * 2 + Math.floor(lv / 4);
      out.push(makeItem(it));
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Ashen bursts, and the freed captive's walk. Dungeon only. */
  private tickRun(dt: number) {
    for (const a of [...this.ashes]) {
      a.tell -= dt;
      if (a.tell > 0) continue;
      this.ashes = this.ashes.filter((o) => o !== a);
      this.bolt(a.x, a.y - 14, a.x, a.y, "ring", "#d8b090");
      this.fx.death(a.x, a.y - 4, "trash", false);
      this.audio?.spell();
      // The same circle test as a foe mark or a plate. Hits like a trap of this floor's level.
      const dealt = Math.max(1, trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel()) - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
      if (this.inMark(a.x, a.y, RUNS.ashRadius)) this.bite(dealt, `The ash bursts for ${dealt}.`);
      else this.logLine = "You step clear of the ash.";
    }
    const e = this.escort;
    if (e) e.flash = Math.max(0, (e.flash ?? 0) - dt);
    if (!e || e.state !== "follow") return;
    const b = RUNS.escortBack;
    const back = this.facing === "n" ? [0, b] : this.facing === "s" ? [0, -b] : this.facing === "e" ? [-b, 0] : [b, 0];
    const dx = this.px + back[0] - e.x;
    const dy = this.py + back[1] - e.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (this.solidAt(e.x, e.y) || this.blockedProp(e.x, e.y)) this.freeBody(e);
    // Lost round a corner and off screen: step up behind the hero rather than stay lost.
    if (Math.hypot(this.px - e.x, this.py - e.y) > RUNS.escortSnap) {
