      const at = this.openNear(this.px + back[0], this.py + back[1]);
      e.x = at.x;
      e.y = at.y;
      e.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    if (dist < 1.2) {
      e.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    const step = Math.min(dist, RUNS.escortSpeed * dt);
    const ox = e.x;
    const oy = e.y;
    this.tryBody(e, (dx / dist) * step, (dy / dist) * step);
    e.moving = Math.hypot(e.x - ox, e.y - oy) > 0.2;
  }

  private seedDungeonFoes() {
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    if (this.gen && (def?.gen || def?.rift)) {
      this.seedWrittenFoes(this.gen, !!def.rift, this.floor >= def.floors);
      return;
    }
    const last = this.floor >= (def?.floors ?? 5);
    if (last && def?.boss) {
      const dead = !!(this.bossDead[def.boss] && this.worldMs < this.bossDead[def.boss]);
      const boss = monsterById(def.boss);
      const spot = findOpen(this.tiles, this.w, this.h, this.w - 4, this.feats?.hidden);
      this.roamers.push({
        id: def.boss,
        x: spot.x * TILE + 8,
        y: spot.y * TILE + 8,
        family: boss.family,
        tint: boss.tint,
        boss: !dead,
        mini: dead,
        def: def.boss,
        level: this.dungeonLevel(),
        ang: 0,
      });
      return;
    }
    const families = FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id !== "bat");
    for (let n = 0; n < 2; n++) {
      const m = families[Math.floor(hash(this.floor, n, this.dungeon.length) * families.length)];
      const spot = findOpen(this.tiles, this.w, this.h, 6 + n * 8, this.feats?.hidden);
      this.roamers.push({
        id: `d${this.floor}-${n}`,
        x: spot.x * TILE + 8,
        y: spot.y * TILE + 8,
        family: m.family,
        tint: m.tint,
        def: m.id,
        level: this.dungeonLevel(),
        ang: n,
      });
    }
  }

  /** The guard's slain key: once felled it stays down for the rest of this day-night cycle. */
  guardKey() {
    return `guard:${this.dungeon}:${Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS)}`;
  }

  /**
   * OWNER-APPROVED EXCEPTION 2026-10-01: MAP WRITER. The writer's foes stand where it put them (trash only, the one
   * level rule). A generated dungeon's last floor holds a Stalker pack in its boss room, never a boss. A rift may hold
   * one Stalker and one Loot Goblin: seeded rolls at the odds the game already uses (wild Stalkers ~1/14, camp goblins).
   */
  private seedWrittenFoes(m: MapResult, rift: boolean, last: boolean) {
    const lv = this.dungeonLevel();
    m.foes.forEach((f, n) => {
      const d = monsterById(f.id);
      this.roamers.push({ id: `w${this.floor}-${n}`, x: f.x * TILE + 8, y: f.y * TILE + 8, family: d.family, tint: d.tint, def: d.id, level: lv, ang: n });
    });
    if (!rift && last && m.bossSpot && !this.opened.has(this.guardKey())) {
      const g = guardPick(m);
      const d = monsterById(g.id);
      this.roamers.push({ id: "guard", x: m.bossSpot.x * TILE + 8, y: m.bossSpot.y * TILE + 8, family: d.family, tint: d.tint, def: d.id, level: lv, ang: 0, pack: "guard", affix: g.affix, name: `${d.name} Warden` });
    }
    if (!rift || !m.foes.length) return;
    const roll = rng32(subSeed(m.seed, "extras"));
    if (roll() < 1 / 14) {
      const at = m.foes[Math.floor(roll() * m.foes.length)];
      const r = this.roamers.find((o) => o.x === at.x * TILE + 8 && o.y === at.y * TILE + 8);
      if (r) {
        r.pack = "stalker";
        r.affix = roll() < 0.5 ? "fast" : "vortex";
      }
    } else roll();
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    if (def && roll() < goblinChance(def.tx, def.ty)) {
      const spot = this.openNear(m.arrive ? m.arrive.x * TILE + 8 + 48 : this.px + 48, m.arrive ? m.arrive.y * TILE + 8 : this.py);
      const gob = monsterById("goblin");
      this.roamers.push({ id: "riftgoblin", x: spot.x, y: spot.y, family: gob.family, tint: gob.tint, def: "goblin", level: Math.max(3, this.level), ang: 0, riftGoblin: true });
    }
  }

  dungeonLevel() {
    const d = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    if (!d) return Math.max(1, this.level);
    if (d.scale) return Math.max(10, this.entryLevel || this.level);
    return Math.max(d.level, zoneLevel(d.tx, d.ty, this.level));
  }

  private placeBosses() {
    const spots = WORLD_BOSSES;
    this.roamers = [];
    for (const s of spots) {
      this.clearStamp(s.tx, s.ty);
      const dead = !!(this.bossDead[s.id] && this.worldMs < this.bossDead[s.id]);
      const def = monsterById(s.id);
      const lv = Math.max(s.lv, zoneLevel(s.tx, s.ty, this.level));
      const spot = this.openNear(s.tx * TILE + 8, s.ty * TILE + 8);
      this.roamers.push({
        id: s.id,
        x: spot.x,
        y: spot.y,
        family: def.family,
        tint: def.tint,
        boss: !dead,
        mini: dead,
        def: s.id,
        level: lv,
        ang: hash(s.tx, s.ty) * Math.PI * 2,
      });
    }
  }

  /** Nearest tile a body can stand on. Used when a boss or a roamer would be left inside a wall. */
  private openNear(x: number, y: number) {
    if (!this.solidAt(x, y) && !this.blockedProp(x, y)) return { x, y };
    for (let r = 16; r <= 80; r += 16) {
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
        const nx = x + Math.cos(a) * r;
        const ny = y + Math.sin(a) * r;
        if (!this.solidAt(nx, ny) && !this.blockedProp(nx, ny)) return { x: nx, y: ny };
      }
    }
    return { x, y };
  }
  private tryBody(body: { x: number; y: number }, dx: number, dy: number) {
    const nx = body.x + dx;
    const ny = body.y + dy;
    if (!this.solidAt(nx, body.y) && !this.blockedProp(nx, body.y)) body.x = nx;
    if (!this.solidAt(body.x, ny) && !this.blockedProp(body.x, ny)) body.y = ny;
  }

  private freeBody(body: { x: number; y: number }) {
    if (!this.solidAt(body.x, body.y) && !this.blockedProp(body.x, body.y)) return;
    const spot = this.openNear(body.x, body.y);
    body.x = spot.x;
    body.y = spot.y;
  }

  private nudge(body: { x: number; y: number } | "hero", dx: number, dy: number) {
    const hero = body === "hero";
    const ox = hero ? this.px : body.x;
    const oy = hero ? this.py : body.y;
    const tries = [
      [dx, dy],
      [dy, -dx],
      [-dy, dx],
      [-dx, -dy],
      [1, 0],
      [-1, 0],
      [0, 1],
      [0, -1],
    ];
    for (const [sx, sy] of tries) {
      const len = Math.hypot(sx, sy) || 1;
      const nx = ox + (sx / len) * 18;
      const ny = oy + (sy / len) * 18;
      if (hero) {
        this.px = nx;
        this.py = ny;
        if (!this.solidFeet()) {
          this.followCompanion();
          return;
        }
      } else if (!this.solidAt(nx, ny) && !this.blockedProp(nx, ny)) {
        body.x = nx;
        body.y = ny;
        return;
      }
    }
    if (hero) {
      this.px = ox;
      this.py = oy;
    }
  }

  private blockedProp(px: number, py: number) {
    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h) return true;
    const t = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
    if (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside === "croft" && this.decorAt(tx, ty)) return true;
    return t === T.tree || t === T.rock || t === T.pump || t === T.grave || t === T.wall || t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.ice || t === T.shelf || t === T.cauldron || t === T.moon || secretTile(t);
  }

  private tickFolk(dt: number) {
    for (const n of this.npcs) {
      if (n.role === "portal") {
        n.moving = false;
        continue;
      }
      if (n.homeX == null || n.homeY == null) {
        n.homeX = n.x;
        n.homeY = n.y;
      }
      const tx = Math.floor(n.x / TILE);
      const ty = Math.floor(n.y / TILE);
      const home = HOMES.find((h) => insideHome(h, tx, ty));
      const blocked = this.solidAt(n.x, n.y) || this.blockedProp(n.x, n.y);
      const strolls = (n.role === "folk" || n.role === "wander") && !blocked;
      if (!strolls) {
        n.moving = false;
        if (blocked) {
          n.stuck = 0;
          this.freeBody(n);
        }
        continue;
      }
      const ang = this.frame * 0.35 + n.x * 0.01;
      let nx = n.x + Math.cos(ang) * 10 * dt;
      let ny = n.y + Math.sin(ang) * 10 * dt;
      if (Math.hypot(nx - n.homeX, ny - n.homeY) > 18) {
        nx = n.x + Math.sign(n.homeX - n.x) * 12 * dt;
        ny = n.y + Math.sign(n.homeY - n.y) * 12 * dt;
      }
      const ntx = Math.floor(nx / TILE);
      const nty = Math.floor(ny / TILE);
      if (home && !insideHome(home, ntx, nty)) {
        n.moving = false;
        n.stuck = 0;
        continue;
      }
      if (this.solidAt(nx, ny) || this.blockedProp(nx, ny)) {
        n.stuck = (n.stuck ?? 0) + dt;
        n.moving = false;
        if ((n.stuck ?? 0) > 1) {
          n.stuck = 0;
          this.freeBody(n);
        }
      } else {
        n.stuck = 0;
        n.moving = true;
        n.x = nx;
        n.y = ny;
      }
    }
  }

  private clearStamp(tx: number, ty: number) {
    for (let y = ty - 2; y <= ty + 2; y++) {
      for (let x = tx - 2; x <= tx + 2; x++) {
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.w || y >= this.h) continue;
        const i = y * this.w + x;
        const t = this.tiles[i];
        if (t === T.tree || t === T.rock || t === T.pump || t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.ice || t === T.grave) this.tiles[i] = biomeTile(x, y);
      }
    }
  }

  private syncHunter() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return;
    const phase = this.phase;
    if (this.hunterPhase === phase && this.npcs.some((n) => n.role === "hunter")) return;
    this.hunterPhase = phase;
    this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => n.role !== "hunter");
    if (this.worldMs < this.hunterGoneUntil) return;
    const night = phase === "night";
    this.npcs.push(
      night
        ? { id: "hunter", name: "Vampire Hunter", x: 23 * TILE + 8, y: 21 * TILE + 8, coat: "#3a3228", role: "hunter" }
        : { id: "hunter", name: "Vampire Hunter", x: 11 * TILE + 8, y: 5 * TILE + 8, coat: "#3a3228", role: "hunter" },
    );
  }

  private syncPortal() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return;
    this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => n.role !== "portal");
    if (!this.portal) return;
    this.npcs.push({ id: "portal", name: "Town portal", x: 22 * TILE + 8, y: 13 * TILE + 8, coat: "#7a5ad0", role: "portal" });
  }

  /** Place townsfolk for this day or night. Home bodies sleep inside; the rest keep their day tiles. */
  private syncFolk() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return;
    const day = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
    const key = `${this.phase}:${day}:${this.companion?.sourceId ?? ""}`;
    if (key === this.folkKey && this.npcs.some((n) => n.role === "folk" || n.id === "acolyte" || n.id === "vetch")) return;
    this.folkKey = key;
    const kept = new Set(["acolyte", "vetch", ...FOLK.map((f) => f.id)]);
    this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => !kept.has(n.id));
    const gone = this.companion?.sourceId;
    for (const f of FOLK) {
      if (gone === f.id) continue;
      const spot = this.folkSpot(f, day);
      if (!spot) continue;
      this.npcs.push({ id: f.id, name: f.name, x: spot.x, y: spot.y, coat: f.coat, role: "folk", look: KITS[f.kit].look });
    }
    if (gone) this.npcs = this.npcs.filter((n) => n.id !== gone);
  }

  /** Where one townsfolk stands. Night sends some indoors and leaves Mara on the street. */
  private folkSpot(f: Folk, day: number): { x: number; y: number } | null {
    const night = this.phase === "night";
    if (!night || f.night === "out") return { x: f.dayX * TILE, y: f.dayY * TILE };
    if (f.night === "sometimes" && hash(day, f.id.length, 4) > 0.45) return { x: f.dayX * TILE, y: f.dayY * TILE };
    return null;
  }

  private barHome() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town" || this.cls !== "vampire") {
      this.lastBar = "";
      return;
    }
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const home = HOMES.find((h) => insideHome(h, tx, ty));
    if (!home) {
      this.lastBar = "";
      return;
    }
    if ((this.favor[home.id] ?? 0) >= 3) return;
    this.px = home.outX * TILE + 8;
    this.py = home.outY * TILE + 8;
    if (this.lastBar !== home.id) {
      this.lastBar = home.id;
      this.logLine = `${home.who} has not invited you. A trick, or the gift they want, opens the door.`;
    }
  }

  /** The companion trails the hero and stops short of their heels. Called once per move. */
  private followCompanion(dt = 1 / 60) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return;
    if (this.mapId === "camp") {
      this.wanderCompanion(dt);
      return;
    }
    const back = this.facing === "n" ? [0, 18] : this.facing === "s" ? [0, -18] : this.facing === "e" ? [-18, 0] : [18, 0];
    let tx = this.px + back[0];
    let ty = this.py + back[1];
    // playtest1k: orders. Stay holds the spot (until the hero is past the leash or on another map); Focus walks it to
    // the target; Guard stands it at the hero's side, toward the nearest foe.
    const here = `${this.mapId}:${this.inside}`;
    if (this.allyStay && (this.allyStayAt !== here || Math.hypot(this.px - c.x, this.py - c.y) > ORDER.stayLeash * TILE)) {
      this.allyStay = false;
      this.logLine = `${c.name} catches up.`;
    }
    const focus = this.focusFoe();
    if (focus) {
      const d = Math.hypot(focus.x - c.x, focus.y - c.y) || 1;
      tx = d > 20 ? focus.x - ((focus.x - c.x) / d) * 18 : c.x;
      ty = d > 20 ? focus.y - ((focus.y - c.y) / d) * 18 : c.y;
    } else if (this.guardUntil > this.worldMs) {
      const near = this.roamers.find((r) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) < 96);
      const d = near ? Math.hypot(near.x - this.px, near.y - this.py) || 1 : 1;
      tx = near ? this.px + ((near.x - this.px) / d) * 14 : this.px + 12;
      ty = near ? this.py + ((near.y - this.py) / d) * 14 : this.py;
    } else if (this.allyStay) {
      c.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    const dx = tx - c.x;
    const dy = ty - c.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (this.solidAt(c.x, c.y) || this.blockedProp(c.x, c.y)) this.freeBody(c);
    if (dist < 1.2) {
      c.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    const step = Math.min(dist, 70 * dt);
    const ox = c.x;
    const oy = c.y;
    this.tryBody(c, (dx / dist) * step, (dy / dist) * step);
    c.moving = Math.hypot(c.x - ox, c.y - oy) > 0.2;
  }

  /** In camp they pace the fire and the tent. They do not glue to your heels. */
  private wanderCompanion(dt: number) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return;
    if (this.solidAt(c.x, c.y) || this.blockedProp(c.x, c.y)) this.freeBody(c);
    this.campWalk -= dt;
    if (this.campWalk <= 0 || !this.campGoal) {
      this.campWalk = 2.4;
      const spots = [
        [11, 8],
        [6, 8],
        [9, 7],
        [12, 6],
      ];
      const s = spots[Math.floor(Math.random() * spots.length)];
      this.campGoal = { x: s[0] * TILE + 8, y: s[1] * TILE + 8 };
    }
    const dx = this.campGoal.x - c.x;
    const dy = this.campGoal.y - c.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (dist < 2) {
      c.moving = false;
      return;
    }
    const ox = c.x;
    const oy = c.y;
    this.tryBody(c, (dx / dist) * 28 * dt, (dy / dist) * 28 * dt);
    c.moving = Math.hypot(c.x - ox, c.y - oy) > 0.2;
  }

  /** The companion swings for real when something is in reach. */
  private tickAlly(dt: number) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c || c.hp <= 0) return;
    c.actFor = Math.max(0, (c.actFor ?? 0) - dt);
    c.cool = Math.max(0, (c.cool ?? 0) - dt);
    c.artCool = Math.max(0, (c.artCool ?? 0) - dt);
    if ((c.cool ?? 0) > 0) return;
    // playtest1k: "Focus my target" puts the companion on that foe (it walks over in followCompanion).
    const focus = this.focusFoe();
    const foe = focus ? (Math.hypot(focus.x - c.x, focus.y - c.y) < 64 ? focus : undefined) : this.roamers.find((r) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - c.x, r.y - c.y) < 48);
    const art = this.allyArt(c, foe);
    if (art) {
      this.castAlly(c, art, foe);
      c.artCool = 4.5;
      c.cool = 0.8;
      return;
    }
    if (!foe || Math.hypot(foe.x - c.x, foe.y - c.y) > 28) return;
    c.cool = 1.1;
    c.act = "swing";
    c.actFor = 0.28;
    const dealt = Math.max(1, 2 + this.companionAtk() - Math.floor((foe.ac ?? 0) / 2));
    this.hurtFoe(foe, dealt, "slash", true);
    this.logLine = `${c.name} swings for ${dealt}.`;
  }

  /** Heal when someone is hurt. Ward and bless only if they are down. Otherwise the best harm. */
  private allyArt(c: Companion, foe?: Roamer): Art | undefined {
    if ((c.artCool ?? 0) > 0) return;
    const known = KITS[c.kit].tree.filter((a) => this.level >= a.need);
    if (!known.length) return;
    const hurt = this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.6 || c.hp < this.companionMax() * 0.6;
    if (hurt) {
      const mend = [...known].reverse().find((a) => a.kind === "heal" || a.kind === "cleanse");
      if (mend) return mend;
    }
    if (this.wardUntil <= this.worldMs) {
      const ward = known.find((a) => a.kind === "ward");
      if (ward) return ward;
    }
    if (this.cryUntil <= this.worldMs) {
      const bless = known.find((a) => a.kind === "buff");
      if (bless) return bless;
    }
    if (!foe) return;
    return [...known].reverse().find((a) => a.kind === "drain" || a.kind === "curse" || a.kind === "aggro");
  }

  companionMax() {
    const gear = this.companion
      ? Object.values(this.companion.equip).reduce((n, it) => n + (it?.ac ?? 0) + (it?.kind === "armor" ? (it.bonus ?? 0) + (it.gemBonus ?? 0) : 0), 0)
      : 0;
    return 14 + this.level * 2 + gear;
  }

  private wardAura() {
    let n = 0;
    const piles = [this.equip, this.companion?.equip ?? {}];
    for (const pile of piles) for (const it of Object.values(pile)) if (it?.aura === "Ward") n += 1;
    return n;
  }

  private companionAtk() {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return 0;
    return Object.values(c.equip).reduce((n, it) => n + (it?.atk ?? 0) + (it?.active ? 0 : 0), 0);
  }

  private companionBonus() {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return 0;
    return Object.values(c.equip).reduce((n, it) => n + (it?.bonus ?? 0) + (it?.gemBonus ?? 0), 0);
  }

  allyOptions(): string[] {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return [];
    return KITS[c.kit].tree.filter((a) => this.level >= a.need).map((a) => a.name);
  }

  private artNamed(name: string): Art | undefined {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return;
    return KITS[c.kit].tree.find((a) => a.name === name);
  }

  choices(): { id: string; label: string }[] {
    if (!this.talk) return [];
    const out: { id: string; label: string }[] = [];
    const role = this.talk.role;
    const hire = role === "priest" || role === "alchemist" || role === "mystic" ? HIRE[role] : undefined;
    if (hire && this.cls !== "vampire") {
      const cross = this.cls !== hire.native;
      const need = hire.gate + (cross ? 5 : 0);
      const cost = cross ? 40 : 15;
      out.push({ id: "hire", label: this.companion?.id === hire.id ? "Already with you" : `Hire ${hire.name} · Lv ${need} · ${cost}s` });
    }
    if (role === "wander" && this.talk.npcId === "acolyte" && this.cls !== "vampire") out.push({ id: "hire", label: "Ask Bren to walk with you" });
    if (role === "wander" && this.talk.npcId === "vetch" && this.cls !== "vampire") out.push({ id: "hire", label: "Ask Vetch to walk with you" });
    const folk = FOLK.find((f) => f.id === this.talk?.npcId);
    if (folk && this.cls === "vampire") {
      const gift = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "gift" && i.gift === folk.gift);
      if (folk.home) out.push({ id: "trick", label: "Trick an invitation" });
      if (gift) out.push({ id: "gift", label: `Offer ${gift.name}` });
      out.push({ id: "bite", label: "Bite, and bind them" });
    }
    if (role === "companion" && this.cls === "vampire" && this.mapId === "camp") out.push({ id: "stake", label: "Stake the bond" });
    if (role === "festival" && this.festivalId() === "harvest") {
      const day = this.festDay();
      if (this.carving?.day === day && this.carving.eyes) for (const m of HARVEST.mouths) out.push({ id: `mouth:${m}`, label: `Cut a ${m} mouth` });
      else if (this.carving?.day === day) for (const e of HARVEST.eyes) out.push({ id: `eyes:${e}`, label: `Cut ${e} eyes` });
      else if (!this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${day}:carved`)) out.push({ id: "carve", label: `Buy a pumpkin to carve · ${HARVEST.pumpkinPrice}s` });
      if (!this.opened.has(`fest:harvest:${day}:lanterns`) && !this.lanternRun) out.push({ id: "lanterns", label: `Light the plaza lanterns · ${HARVEST.runSeconds} s` });
    }
    if (role === "festival" && this.festivalId() === "bloom") {
      const blooms = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "junk" && i.name === DROWNED_BLOOM.bloom.name)?.stack ?? 0;
      if (blooms) out.push({ id: "blooms", label: `Hand over ${blooms} drowned bloom${blooms === 1 ? "" : "s"} · ${DROWNED_BLOOM.bloomPay}s each` });
    }
    if (role === "festival" && this.festivalId() === "ashen" && !this.danceRun && this.danceTurns() < ASHEN_FAIR.turns) {
      out.push({ id: "dance", label: `Dance the braziers · ${ASHEN_FAIR.turnPrice}s (turn ${this.danceTurns() + 1} of ${ASHEN_FAIR.turns})` });
    }
    if (role === "fisher" && this.derbyTonight()) {
      if (!this.derbyIn()) out.push({ id: "derby", label: "Enter the Midnight Derby" });
      else if (this.witchlit().length) out.push({ id: "weigh", label: `Weigh in: ${this.witchlit()[0].name}` });
    }
    const relic = RELIC_FOR[role];
    if (relic && this.cls === "vampire" && this.inv.some((i) => i.kind === "relic" && i.name === relic.name)) {
      out.push({ id: "pact", label: `Offer the ${relic.name}` });
    }
    return out;
  }

  choose(id: string) {
    if (id === "hire") this.hireCompanion();
    else if (id === "trick") this.trickInvite();
    else if (id === "gift") this.offerGift();
    else if (id === "bite") this.bindBite();
    else if (id === "pact") this.bindPact();
    else if (id === "stake") this.stakeCompanion();
    else if (id === "derby") this.enterDerby();
    else if (id === "weigh") this.weighIn();
    else if (id === "carve") this.buyPumpkin();
    else if (id.startsWith("eyes:")) this.cutEyes(id.slice(5));
    else if (id.startsWith("mouth:")) this.cutMouth(id.slice(6));
    else if (id === "lanterns") this.startLanternRun();
    else if (id === "blooms") this.handBlooms();
    else if (id === "dance") this.startDance();
  }

  /** Take the guild hire for this class, if the level gate is met and the seat is empty. */
  private hireCompanion() {
    if (!this.talk || this.cls === "vampire") return;
    const fromWander = this.talk.npcId === "acolyte" ? HIRE.priest : this.talk.npcId === "vetch" ? HIRE.alchemist : undefined;
    const hire = fromWander ?? (this.talk.role === "priest" || this.talk.role === "alchemist" || this.talk.role === "mystic" ? HIRE[this.talk.role] : undefined);
    if (!hire) return;
    if (this.companion?.id === hire.id) {
      this.logLine = `${hire.name} is already at your heel.`;
      return;
    }
    const cross = this.cls !== hire.native;
    const need = hire.gate + (cross ? 5 : 0);
    if (this.level < need) {
      this.logLine = cross
        ? `${hire.name} does not know your road yet. Come back at level ${need}, coin in hand.`
        : `Not until level ${need}.`;
      return;
    }
    const cost = cross ? 40 : 15;
    if (this.coin < cost) {
      this.logLine = cross ? `A stranger's bribe is ${cost} silver.` : `${cost} silver retains them.`;
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= cost;
    this.audio?.coin();
    const source = hire.id === "witch" ? "vetch" : hire.id;
    this.takeCompanion(hire.id, hire.name, hire.kit, source);
    this.logLine =
      hire.id === "shade"
        ? "The veil opens. A shade-at-arms steps through."
        : cross
          ? `${hire.name} takes the bribe, and the extra years of caution.`
          : `${hire.name} falls in beside you.`;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
    this.folkKey = "";
  }

  private trickInvite() {
    const folk = this.folkTalk();
    if (!folk?.home) return;
    const day = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
    if (this.trickDay[folk.id] === day) {
      this.logLine = "They will not hear another story until dawn.";
      return;
    }
    this.trickDay[folk.id] = day;
    const have = this.favor[folk.home] ?? 0;
    if (Math.random() < 0.28 + have * 0.14) {
      this.favor[folk.home] = 3;
      this.logLine = `${folk.name} believes you. The door is yours tonight and after.`;
    } else {
      this.favor[folk.home] = Math.min(2, have + 1);
      this.logLine = "Not tonight. Another day, or the gift they actually want.";
    }
  }

  private offerGift() {
    const folk = this.folkTalk();
    if (!folk) return;
    const gift = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "gift" && i.gift === folk.gift);
    if (!gift) {
      this.logLine = "You are not holding what they want.";
      return;
    }
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== gift.uid);
    if (folk.home) this.favor[folk.home] = 3;
    this.logLine = `${folk.name} takes the ${gift.name}. You are welcome inside.`;
    this.audio?.coin();
  }

  /** Turn a willing townsfolk into a thrall. The civic law still treats the hero as a vampire. */
  private bindBite() {
    if (this.cls !== "vampire") return;
    if (this.phase !== "night") {
      this.logLine = "The bite is a night privilege.";
      return;
    }
    const folk = this.folkTalk();
    if (!folk) {
      this.logLine = "Vendors are not prey.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.companion) {
      this.logLine = "You already keep one. Only a stake in the crypt cuts that bond.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.level < folk.gate) {
      this.logLine = `${folk.name} would not survive your company before level ${folk.gate}.`;
      return;
    }
    if (!this.biteAllowed(folk)) {
      this.logLine = folk.home ? "Cross their threshold first. The street is not their vein." : "They are not here tonight.";
      return;
    }
    this.takeCompanion(folk.id, folk.name, folk.kit, folk.id);
    this.logLine = `${folk.name} rises pale, and will not leave you.`;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  private bindPact() {
    if (this.cls !== "vampire" || this.phase !== "night" || !this.talk) return;
    const relic = RELIC_FOR[this.talk.role];
    if (!relic) return;
    if (this.companion) {
      this.logLine = "One underling. Stake the one you have before you take a greater one.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.level < 20) {
      this.logLine = "That pact waits for level 20.";
      return;
    }
    const item = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "relic" && i.name === relic.name);
    if (!item) return;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== item.uid);
    this.takeCompanion(this.talk.role, relic.who, relic.kit, this.talk.role);
    this.logLine = `${relic.who} accepts the relic and a darker office.`;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  private biteAllowed(folk: Folk) {
    if (!folk.home) return true;
    if (folk.night === "sometimes" && hash(Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS), folk.id.length, 4) > 0.45) return true;
    if (folk.night === "out") return true;
    const home = HOMES.find((h) => h.id === folk.home);
    if (!home) return false;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    return insideHome(home, tx, ty) && (this.favor[home.id] ?? 0) >= 3;
  }

  private folkTalk() {
    return FOLK.find((f) => f.id === this.talk?.npcId);
  }

  private takeCompanion(id: string, name: string, kit: KitId, sourceId: string) {
    const kitDef = KITS[kit];
    this.companion = {
      id,
      name,
      kit,
      sourceId,
      look: kitDef.look,
      coat: "#888",
      focus: kitDef.focus,
      hp: 1,
      equip: {},
      x: this.px,
      y: this.py + 16,
    };
    this.companion.hp = this.companionMax();
  }

  releaseCompanion() {
    if (!this.companion) return;
    if (this.cls === "vampire") {
      this.logLine = "They are bound. Only a stake at the campfire releases them.";
      return;
    }
    const name = this.companion.name;
    this.companion = null;
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.logLine = `${name} returns to their post.`;
  }

  equipCompanion(it: Item) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c || !it.slot) {
      this.logLine = "Nothing to hang that on.";
      return;
    }
    const prev = c.equip[it.slot];
    if (it.hands === 2 && c.equip.off) {
      this.inv.push(c.equip.off);
      delete c.equip.off;
    }
    c.equip[it.slot] = it;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== it.uid);
    if (prev) this.inv.push(prev);
    c.hp = Math.min(c.hp, this.companionMax());
    this.logLine = it.active
      ? `${c.name} wears ${it.name}. The spell sleeps. The stats, the socket, and the aura stay.`
      : `${c.name} wears ${it.name}.`;
  }

  unequipCompanion(slot: string) {
    const c = this.companion;
    const it = c?.equip[slot];
    if (!c || !it) return;
    if (this.inv.length >= 20) {
      this.logLine = "The pack is full.";
      return;
    }
    delete c.equip[slot];
    this.inv.push(it);
    this.logLine = `${it.name} comes back to you.`;
  }

  unequipItem(slot: string) {
    const it = this.equip[slot];
    if (!it) return;
    if (this.inv.length >= 20) {
      this.logLine = "The pack is full.";
      return;
    }
    this.inv.push(it);
    delete this.equip[slot];
    this.logLine = `${it.name} comes off.`;
  }

  stakeCompanion() {
    if (this.cls !== "vampire" || !this.companion) return;
    const name = this.companion.name;
    // Item 11: the stake ends the bond meter for that one too.
    for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (readBond(k)?.id === this.companion.id) this.opened.delete(k);
    this.companion = null;
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.talk = null;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = `You stake ${name}. The bond is ash. Another bite can be chosen.`;
    this.audio?.hurt();
  }

  /** One frame of the world. Combat, talk, and menus do not move the hero. */
  update(dt: number) {
    dt = Math.min(0.05, dt);
    this.frame += dt * 8;
    if (this.shake > 0) this.shake = Math.max(0, this.shake - dt);
    if (this.calm > 0) this.calm = Math.max(0, this.calm - dt);
    this.pollPad();
    this.audio?.pump();
    if (this.actFor > 0) this.actFor = Math.max(0, this.actFor - dt);
    if (this.spellCool > 0) this.spellCool = Math.max(0, this.spellCool - dt);
    if (this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "dead" || this.mode === "pause" || this.mode === "map" || this.mode === "level") return;
    if (this.askLeave && (this.mapId !== "camp" || this.mode !== "play")) this.askLeave = false;
    if (this.askLeave) return;
    this.worldMs += dt * 1000;
    this.tickWeather();
    this.syncMood();
    this.derbyClock -= dt;
    if (this.derbyClock <= 0) {
      this.derbyClock = 1;
      this.settleDerby();
    }
    if (this.mode === "fish") {
      this.tickFish(dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "battle") {
      this.tickBattle();
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "shop" || this.mode === "casino" || this.mode === "bank" || this.mode === "zeppelin" || this.mode === "crypt") return;
    if (this.fallen > 0) {
      this.fallen -= dt;
      if (this.fallen <= 0) {
        const coin = this.coin;
        if (this.readSaves()[0]) this.loadSlot(0);
        else {
          this.hp = this.maxHp;
          this.stam = 40;
          this.blood = 20;
          this.bringOut(false);
          this.enterTown();
        }
        this.coin = Math.max(this.coin, coin);
        this.calm = 2;
        this.logLine = "You drop. The ash is patient.";
      }
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "talk") {
      this.tickLinger(dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.travel) {
      // playtest1f: inside a wayrift's swirl nothing moves (no input from any scheme, no foe steps up)
      this.tickTravel(dt);
      return;
    }
    this.regen(dt);
    this.move(dt);
    this.tickDrops(dt);
    this.tickCritters(dt);
    this.tickRoamers(dt);
    this.tickFestival(dt);
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") this.tickTraps(dt);
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") this.tickRoom(dt); // playtest1n: spikes on foes, fire, oil, fuses, falls
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") this.tickRun(dt);
    this.tickFolk(dt);
    if (this.mapId === "town") {
      this.syncHunter();
      this.syncPortal();
      this.syncFolk();
    } else if (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside) {
      const stamp = `${this.inside}:${this.phase}:${Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS)}:${this.companion?.sourceId ?? ""}`;
      if (stamp !== this.roomKey) {
        this.roomKey = stamp;
        const room = ROOMS.find((r) => r.id === this.inside);
        if (room) this.npcs = this.roomFolk(room);
      }
    }
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") this.reveal();
    this.noteBiome();
  }

  /** Note a weather change in the log, and rumble once when a bolt is on screen. */
  private tickWeather() {
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon") return;
    const w = seasonWeather(this.worldMs);
    if (this.weatherMark === "") this.weatherMark = w;
    else if (w !== this.weatherMark) {
      this.weatherMark = w;
      if (w === "light") this.logLine = "Light rain.";
      else if (w === "heavy") this.logLine = "The rain comes down hard.";
      else if (w === "storm") this.logLine = "Thunder walks the vale.";
      else if (w === "snow") this.logLine = "Snow begins to fall.";
      else this.logLine = "The air goes still.";
    }
    if (w !== "storm" || !this.boltFlash()) return;
    const bucket = Math.floor(this.worldMs / 900);
    if (bucket === this.thunderMark) return;
    this.thunderMark = bucket;
    this.audio?.thunder();
    this.shake = Math.min(0.45, this.shake + 0.16);
  }

  private syncMood() {
    const fighting = this.roamers.some((r) => r.aggro && (r.hp ?? 0) > 0);
    const key = fighting ? "battle" : this.mode === "battle" ? "battle" : this.mapId === "inside" ? `inside:${this.theme}:${this.phase}` : `${this.mapId}:${this.phase}`;
    if (key === this.mood) return;
    this.mood = key;
    this.audio?.mood(key);
  }

  private regen(dt: number) {
    const bless = this.blessing > this.worldMs ? 2 : 1;
    const hpRate = (0.15 + this.sta * 0.02) * bless * (this.cls === "vampire" && this.blessing > this.worldMs ? 1.5 : 1);
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + hpRate * dt);
    if (this.cls !== "vampire") {
      // [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02 Bill]: regen is the old rate times MANA.regen (a fresh wizard: 0.30 -> 0.90 MP/s,
      // a Smite back every ~13 s instead of ~40 s).
      const er = (0.12 + this.fort * 0.03) * bless * MANA.regen;
      this.energy = Math.min(this.maxEnergy, this.energy + er * dt);
      if (this.stam < 40) this.stam = Math.min(40, this.stam + 8 * dt);
    } else if (this.blood < 20) this.blood = Math.min(20, this.blood + 2 * dt);
  }

  /**
   * Walk, swim, or slide.
   * Ice keeps momentum after the key is released. Land and water do not coast.
   * Swimming is half of walking. Bog-step boots treat water as land.
   */
  private move(dt: number) {
    let x = 0;
    let y = 0;
    if (this.held.has(this.keyBind.up) || this.held.has("ArrowUp")) y -= 1;
    if (this.held.has(this.keyBind.down) || this.held.has("ArrowDown")) y += 1;
    if (this.held.has(this.keyBind.left) || this.held.has("ArrowLeft")) x -= 1;
    if (this.held.has(this.keyBind.right) || this.held.has("ArrowRight")) x += 1;
    if (x !== 0 || y !== 0 || this.stickX || this.stickY) this.goal = null;
    if (this.stickX || this.stickY) {
      x += this.stickX;
      y += this.stickY;
    }
    if ((x === 0 && y === 0) && this.goal) {
      const dx = this.goal.x - this.px;
      const dy = this.goal.y - this.py;
      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 6) this.goal = null;
      else {
        x = dx;
        y = dy;
      }
    }
    const len = Math.hypot(x, y);
    const wishX = len > 0.01 ? x / len : 0;
    const wishY = len > 0.01 ? y / len : 0;
    const kind = this.groundKind();
    const beforeX = this.px;
    const beforeY = this.py;
    /** Accelerate, then bleed speed. A small leftover velocity is dropped so the hero does not creep. */
    if (kind === "ice") {
      this.wading = false;
      if (len > 0.01) {
        this.slideX += wishX * 120 * dt;
        this.slideY += wishY * 120 * dt;
      }
      const drag = Math.exp(-0.65 * dt);
      this.slideX *= drag;
      this.slideY *= drag;
      const mag = Math.hypot(this.slideX, this.slideY);
      const cap = 100;
      if (mag > cap) {
        this.slideX = (this.slideX / mag) * cap;
        this.slideY = (this.slideY / mag) * cap;
      }
      if (mag > 4) this.try(this.slideX * dt, this.slideY * dt);
      this.moving = false;
      this.sliding = mag > 10;
      if (this.sliding) {
        if (Math.abs(this.slideX) > Math.abs(this.slideY)) this.facing = this.slideX > 0 ? "e" : "w";
        else this.facing = this.slideY > 0 ? "s" : "n";
      }
    } else {
      this.slideX = 0;
      this.slideY = 0;
      this.sliding = false;
      this.wading = kind === "water";
      if (len > 0.01) {
        /** 37 is half of 74. Water never inherits the ice slide. */
        const sp = kind === "water" ? 37 : this.running ? 110 : 74;
        this.try(wishX * sp * dt, wishY * sp * dt);
        if (Math.abs(x) > Math.abs(y)) this.facing = x > 0 ? "e" : "w";
        else this.facing = y > 0 ? "s" : "n";
      }
    }
    const moved = Math.hypot(this.px - beforeX, this.py - beforeY);
    if (kind !== "ice") this.moving = moved > 0.2 && len > 0.01;
    if (this.goal) {
      this.goalStuck = moved < 0.2 ? this.goalStuck + 1 : 0;
      if (this.goalStuck > 10) {
        this.goal = null;
        this.goalStuck = 0;
      }
    }
    if (moved > 0.2) {
      this.walk += dt;
      this.stuckT = 0;
      if (this.walk > 3.4) {
        this.walk = 0;
        this.rollEncounter();
      }
    } else if (len > 0.2) {
      this.stuckT += dt;
      if (this.stuckT > 0.8) {
        this.stuckT = 0;
        this.goal = null;
        this.slideX = 0;
        this.slideY = 0;
        this.nudge("hero", Math.sign(wishX) || 1, Math.sign(wishY) || 0);
      }
    } else this.stuckT = 0;
    if (this.wading && !this.notedWade) {
      this.notedWade = true;
      this.logLine = WADE_LINE;
    }
    // playtest1b (owner-reported 2026-10-02 01:03 ET, "swim message on dry town cobble"): the wade line used to stay in
    // the log after you climbed out, so it still read "You sink in" on the road. Leaving the water now says so.
    if (!this.wading && this.notedWade && this.logLine === WADE_LINE) this.logLine = DRY_LINE;
    if (!this.wading) this.notedWade = false;
    if (this.sliding && !this.notedSlide) {
      this.notedSlide = true;
      this.logLine = "The ice takes your feet.";
    }
    if (!this.sliding) this.notedSlide = false;
    this.followCompanion(dt);
    this.tickAlly(dt);
    // After the companion's own swing or cast, so its call-out is the line you read this frame.
    this.callTraps();
    this.edgeCheck();
    this.checkDoor(dt);
    this.tickField(dt);
  }

  setGoal(x: number, y: number) {
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    // playtest1f: a tap on a wayrift's art walks you into its mouth (the stones would stop a straight line to the tap)
    const way = this.mapId === "world" ? wayArtAt(x, y, TILE) : undefined;
    if (way) {
      const m = wayMouth(way);
      const f = wayFront(way);
      const below = this.py >= f.y * TILE;
      x = m.x * TILE + 8;
      y = (below ? m.y : f.y) * TILE + 8;
    }
    this.goal = { x, y };
    this.goalStuck = 0;
  }

  bumpZoom(dir: number) {
    const steps = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6];
    let i = steps.indexOf(this.zoom);
    if (i < 0) i = 1;
    this.zoom = steps[Math.max(0, Math.min(steps.length - 1, i + dir))];
  }

  /** Move on x, then on y, so a corner slides instead of sticking. */
  private try(dx: number, dy: number) {
    const ox = this.px;
    const oy = this.py;
    this.px += dx;
    if (this.solidFeet()) this.px = ox;
    this.py += dy;
    if (this.solidFeet()) this.py = oy;
    this.slideOffProps();
    if (this.solidFeet()) {
      this.px = ox;
      this.py = oy;
    }
    this.barHome();
  }

  private solidFeet() {
    const pts = [
      [this.px - 5, this.py - 2],
      [this.px + 5, this.py - 2],
      [this.px - 5, this.py + 3],
      [this.px + 5, this.py + 3],
    ];
    return pts.some(([x, y]) => this.solidAt(x, y, true));
  }

  private tileUnder(px: number, py: number) {
    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h) return -1;
    return this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
  }

  private wet(t: number) {
    return t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.ice;
  }

  hasWaterWalk() {
    return this.equip.feet?.special === "Waterwalk";
  }

  /**
   * playtest1 (owner-reported bug 2026-10-01): you swim only once your whole foot box (py - 2 .. py + 3, the same box
   * solidFeet uses) is over water. Before, the one pixel at your heel decided, so a hero on the road with a heel
   * over the pond's top row read as swimming.
   */
  private wetFeet(x: number, y: number) {
    const a = this.tileUnder(x, y - 2);
    const b = this.tileUnder(x, y + 3);
    return (a === T.water || a === T.pool) && (b === T.water || b === T.pool);
  }

  /** Ice wins over water. Water-walking boots report land, so the pose is a walk. */
  private groundKind(): "land" | "water" | "ice" {
    const t = this.tileUnder(this.px, this.py);
    if (t === T.ice) return "ice";
    if (this.wetFeet(this.px, this.py) && !this.hasWaterWalk()) return "water";
    return "land";
  }

  poseFor(x: number, y: number, moving: boolean, waterwalk: boolean): "stand" | "walk" | "wade" | "slide" {
    const t = this.tileUnder(x, y);
    if (this.wetFeet(x, y) && !waterwalk) return "wade";
    if (t === T.ice) return moving ? "slide" : "stand";
    return moving ? "walk" : "stand";
  }

  /** Walls and furnishings stop everyone. Water, pools, and ice stop everyone but the hero. */
  private solidAt(px: number, py: number, hero = false) {
    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
    // playtest1: off the town map only through a road gate; the rest of the edge is a fence.
    if (this.mapId === "town" && (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h)) return !townGateSide(Math.max(0, Math.min(this.w - 1, tx)), Math.max(0, Math.min(this.h - 1, ty)));
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h) return true;
    const t = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
    if (t === T.wall || t === T.shelf || t === T.cauldron || t === T.moon || secretTile(t)) return true;
    // playtest1f: a wayrift's standing stones (its footprint but the mouth) stop everyone
    if (this.mapId === "world" && waySolid(tx, ty)) return true;
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon" && this.room.solid.has(ty * this.w + tx)) return true; // playtest1n: a standing room prop
    // Item 10: bought furniture stands in the croft and stops everyone, like any furnishing.
    if (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside === "croft" && this.decorAt(tx, ty)) return true;
    /** The hero may enter. Townsfolk, critters, and roamers may not. */
    if (t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.ice) return !hero;
    return false;
  }

  /** Trees, rocks, pumps, and graves push the hero out. They are not whole solid tiles. */
  private slideOffProps() {
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    for (let y = ty - 2; y <= ty + 2; y++) {
      for (let x = tx - 2; x <= tx + 2; x++) {
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.w || y >= this.h) continue;
        const t = this.tiles[y * this.w + x];
        if (t !== T.tree && t !== T.rock && t !== T.pump && t !== T.grave) continue;
        this.pushFrom(x * TILE + 8, y * TILE + 12, t === T.tree ? 5 : 6);
      }
    }
    for (const n of this.npcs) this.pushFrom(n.x, n.y - 4, 7);
  }

  private pushFrom(cx: number, cy: number, rad: number) {
    const dx = this.px - cx;
    const dy = this.py - cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.001;
    if (dist < rad) {
      this.px += (dx / dist) * (rad - dist);
      this.py += (dy / dist) * (rad - dist);
    }
  }

  /**
   * playtest1: the vale tile on one side of the town door (32,44): south is the road it always used (32,46); north,
   * west and east are two tiles off the door when that tile is open ground, else the south road.
   */
  private worldBeside(side: TownSide) {
    const D = WORLD_DOOR;
    const at = { s: [GATE.x, GATE.y], n: [D.x, D.y - 2], w: [D.x - 2, D.y], e: [D.x + 2, D.y] }[side];
    if (side !== "s") {
      const world = buildWorld();
      const t = world.tiles[at[1] * world.w + at[0]];
      const open = t === T.road || t === T.grass || t === T.dirt || t === T.swamp || t === T.ash || t === T.sand || t === T.snow;
      if (!open) return { x: GATE.x * TILE + 8, y: GATE.y * TILE + 8 };
    }
    return { x: at[0] * TILE + 8, y: at[1] * TILE + 8 };
  }

  /** playtest1: walking into the vale's town door arrives at the town gate on the side you came from. */
  private enterTownFrom(facing: "n" | "s" | "e" | "w") {
    const side: TownSide = facing === "n" ? "s" : facing === "s" ? "n" : facing === "e" ? "w" : "e";
    const at = TOWN_ARRIVE[side];
    this.enterTown(at.x, at.y);
    this.facing = at.facing;
  }

  private edgeCheck() {
    if (this.mapId !== "town") return;
    if (this.px < 8 || this.py < 8 || this.px > this.w * TILE - 8 || this.py > this.h * TILE - 8) {
      // playtest1: only a road gate leads out, and it leads out on its own side of the vale's town door.
      const side = townGateSide(Math.max(0, Math.min(this.w - 1, Math.floor(this.px / TILE))), Math.max(0, Math.min(this.h - 1, Math.floor(this.py / TILE))));
      if (!side) return;
      const out = this.worldBeside(side);
      this.enterWorld(out.x, out.y);
      this.facing = side === "n" ? "n" : side === "s" ? "s" : side === "w" ? "w" : "e";
      this.doorCool = 0.45;
      this.logLine = "The vale is gloomy even at noon. Roads are quieter than the brush.";
      if (this.level < 3) this.logLine = "Stay on the road. The east bridge is not for a first night.";
    }
  }

  /** Step onto a door and the building becomes its own room. The door inside leads back out. */
  private checkDoor(dt: number) {
    // playtest1b (owner-reported 2026-10-01, the camp door cooldown pass-through): the camp's south mark asks the
    // moment your feet reach it, or slip past it into the tree line below, cooldown or not. Before, walking south on
    // arrival crossed the mark inside the 0.45 s door cooldown and the trees slid you off it, so it never asked.
    if (this.mapId === "camp" && this.mode === "play" && !this.askLeave && this.onCampMark()) {
      this.doorCool = 0;
      this.askLeaveCamp();
      return;
    }
    if (this.doorCool > 0) {
      this.doorCool = Math.max(0, this.doorCool - dt);
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    if (this.riftHold && (this.mapId !== "world" || this.riftHold !== `${tx},${ty}`)) this.riftHold = "";
    if (this.tryDoor(tx, ty)) return;
    this.tryEntrance(tx, ty);
  }

  /** A gate or a stair takes you the moment you stand on it. No key. */
  private tryEntrance(tx: number, ty: number) {
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h) return;
    const here = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
    const hearth = (this.mapId === "town" || (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside === "chapel")) && here === T.hearth;
    const grave = this.mapId === "town" && here === T.stairD;
    const pit = this.mapId === "dungeon" && (here === T.stairD || here === T.stairU);
    const gate = this.mapId === "world" && here === T.door;
    const mouth = this.mapId === "world" ? this.entrances[`${tx},${ty}`] : "";
    const rift = this.mapId === "world" && !this.riftHold ? RIFTS.find((r) => r.tx === tx && r.ty === ty) : undefined;
    if (this.wayHold) {
      const held = wayriftById(this.wayHold);
      const m = held && wayMouth(held);
      if (!m || this.mapId !== "world" || tx !== m.x || (ty !== m.y && ty !== m.y + 1)) this.wayHold = "";
    }
    const way = this.mapId === "world" && !this.riftHold && !this.travel ? wayMouthAt(tx, ty) : undefined;
    if (way && way.id === this.wayHold) return;
    if (way) {
      this.startTravel(way);
      return;
    }
    if (rift && !hearth && !grave && !pit && !gate && !mouth) {
      this.enterDungeon(rift.id);
      this.doorCool = 0.45;
      return;
    }
    if (!hearth && !grave && !pit && !gate && !mouth) return;
    const before = `${this.mapId}:${this.floor}:${this.dungeon}`;
    if (hearth || grave || mouth) this.climbUntil = this.worldMs + 500; // playtest1: down the ladder
    if (hearth) this.enterDungeon("hearth");
    else if (grave) this.enterDungeon("grave");
    else if (pit) this.useStair(here);
    else if (gate) {
      this.audio?.door();
      this.enterTownFrom(this.facing);
      this.logLine = "The town keeps its twilight.";
    } else this.enterDungeon(mouth);
    const after = `${this.mapId}:${this.floor}:${this.dungeon}`;
    this.doorCool = 0.45;
    if (before === after) {
      const off = stepOff(this.tiles, this.w, this.h, tx, ty);
      this.px = off.x * TILE + 8;
      this.py = off.y * TILE + 8;
      this.doorCool = 0.7;
    }
  }

  private tryDoor(tx: number, ty: number) {
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= this.w || ty >= this.h) return false;
    if (this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx] !== T.door) return false;
    if (this.mapId === "town") {
      const room = roomByDoor(tx, ty);
      if (!room) return false;
      // playtest1 (owner-reported bug): a house door opens only from the front, walking north into it from its stoop.
      if (this.py < ty * TILE + 8) return false;
      this.enterInside(room.id);
      return true;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "inside") {
      this.leaveInside();
      return true;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "camp") {
      // playtest1: the south mark asks first.
      this.askLeaveCamp();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /** Load one building. forced skips the cottage door, for a save or the hearth stair. */
  enterInside(id: string, forced = false) {
    const room = ROOMS.find((r) => r.id === id);
    if (!room) return;
    if (!forced && room.id === "croft" && !this.ownedHome) {
      this.px = room.outX * TILE + 8;
      this.py = room.outY * TILE + 8;
      this.doorCool = 0.6;
      this.logLine = "The door is barred. The bank sells the deed.";
      return;
    }
    if (!forced && room.home && this.cls === "vampire" && (this.favor[room.home] ?? 0) < 3) {
      this.px = room.outX * TILE + 8;
      this.py = room.outY * TILE + 8;
      this.doorCool = 0.6;
      this.logLine = `${room.who ?? "They"} has not invited you. A trick, or the gift they want, opens the door.`;
      return;
    }
    const built = buildRoom(room.kind);
    this.mapId = "inside";
    this.inside = room.id;
    this.theme = `room:${room.kind}`;
    this.fog = null;
    this.w = built.w;
    this.h = built.h;
    this.tiles = built.tiles;
    this.npcs = this.roomFolk(room);
    this.roamers = [];
    this.entrances = {};
    this.critters = [];
    this.px = 7 * TILE + 8;
    this.py = 8 * TILE + 10;
    this.facing = "n";
    this.doorCool = 0.45;
    this.audio?.door();
    this.logLine = `${room.name}. The door behind you leads out.`;
  }

  private leaveInside() {
    const room = ROOMS.find((r) => r.id === this.inside);
    const x = (room?.outX ?? 20) * TILE + 8;
    const y = (room?.outY ?? 14) * TILE + 8;
    this.enterTown(x, y);
    if (room) {
      if (room.outX > room.doorX) this.facing = "e";
      else if (room.outX < room.doorX) this.facing = "w";
      else if (room.outY > room.doorY) this.facing = "s";
      else this.facing = "n";
    }
    this.doorCool = 0.45;
    this.logLine = room ? `You step out of ${room.name}.` : "You step back into the street.";
  }

  private roomFolk(room: RoomDef): Npc[] {
    const gone = this.companion?.sourceId ?? "";
    const at = (id: string, name: string, role: string, coat: string, tx: number, ty: number, look?: string): Npc => ({
      id,
      name,
      role,
      coat,
      look,
      x: tx * TILE + 8,
      y: ty * TILE + 12,
    });
    const list: Npc[] = [];
    const keep = (id: string) => gone !== id;
    if (room.id === "inn") list.push(at("inn", "Innkeeper", "inn", "#6a3a28", 4, 4));
    if (room.id === "shop") list.push(at("shop", "Shopkeeper", "shop", "#2a4a38", 8, 4));
    if (room.id === "guild") list.push(at("guild", "Guild Trainer", "guild", "#2a3a6a", 8, 4));
    if (room.id === "bank") list.push(at("bank", "Teller", "bank", "#1e3a32", 8, 4));
    if (room.id === "chapel") {
      list.push(at("priest", "Priest", "priest", "#e6e0d4", 4, 4, "priest"));
      if (keep("acolyte")) list.push(at("acolyte", "Acolyte Bren", "wander", "#e6e0d4", 9, 5, "priest"));
    }
    if (room.id === "casino") {
      list.push(at("dealer", "Dealer", "casino", "#8a2030", 7, 3));
      list.push(at("patron1", "Gambler", "patron", "#3a1830", 4, 5, "patron"));
      list.push(at("patron2", "Gambler", "patron", "#1a2838", 10, 5, "patron"));
    }
    if (room.id === "smith") list.push(at("smith", "Blacksmith", "smith", "#5a4030", 5, 4, "smith"));
    if (room.id === "tailor") list.push(at("tailor", "Tailor", "tailor", "#6a4060", 8, 4));
    if (room.id === "alchemy") {
      list.push(at("alchemist", "Madam Yarrow", "alchemist", "#4a1848", 5, 4, "witch"));
      if (keep("vetch") && keep("witch")) list.push(at("vetch", "Sister Vetch", "wander", "#4a1848", 9, 5, "witch"));
    }
    if (room.id === "mystic") list.push(at("mystic", "Magus Quill", "mystic", "#2a1848", 5, 4, "mystic"));
    if (room.id === "fisher") list.push(at("fisher", "Hookkeep Vell", "fisher", "#1c3040", 8, 4, "fisher"));
    if (room.home) {
      const folk = FOLK.find((f) => f.id === room.home);
      const day = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
      const homeNow =
        !!folk && this.phase === "night" && (folk.night === "home" || (folk.night === "sometimes" && hash(day, folk.id.length, 4) <= 0.45));
      if (folk && homeNow && keep(folk.id)) list.push(at(folk.id, folk.name, "folk", folk.coat, 8, 4, KITS[folk.kit].look));
    }
    return list;
  }

  private rollEncounter() {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.calm > 0 || this.mapId === "town" || this.mapId === "inside" || this.mapId === "camp") return;
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.phase === "night") return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const t = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
    if (t === T.road || t === T.cobble || t === T.door || t === T.stairD || t === T.stairU) {
      if (Math.random() < 0.07) this.startWildFight(false);
      return;
    }
    const bridge = this.mapId === "world" && tx === WORLD_BRIDGE.x && ty >= WORLD_BRIDGE.y0 && ty <= WORLD_BRIDGE.y1;
    if (bridge && this.level < 3) {
      if (!this.warnedBridge) {
        this.warnedBridge = true;
        this.logLine = "Turn back. This crossing is not for level 1.";
      }
      if (Math.random() < 0.55) this.startWildFight(true);
      return;
    }
    const chance = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? 0.2 : 0.24;
    if (Math.random() < chance) this.startWildFight(false);
  }

  private startWildFight(hard: boolean) {
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const lv = (this.mapId === "dungeon" ? this.dungeonLevel() : zoneLevel(tx, ty, this.level)) + (hard ? 2 : 0);
    const riftDef = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? dungeonById(this.dungeon) : undefined;
    const picks = riftDef?.rift ? familiesFor(riftDef.tx, riftDef.ty, "world") : familiesFor(tx, ty, this.mapId);
    // Item 13: out in the wild the season weights the biome's own families; dungeons keep the even pick.
    const def = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? picks[Math.floor(Math.random() * picks.length)] : seasonPick(picks, this.season(), Math.random());
    const count = lv > this.level + 4 ? 2 : 1;
    const foes: Foe[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const s = scaleMonster(def, lv);
      foes.push({ ...s, hp: s.hp, max: s.hp });
    }
    if (Math.random() < 1 / 14) {
      const pack = this.stalkerPack(def, lv, Math.random() < 0.5 ? "fast" : "vortex");
      foes[0] = pack[0];
      foes.push(...pack.slice(1));
    }
    this.openBattle(foes, { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
  }

  /** A Family Stalker is a rare, and a rare is a pack: the rare (L+2, HP*1.45, one affix) and two minions. */
  stalkerPack(def: MonsterDef, lv: number, affix: string, name = `${def.name} Stalker`): Foe[] {
    const rare = scaleMonster(def, lv + 2);
    rare.name = name;
    rare.hp = Math.round(rare.hp * 1.45);
    const out: Foe[] = [{ ...rare, hp: rare.hp, max: rare.hp, rare: true, affix }];
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const m = scaleMonster(def, Math.max(1, lv - 1));
      m.hp = Math.round(m.hp * 0.45);
      m.name = "Pack";
      out.push({ ...m, hp: m.hp, max: m.hp, helper: true });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * OWNER-APPROVED EXCEPTION 2026-09-30: the mimic is the one solo rare. No pack affix, no minions.
   * The Stalker's rare numbers on the one curve: L+2, HP*1.45.
   */
  mimicFoe(): Foe {
    const s = scaleMonster(MIMIC_DEF, this.dungeonLevel() + MIMICS.levelUp);
    s.hp = Math.round(s.hp * MIMICS.hpMult);
    return { ...s, hp: s.hp, max: s.hp, rare: true, mimic: true };
  }

  private tickRoamers(dt: number) {
    if (this.mapId === "town" || this.mapId === "inside" || this.mapId === "camp") return;
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.phase === "day") {
      // OWNER-APPROVED FIX 2026-10-01 (day-sweep): the day clears wanderers, never a foe already in a fight.
      // A festival boss leaves at dawn too, unless you are fighting it.
      this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((r) => (r.boss && !r.festival) || r.mini || r.aggro);
    } else if (this.mapId === "world" && this.roamers.filter((r) => !r.boss && !r.mini && !r.bounty && !r.naughty).length < 3) {
      this.spawnNightRoamer(dt);
    }
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.phase === "night") this.spawnBounty();
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.phase === "night") this.spawnFestival();
    for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
      if (this.mode !== "play") break;
      if ((r.stun ?? 0) > 0 || (r.shoveT ?? 0) > 0) continue; // playtest1n [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03 15:41 ET: use-the-room combat]: held by the room
      if (r.boss || r.mini) {
        const gap = Math.abs(r.level - this.level) / Math.max(8, r.level);
        const rad = (1 - Math.min(1, gap)) * 64 + 16;
        if (Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) < rad) this.touchFoe(r);
      } else {
        r.ang += Math.sin(this.frame / 20 + r.x) * 0.02;
        const nx = r.x + Math.cos(r.ang) * 18 * dt;
        const ny = r.y + Math.sin(r.ang) * 14 * dt;
        const beforeX = r.x;
        const beforeY = r.y;
        if (this.solidAt(nx, ny) || this.blockedProp(nx, ny)) {
          r.ang += 1.8;
          r.stuck = (r.stuck ?? 0) + dt;
          if ((r.stuck ?? 0) > 1.1) {
            r.stuck = 0;
            this.nudge(r, Math.cos(r.ang), Math.sin(r.ang));
            if (this.solidAt(r.x, r.y) || this.blockedProp(r.x, r.y)) {
              const spot = this.openNear(r.x, r.y);
              r.x = spot.x;
              r.y = spot.y;
            }
          }
        } else {
          r.x = nx;
          r.y = ny;
          r.stuck = Math.hypot(r.x - beforeX, r.y - beforeY) < 0.15 ? (r.stuck ?? 0) + dt : 0;
          if ((r.stuck ?? 0) > 1.1) {
            r.stuck = 0;
            this.nudge(r, Math.cos(r.ang), Math.sin(r.ang));
            if (this.solidAt(r.x, r.y) || this.blockedProp(r.x, r.y)) {
              const spot = this.openNear(r.x, r.y);
              r.x = spot.x;
              r.y = spot.y;
            }
          }
        }
        if (Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) < 12) this.touchFoe(r);
      }
    }
  }

  private nightCool = 0;

  private spawnNightRoamer(dt: number) {
    this.nightCool -= dt;
    if (this.nightCool > 0 || this.calm > 0) return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    if (this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx] === T.road) return;
    this.nightCool = 7;
    const picks = familiesFor(tx, ty, "world");
    for (let n = 0; n < 6; n++) {
      const a = hash(tx, ty, n + Math.floor(this.frame)) * Math.PI * 2;
      const x = this.px + Math.cos(a) * 96;
      const y = this.py + Math.sin(a) * 72;
      if (this.solidAt(x, y)) continue;
      const def = seasonPick(picks, this.season(), hash(tx, ty, n + 31 + Math.floor(this.frame)));
      this.roamers.push({
        id: `r${this.worldMs}-${n}`,
        x,
        y,
        family: def.family,
        tint: def.tint,
        def: def.id,
        level: zoneLevel(Math.floor(x / TILE), Math.floor(y / TILE), this.level),
        ang: a,
      });
      return;
    }
  }

  private touchFoe(r: Roamer) {
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    // OWNER-APPROVED COMBAT FIX 2026-09-30: a foe already in the fight is not a fresh meeting. It keeps
    // its HP, its pack-minion tag, its rare status, and its affix; nothing is rebuilt.
    if (r.aggro) return;
    if (r.naughty && !r.boss) {
      this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
      const pack = this.stalkerPack(monsterById(r.def), r.level, r.affix ?? "fast", r.name);
      pack[0].naughty = r.naughty;
      this.openBattle(pack, { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      return;
    }
    if (r.pack) {
      // Map writer: the boss-room guard and a rift's Stalker are Family Stalkers, built by the one pack builder.
      this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
      const pack = this.stalkerPack(monsterById(r.def), r.level, r.affix ?? "fast", r.name);
      if (r.pack === "guard") pack[0].guard = this.guardKey();
      this.openBattle(pack, { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      return;
    }
    if (r.riftGoblin) {
      this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
      const g = scaleMonster(monsterById("goblin"), Math.max(3, this.level));
      this.openBattle([{ ...g, id: "goblin", hp: g.hp, max: g.hp }], { guard: false, goblin: true, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      this.logLine = "Something small is running off with the ash.";
      return;
    }
    if (r.mimic || r.bounty) {
      this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
      const post = this.bountyPost();
      const foes = r.mimic ? [this.mimicFoe()] : post ? this.stalkerPack(monsterById(post.id), r.level, post.affix, post.name) : [];
      if (!foes.length) return;
      if (r.bounty) foes[0].bounty = true;
      this.openBattle(foes, { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      return;
    }
    const base = monsterById(r.def);
    const s = scaleMonster(base, r.level);
    const name = r.mini ? (MINI_NAME[r.def] ?? `${base.name} remnant`) : s.name;
    const foe: Foe = { ...s, id: r.def, name, hp: s.hp, max: s.hp, boss: !!r.boss, mini: !!r.mini, festival: r.festival };
    this.pending = r.boss || r.mini ? { ...r } : null;
    this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
    const foes = [foe];
    if (r.boss) {
      const kit = BOSS_KITS[r.def];
      const help = FAMILIES.find((f) => f.id === kit?.helper) ?? FAMILIES[0];
      const n = helperCount(this.level);
      const linked = kit?.personality === "tyrant" || r.def === "rootwidow";
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const h = scaleMonster(help, Math.max(1, Math.round(r.level * 0.55)));
        h.hp = Math.round(h.hp * 0.45);
        h.name = help.name;
        foes.push({ ...h, hp: h.hp, max: h.hp, helper: true, affix: linked ? "linked" : undefined });
      }
    }
    this.openBattle(foes, { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
  }

  private tickCritters(dt: number) {
    for (const c of this.critters) {
      c.x += c.vx * dt;
      if (this.solidAt(c.x, c.y) || this.blockedProp(c.x, c.y)) {
        c.vx *= -1;
        c.stuck = (c.stuck ?? 0) + dt;
        if ((c.stuck ?? 0) > 1) {
          c.stuck = 0;
          this.nudge(c, Math.sign(c.vx) || 1, 0);
        }
      } else c.stuck = 0;
      if (Math.hypot(c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py) < 26) c.vx = Math.sign(c.x - this.px) * 28 || 18;
    }
  }

  private tickLinger(dt: number) {
    if (!this.talk || this.cls !== "vampire" || this.phase !== "day" || this.mapId !== "town") {
      this.linger = 0;
      return;
    }
    const role = this.talk.role;
    if (role === "undertaker" || role === "folk" || role === "guard" || role === "portal" || role === "wander") {
      this.linger = 0;
      return;
    }
    this.linger += dt;
    if (this.linger < 12) return;
    this.linger = 0;
    const who = this.talk.role;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
    if (who === "hunter") this.fightHunter();
    else this.callGuards(who);
  }

  private reveal() {
    if (!this.fog) return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    for (let y = ty - 4; y <= ty + 4; y++) {
      for (let x = tx - 4; x <= tx + 4; x++) {
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.w || y >= this.h) continue;
        if ((x - tx) ** 2 + (y - ty) ** 2 <= 18) this.fog[y * this.w + x] = 1;
      }
    }
  }

  private noteBiome() {
    if (this.mapId !== "world") return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const name = biomeName(tx, ty);
    if (name === this.seenBiome) return;
    this.seenBiome = name;
    const hint =
      name === "Winter hollow"
        ? "Snow road. The Wolfman keeps it."
        : name === "Dry waste"
          ? "Sand and wrappings. Carrion Saint is east."
          : name === "Cinder"
            ? "Ash fields. The mare runs here."
            : name === "Scourge swamp"
              ? "The center of the map. Do not rush it."
              : "Decayed vale. The gallows stand off the west road.";
    this.logLine = `${name}. ${hint}`;
  }

  /** E. Doors, stairs, chests, and the nearest person, in that order. */
  interact() {
    if (this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "battle") return;
    if (this.mode === "fish") {
      this.reel();
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "talk" || this.mode === "shop" || this.mode === "bank" || this.mode === "casino" || this.mode === "zeppelin") {
      this.mode = "play";
      this.talk = null;
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    if (this.mapId === "camp" && this.companion && Math.hypot(this.companion.x - this.px, this.companion.y - this.py) < 28) {
      const c = this.companion;
      this.talk = {
        who: c.name,
        role: "companion",
        npcId: c.id,
        text: this.campTalk(),
      };
      this.mode = "talk";
      return;
    }
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const here = this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx];
    if (this.tryDoor(tx, ty) || this.tryDoor(tx + (this.facing === "e" ? 1 : this.facing === "w" ? -1 : 0), ty + (this.facing === "s" ? 1 : this.facing === "n" ? -1 : 0))) return;
    if ((this.mapId === "town" || (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside === "chapel")) && here === T.hearth) {
      this.enterDungeon("hearth");
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "town" && here === T.stairD) {
      this.enterDungeon("grave");
      return;
    }
    if (here === T.chest) {
      this.openChest(tx, ty);
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon" && (here === T.stairD || here === T.stairU)) {
      this.useStair(here);
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon" && this.freeCaptive()) return;
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon" && this.touchSecret()) return;
    if (this.mapId === "world" && here === T.door) {
      this.audio?.door();
      this.enterTownFrom(this.facing);
      this.logLine = "The town keeps its twilight.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "world" && this.entrances[`${tx},${ty}`]) {
      this.enterDungeon(this.entrances[`${tx},${ty}`]);
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId === "inside" && this.inside === "guild" && this.facing === "n" && tx === BOUNTY.boardX && ty === BOUNTY.boardY + 1) {
      this.readBoard();
      return;
    }
    // Item 8: hold Main facing a town grave to dig it (night, with a spade).
    const gx = tx + (this.facing === "e" ? 1 : this.facing === "w" ? -1 : 0);
    const gy = ty + (this.facing === "s" ? 1 : this.facing === "n" ? -1 : 0);
    if (this.mapId === "town" && this.tiles[gy * this.w + gx] === T.grave) {
      this.digGrave(gx, gy);
      return;
    }
    let best: Npc | null = null;
    let bestD = 42;
    for (const n of this.npcs) {
      const d = Math.hypot(n.x - this.px, n.y - this.py);
      if (d < bestD) {
        best = n;
        bestD = d;
      }
    }
    const animal = this.critters.find((c) => Math.hypot(c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py) < 20);
    if (animal && !(best && bestD < 24)) {
      this.fightAnimal(animal);
      return;
    }
    if (!best && !this.nearFish()) {
      this.logLine = here === T.grave ? "A quiet stone. The open grave is the one with the ladder." : "Nothing here answers.";
      return;
    }
    if (!(best && bestD < 24) && this.nearFish()) {
      this.castLine();
      return;
    }
    if (!best) {
      this.logLine = "Nothing here answers.";
      return;
    }
    this.openTalk(best);
  }

  /** A town animal is a small fight, not scenery. */
  private fightAnimal(c: Critter) {
    this.critters = this.critters.filter((x) => x !== c);
    const base = monsterById(c.kind === "bat" ? "bat" : "zombie");
    const s = scaleMonster(base, 1);
    s.name = c.kind === "bat" ? "Bat" : c.kind === "rat" ? "Alley rat" : "Stray cat";
    s.hp = Math.max(4, Math.round(s.hp * 0.4));
    s.xp = 4;
    s.silver = 1;
    this.openBattle([{ ...s, hp: s.hp, max: s.hp }], { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
    this.logLine = `${s.name} turns on you.`;
  }

  private useStair(here: number) {
    if (this.escortHolds()) return;
    this.climbUntil = this.worldMs + 500;
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    const max = def?.floors ?? 5;
    if (here === T.stairD) {
      if (this.floor >= max) {
        this.logLine = "The stair ends.";
        return;
      }
      this.floor += 1;
      this.audio?.door();
      this.loadFloor("down");
      this.noteCurse(false);
      return;
    }
    if (this.floor <= 1 && def?.rift) {
      this.leaveRift();
      return;
    }
    if (this.floor <= 1) {
      this.audio?.door();
      const home = this.bringOut();
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("run:")) this.opened.delete(k);
      this.curse = "";
      if (def?.town || this.dungeon === "grave" || this.dungeon === "hearth") {
        if (this.dungeon === "hearth") this.enterInside("chapel", true);
        else this.enterTown(this.dungeon === "grave" ? 14 * TILE : 6 * TILE, this.dungeon === "grave" ? 18 * TILE : 20 * TILE);
      } else this.enterWorld(Math.max(TILE, (def?.tx ?? WORLD_DOOR.x) * TILE), Math.max(TILE, ((def?.ty ?? 80) + 1) * TILE));
      this.logLine = home ? `You climb back into the open air. ${home}` : "You climb back into the open air.";
      return;
    }
    this.floor -= 1;
    this.audio?.door();
    this.loadFloor("up");
    this.noteCurse(false);
  }

  private openChest(tx: number, ty: number) {
    // playtest1e: a vale trail cache is keyed by its tile alone (the last dungeon's id and floor linger on the vale).
    const key = this.mapId === "world" ? `world:cache:${tx}:${ty}` : `${this.mapId}:${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}:${tx}:${ty}`;
    const mim = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? this.feats?.mimic : undefined;
    if (mim && mim.x === tx && mim.y === ty && !this.opened.has(this.mimicKey())) {
      this.wakeMimic(mim);
      return;
    }
    if (this.opened.has(key)) {
      this.logLine = "The chest is empty.";
      return;
    }
    this.opened.add(key);
    const secret = this.secretChest(tx, ty);
    if (secret) {
      // Secret rooms pay from the rare table: three from a rune vault, two from a pocket room.
      const n = secret === "rune" ? 3 : 2;
      // Lay the loot on the room's own floor, nearest tiles first. These rooms are small, and the
      // usual spill to the right would land in rock out of pickup reach.
      const part = secret === "rune" ? this.feats!.rune! : this.feats!.crack!;
      const spots = this.roomTiles(part.room)
        .filter((i) => i !== ty * this.w + tx)
        .sort((a, b) => Math.hypot((a % this.w) - tx, Math.floor(a / this.w) - ty) - Math.hypot((b % this.w) - tx, Math.floor(b / this.w) - ty) || a - b);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const at = spots[i % spots.length];
        this.dropItem((at % this.w) * TILE + 8, Math.floor(at / this.w) * TILE + 10, this.makeDrop("rare", false));
      }
      this.audio?.coin();
      this.logLine = secret === "rune" ? "The vault chest. Kept dry, kept for someone." : "A stash behind the brick.";
      return;
    }
    const world = this.mapId === "world";
    const n = world ? 1 + (Math.random() < 0.4 ? 1 : 0) : 2 + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) this.dropItem(this.px + 10 + i * 12, this.py + 8, this.makeDrop(world ? "world" : "chest", false));
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = world ? "A trail cache. Modest." : "The chest gives up its dead.";
  }

  // ---- Dungeon secrets. Placement lives in feats.ts; this is how the hero uses them. ----

  private secretKey(kind: "rune" | "crack") {
    return `${kind}:${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}`;
  }

  /** A fresh or cached floor: open what this save already opened, fog the rest. */
  private applyFeats(f: FloorFeats | null) {
    this.feats = f;
    this.runeLit = [];
    this.plates = [];
    this.trapIndex = new Map((f?.traps ?? []).map((t, k) => [t.y * this.w + t.x, k]));
    this.hidden = new Set();
    if (!f) return;
    const at = (p: Spot) => p.y * this.w + p.x;
    if (f.rune) {
      if (this.opened.has(this.secretKey("rune"))) this.tiles[at(f.rune.door)] = T.floor;
      else this.roomTiles(f.rune.room).forEach((i) => this.hidden.add(i));
    }
    if (f.crack) {
      if (this.opened.has(this.secretKey("crack"))) this.tiles[at(f.crack.wall)] = T.floor;
      else this.roomTiles(f.crack.room).forEach((i) => this.hidden.add(i));
    }
  }

  private roomTiles(r: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) {
    const out: number[] = [];
    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) out.push(y * this.w + x);
    return out;
  }

  /** Lift the fog off a secret room and open its mouth. */
  private openSecret(kind: "rune" | "crack") {
    const f = this.feats;
    const part = kind === "rune" ? f?.rune : f?.crack;
    if (!f || !part) return;
    const mouth = kind === "rune" ? f.rune!.door : f.crack!.wall;
    this.tiles[mouth.y * this.w + mouth.x] = T.floor;
    for (const i of this.roomTiles(part.room)) this.hidden.delete(i);
    this.opened.add(this.secretKey(kind));
    this.audio?.door();
  }

  /** The secret tile the hero is beside: within one tile and 24 px of its middle. */
  private secretNear(kinds: number[], reach = 24) {
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    let best: Spot | null = null;
    let bestD = reach;
    for (let y = ty - 1; y <= ty + 1; y++) {
      for (let x = tx - 1; x <= tx + 1; x++) {
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.w || y >= this.h) continue;
        if (!kinds.includes(this.tiles[y * this.w + x])) continue;
        const d = Math.hypot(x * TILE + 8 - this.px, y * TILE + 8 - this.py);
        if (d <= bestD) {
          best = { x, y };
          bestD = d;
        }
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  private glyphList(order: number[]) {
    return order.map((g) => GLYPH_NAMES[g]).join(", ");
  }

  /** Main held (or tapped with no foe in reach) beside a secret. */
  private touchSecret() {
    const rune = this.feats?.rune;
    const spot = this.secretNear([T.runeDoor, T.brazier, T.statue, T.crack]);
    if (!spot) return false;
    const t = this.tiles[spot.y * this.w + spot.x];
    if (t === T.crack) {
      this.logLine = "Cold air through a hairline crack in the brick.";
      return true;
    }
    if (!rune) return false;
    if (t === T.runeDoor) {
      this.logLine =
        rune.kind === "brazier"
          ? `Sealed. Runes across the lintel: ${this.glyphList(rune.order)}. The braziers bear the same marks.`
          : `Sealed. One rune on the lintel: ${GLYPH_NAMES[rune.order[0]]}. A saint nearby wears it.`;
      return true;
    }
    const mark = rune.marks.find((m) => m.x === spot.x && m.y === spot.y);
    if (!mark) return false;
    if (t === T.statue) {
      this.logLine = `A stone saint. The ${GLYPH_NAMES[mark.glyph]} is cut in the plinth. Stone does not listen to hands.`;
      return true;
    }
    if (this.tiles[rune.door.y * this.w + rune.door.x] !== T.runeDoor) {
      this.logLine = "The brazier burns. The door is open.";
      return true;
    }
    if (this.runeLit.includes(mark.glyph)) {
      this.logLine = "That one already burns.";
      return true;
    }
    this.fx.smite(mark.x * TILE + 8, mark.y * TILE + 4);
    if (rune.order[this.runeLit.length] !== mark.glyph) {
      this.runeLit = [];
      this.logLine = `The ${GLYPH_NAMES[mark.glyph]} catches, and every flame gutters out. Wrong order.`;
      return true;
    }
    this.runeLit.push(mark.glyph);
    if (this.runeLit.length === rune.order.length) {
      this.openSecret("rune");
      this.logLine = "The third flame takes. The rune door grinds aside.";
    } else this.logLine = `The ${GLYPH_NAMES[mark.glyph]} brazier takes the flame.`;
    return true;
  }

  /** Smite with no foe in reach strikes the saint you stand beside. */
  private smiteSaint() {
    const rune = this.feats?.rune;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !rune || rune.kind !== "statue") return false;
    const spot = this.secretNear([T.statue], 40);
    if (!spot) return false;
    const mark = rune.marks.find((m) => m.x === spot.x && m.y === spot.y);
    if (!mark) return false;
    const cx = spot.x * TILE + 8;
    const cy = spot.y * TILE + 6;
    this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 12, cx, cy, "bolt");
    this.fx.smite(cx, cy);
    if (this.tiles[rune.door.y * this.w + rune.door.x] !== T.runeDoor) {
      this.logLine = "The saint is quiet. The door already stands open.";
      return true;
    }
    if (mark.glyph !== rune.order[0]) {
      this.logLine = `The ${GLYPH_NAMES[mark.glyph]} saint takes the light and keeps it. Wrong saint.`;
      return true;
    }
    this.openSecret("rune");
    this.logLine = `The ${GLYPH_NAMES[mark.glyph]} saint rings like a bell. The rune door grinds aside.`;
    return true;
  }

  /** Whirl beside cracked brick breaks it. */
  private whirlCrack() {
    const crack = this.feats?.crack;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !crack) return;
    const spot = this.secretNear([T.crack], 28);
    if (!spot) return;
    const cx = spot.x * TILE + 8;
    const cy = spot.y * TILE + 8;
    this.openSecret("crack");
    this.fx.death(cx, cy, "elite", false);
    this.fx.whirl(cx, cy);
    this.shake = Math.max(this.shake, 0.15);
