    this.logLine = "The cracked brick gives. A hollow behind it.";
  }

  /** Which secret room this chest sits in, if any. */
  private secretChest(tx: number, ty: number): "rune" | "crack" | "" {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !this.feats) return "";
    const { rune, crack } = this.feats;
    if (rune && rune.chest.x === tx && rune.chest.y === ty) return "rune";
    if (crack && crack.chest.x === tx && crack.chest.y === ty) return "crack";
    return "";
  }

  /** Speak to a resident. A barred shop or a vampire at noon gets a refusal instead of a service. */
  private openTalk(n: Npc) {
    const vamp = this.cls === "vampire";
    const day = this.phase === "day";
    if (n.role === "portal") {
      this.takePortal();
      return;
    }
    if (n.role === "hunter" && vamp) {
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: "I know what you are. Be gone before you earn a stake." };
      this.mode = "talk";
      this.linger = 0.01;
      return;
    }
    if (vamp && this.worldMs < this.hunterBanUntil && (n.role === "inn" || n.role === "casino")) {
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: "The hunter marked you. Not until night leaves him." };
      this.mode = "talk";
      return;
    }
    if (this.barredNow(n.role) || (this.barredNow("watch") && SERVICES.includes(n.role) && n.role !== "undertaker")) {
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: "Closed. The watch has not forgotten." };
      this.mode = "talk";
      this.linger = vamp && day ? 0.01 : 0;
      return;
    }
    const rented = n.role === "inn" && this.worldMs < this.rentedUntil;
    if (vamp && day && !rented && n.role !== "undertaker" && n.role !== "folk" && n.role !== "guard" && n.role !== "portal" && n.role !== "wander") {
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: "We know what walks in. Leave, before the watch is called." };
      this.mode = "talk";
      this.linger = 0.01;
      return;
    }
    if (n.role === "rescued") {
      this.talkRescued(n);
      return;
    }
    if (n.role === "festival") {
      this.talkFestival(n);
      return;
    }
    const delivered = n.role === "undertaker" ? this.takeDelivery() : "";
    if (delivered) {
      this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: delivered };
      this.mode = "talk";
      return;
    }
    const lines: Record<string, string> = {
      inn: rented ? "Your room is still yours. Eight silver fills you." : "A room keeps your chest. Eight silver. Lapsed rent costs more.",
      shop: "Potions, a spare blade, and I buy what the dead drop.",
      guild: "Train a stat, learn an art, or pay me to undo a choice.",
      priest: "A blessing for three days. Fifteen silver. The hearth behind me is a stair, not a sermon.",
      bank: "I hold coin at no fee. The drawer is shared with your own house.",
      undertaker: vamp ? "The rare cabinet is open for your kind, day or night." : "Coffin nails, a cheap draught, and a spade for the yard after dark. The good stock is not for the living.",
      hunter: "Rare steel for the living. The cabinet changes at dawn.",
      smith: "Rank two and better. Twenty silver cuts one socket.",
      tailor: "Cloth only. Twelve silver turns the stitch.",
      alchemist: "The cauldron does not sleep. Draughts, and a stone if the day allows. My sister hears offers.",
      mystic: "Tomes for the off hand. Rings. The shade behind the veil answers a wizard.",
      casino: "Red, black, or a lonely green. Points buy from the back shelf.",
      zeppelin: this.visited.filter((id) => dungeonById(id)?.zeppelin).length
        ? "The tower reaches dungeons you have already walked. Not the town stairs."
        : "Come back when a deep dungeon has seen you. The tower does not guess.",
      guard: "The watch is quiet unless called.",
      folk: "Roads are walked. The brush is not.",
      wander: "I keep someone else's hours.",
      merchant: "I pack light. Buy, or sell me your junk.",
      fisher: this.derbyTonight()
        ? `The Midnight Derby runs till dawn. Witchlit fish only, weighed here. ${derbyRival(dayOf(this.worldMs))} holds the board at ${derbyMark(dayOf(this.worldMs))} in.${this.derbyIn() ? ` Your best: ${this.derbyBest() ? `${this.derbyBest()} in` : "nothing yet"}.` : ""}`
        : derbyDay(dayOf(this.worldMs))
          ? "The Midnight Derby starts at nightfall. Biggest witchlit fish takes the trophy home."
          : "The Drowned Hook. A rib pole, nightcrawlers, and worse things for the hook. Points buy what silver should not.",
      patron: "The felt is the dealer's. I am only here to lose.",
    };
    this.talk = { who: n.name, role: n.role, npcId: n.id, text: lines[n.role] ?? "Evening." };
    this.mode = "talk";
  }

  /** Pay for the service that the open conversation offered. */
  acceptService() {
    if (!this.talk) return;
    if (this.talk.role === "companion") {
      this.mode = "play";
      this.talk = null;
      return;
    }
    const role = this.talk.role;
    const noonVampire = this.cls === "vampire" && this.phase === "day";
    if (noonVampire && role !== "undertaker" && role !== "folk" && role !== "guard" && role !== "portal" && role !== "wander" && role !== "companion") {
      this.logLine = "They will not serve you until night.";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "inn") {
      const lapsed = this.rentedUntil > 0 && this.worldMs > this.rentedUntil;
      const cost = lapsed ? 14 : 8;
      if (this.coin < cost) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough silver.";
        return;
      }
      this.coin -= cost;
      this.rentedUntil = this.worldMs + 2 * CYCLE_MS;
      this.hp = this.maxHp;
      this.energy = this.maxEnergy;
      if (this.companion) this.companion.hp = this.companionMax();
      this.audio?.coin();
      this.logLine = "You sleep. What was torn is quiet.";
      this.mode = "play";
      this.talk = null;
      this.saveSlot(0);
      return;
    }
    if (role === "priest") {
      if (this.coin < 15) {
        this.logLine = "Fifteen silver.";
        return;
      }
      this.coin -= 15;
      this.blessing = this.worldMs + 3 * CYCLE_MS;
      this.logLine = this.cls === "vampire" ? "The blessing hurries your blood for three days." : "The blessing will last three days.";
      this.mode = "play";
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (role === "smith") {
      const piece = this.inv.find((i) => (i.kind === "weapon" || i.kind === "armor" || i.kind === "jewel") && i.rank >= 2 && i.special !== "Socket");
      if (!piece || this.coin < 20) {
        this.logLine = piece ? "Twenty silver." : "Bring rank 2 gear that has no socket.";
        return;
      }
      this.coin -= 20;
      piece.special = "Socket";
      this.logLine = `${piece.name} can hold a gem.`;
      this.mode = "play";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "tailor") {
      const cloth = this.inv.find((i) => i.armor === "cloth");
      if (!cloth || this.coin < 12) {
        this.logLine = cloth ? "Twelve silver." : "Bring cloth.";
        return;
      }
      this.coin -= 12;
      cloth.ac = (cloth.ac ?? 1) + 1;
      cloth.name = `Restitched ${cloth.name}`;
      this.logLine = "The stitch turns.";
      this.mode = "play";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "shop" || role === "hunter" || role === "undertaker" || role === "alchemist" || role === "merchant" || role === "mystic" || role === "fisher") {
      this.shopRole = role;
      this.stock = this.buildStock(role);
      this.mode = "shop";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "rescued") {
      this.acceptErrand();
      return;
    }
    if (role === "guild") {
      this.shopRole = "guild";
      this.mode = "shop";
      this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 2);
      return;
    }
    if (role === "casino") {
      this.mode = "casino";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "bank") {
      this.mode = "bank";
      return;
    }
    if (role === "zeppelin") {
      this.mode = "zeppelin";
      return;
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
  }

  feed() {
    if (!this.talk || this.cls !== "vampire") return;
    if (this.talk.role !== "folk") {
      this.logLine = "Not this one.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.phase === "day") {
      this.talk = null;
      this.callGuards("street");
      return;
    }
    const night = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
    if (this.fed[this.talk.npcId] === night) {
      this.logLine = "They are already dazed tonight.";
      return;
    }
    this.fed[this.talk.npcId] = night;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + Math.round(this.maxHp * 0.22));
    this.logLine = "They slump, dazed. You leave them breathing.";
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
  }

  attackGuard() {
    if (!this.talk || this.talk.role !== "guard") return;
    this.talk = null;
    this.mode = "play";
    for (const role of SERVICES) {
      if (role === "undertaker") continue;
      this.barred[role] = nextDay(this.worldMs);
    }
    this.callGuards("watch");
  }

  private barredNow(role: string) {
    return this.worldMs < (this.barred[role] ?? 0);
  }

  private callGuards(role: string) {
    this.logLine = "Guards answer the shout.";
    if (role !== "watch") this.barred[role] = this.cls === "vampire" ? nextNight(this.worldMs) : nextDay(this.worldMs);
    const g = scaleMonster({ ...FAMILIES[1], id: "guard", name: "Town Guard", family: "skeleton", hp: 18, atk: 6, ac: 3 }, Math.max(4, this.level));
    const foes: Foe[] = [];
    const n = 1 + (this.level > 8 ? 1 : 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) foes.push({ ...g, name: "Town Guard", hp: g.hp, max: g.hp });
    this.openBattle(foes, { guard: true, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: role });
  }

  private fightHunter() {
    this.logLine = "He keeps his word.";
    const h = scaleMonster(monsterById("wolfman"), Math.max(8, this.level));
    h.name = "Vampire Hunter";
    h.family = "vampire";
    h.tint = "#3a3228";
    this.openBattle([{ ...h, id: "hunter", hp: h.hp, max: h.hp }], { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: true, fromRole: "hunter" });
  }

  trainStat(which: "str" | "dex" | "int" | "sta" | "agi" | "fort") {
    if (this.coin < 20) {
      this.logLine = "Twenty silver a lesson.";
      return;
    }
    if (this[which] >= 30) {
      this.logLine = "That stat will not go higher here.";
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= 20;
    this[which] += 1;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp);
    this.logLine = `${which.toUpperCase()} is now ${this[which]}.`;
    this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 3);
  }

  respec() {
    if (this.coin < 25) {
      this.logLine = "Twenty-five silver to undo it.";
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= 25;
    this.specials = [];
    this.ranks = {};
    this.logLine = "The guild unwrites your arts.";
  }

  /** Seat one gem. A gem already in the socket is destroyed, not returned. */
  socketGem(it: Item) {
    const ready = it.special === "Socket" || it.rank >= 4;
    if (!ready || (it.kind !== "weapon" && it.kind !== "armor" && it.kind !== "jewel")) {
      this.logLine = "Rank 2 and 3 need the smith's cut first. Rank 4 and 5 already have a socket.";
      return;
    }
    const gem = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "gem");
    if (!gem) {
      this.logLine = "No gem in the pack.";
      return;
    }
    it.gem = gem.name;
    it.gemBonus = gem.bonus ?? 1;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== gem.uid);
    if (!it.special || it.special === "Socket") it.special = "Socket";
    this.logLine = `${gem.name} sits in ${it.name}. The last stone, if any, is dust.`;
  }

  /** Junk, bait, and fish stack in the drawer. Gear takes its own slot. */
  stash(it: Item) {
    if (it.kind === "potion") {
      this.logLine = "Draughts stay in the pack.";
      return;
    }
    const stack = it.kind === "junk" || it.kind === "bait" || it.kind === "fish";
    const row = stack ? this.vault.find((v) => v.kind === it.kind && v.name === it.name) : undefined;
    if (row) row.stack = (row.stack ?? 1) + (it.stack ?? 1);
    else {
      if (this.vault.length >= 30) {
        this.logLine = "The drawer is full.";
        return;
      }
      this.vault.push({ ...it });
    }
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== it.uid);
    this.logLine = `${it.name} is in the drawer.`;
  }

  takeVault(uid: string) {
    const it = this.vault.find((i) => i.uid === uid);
    if (!it) return;
    this.vault = this.vault.filter((i) => i.uid !== uid);
    if (!this.gainItem(it)) {
      this.vault.push(it);
      return;
    }
    this.logLine = `${it.name} is back in the pack.`;
  }

  buyHome() {
    if (this.ownedHome) {
      this.logLine = "You already hold the croft deed.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.coin < 80) {
      this.logLine = "The croft is eighty silver.";
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= 80;
    this.ownedHome = true;
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = "The south croft is yours. Its door is on the lane under the pond.";
  }

  deposit(amount: number) {
    const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.coin, Math.floor(amount)));
    this.coin -= n;
    this.bank += n;
    this.logLine = `The drawer holds ${this.bank} silver.`;
  }

  withdraw(amount: number) {
    const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.bank, Math.floor(amount)));
    this.bank -= n;
    this.coin += n;
    this.logLine = `You take ${n} silver.`;
  }

  listZeppelin() {
    return DUNGEONS.filter((d) => d.zeppelin && this.visited.includes(d.id));
  }

  zeppelinTo(id: string) {
    const d = dungeonById(id);
    if (!d?.zeppelin || !this.visited.includes(id)) {
      this.logLine = "The tower has not been shown that place.";
      return;
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.enterDungeon(id);
  }

  /** The Portal ash swirl (8 ash, 0.3s, interact channel) at a rift mouth you are near. Visual only. */
  private riftSwirl(dt: number) {
    this.swirlCool -= dt;
    if (this.swirlCool > 0) return;
    this.swirlCool = MAP_WRITER.swirlEvery;
    for (const r of this.riftMouths()) {
      if (Math.hypot(r.x * TILE + 8 - this.px, r.y * TILE + 8 - this.py) <= MAP_WRITER.swirlRange * TILE) this.fx.portal(r.x * TILE + 8, r.y * TILE + 6);
    }
  }

  /** The generated room you stand in, or null. */
  genRoomAt(px = this.px, py = this.py) {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !this.gen) return null;
    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
    return this.gen.rooms.find((r) => tx >= r.x && ty >= r.y && tx < r.x + r.w && ty < r.y + r.h) ?? null;
  }

  /** First step into a generated dungeon's boss room on its last floor: one line, once per floor visit. */
  private noteBossRoom() {
    const def = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? dungeonById(this.dungeon) : undefined;
    if (!def?.gen || this.floor < def.floors || this.mode !== "play") return;
    const key = `${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}:${this.worldMs > 0 ? Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS) : 0}`;
    if (this.bossRoomNoted === key || this.genRoomAt()?.tag !== "boss") return;
    this.bossRoomNoted = key;
    this.logLine = this.roamers.some((r) => r.pack === "guard") ? "The barrow's heart. A Stalker pack keeps it." : "The barrow's heart. Its keepers are down for now.";
  }

  /** Inside a rift zone, the spot a save is written at: the rift's return spot. */
  private riftSaveSpot() {
    const p = this.riftReturn;
    return this.mapId === "dungeon" && p && p.mapId === "world" && dungeonById(this.dungeon)?.rift ? p : null;
  }

  /**
   * playtest1f [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1f portals]: step into a wayrift's mouth. A sealed festival rift
   * says when it wakes (once, until you step off its mouth); an awake one swirls you to its twin's front.
   */
  private startTravel(w: Wayrift) {
    const m = wayMouth(w);
    this.riftHold = `${m.x},${m.y}`;
    const season = this.season();
    const to = wayLink(w, season);
    if (!to) {
      this.logLine = `The ${w.name} is sealed. It wakes for ${wayFestival(w, season).name}, in ${w.season}.`;
      return;
    }
    this.travel = { t: 0, from: w.id, to: to.id, moved: false };
    this.goal = null;
    this.audio?.spell();
    this.fx.portal(m.x * TILE + 8, m.y * TILE - 6);
    this.logLine = w.festival ? `The rift pulls toward ${wayFestival(w, season).name}.` : "The rift pulls you in.";
  }

  /** The swirl: close, move to the far rift's front (facing out of it), open. Ends on its own; never saved. */
  private tickTravel(dt: number) {
    const tr = this.travel;
    if (!tr) return;
    tr.t += dt;
    const to = wayriftById(tr.to);
    if (!to || this.mapId !== "world") {
      this.travel = null;
      return;
    }
    if (!tr.moved && tr.t >= WAYRIFT.out) {
      tr.moved = true;
      const f = wayFront(to);
      this.px = f.x * TILE + 8;
      this.py = f.y * TILE + 8;
      this.facing = "s";
      this.wayHold = to.id;
      this.unstick();
      const c = this.companion;
      if (c) {
        c.x = this.px + 14;
        c.y = this.py + 4;
        if (this.solidAt(c.x, c.y) || this.blockedProp(c.x, c.y)) this.freeBody(c);
      }
      this.critters = [{ x: this.px + 30, y: this.py + 16, kind: "rat", vx: 10 }];
      this.fx.portal(this.px, this.py - 20);
      this.logLine = `You step out by ${WAYRIFT_PLACE[to.id] ?? "the far rift"}.`;
    }
    if (tr.t >= WAYRIFT.out + WAYRIFT.back) this.travel = null;
  }

  /** The rift mouths on the overworld. Read by draw.ts. */
  riftMouths() {
    return this.mapId === "world" ? RIFTS.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, x: r.tx, y: r.ty })) : [];
  }

  /** Out of a rift: back on the exact spot the Portal record holds, held there until you step off the mouth. */
  leaveRift() {
    const p = this.riftReturn;
    const def = dungeonById(this.dungeon);
    this.audio?.door();
    this.riftReturn = null;
    this.dungeon = "";
    this.floor = 0;
    this.gen = null;
    const px = p?.px ?? ((def?.tx ?? 32) * TILE + 8);
    const py = p?.py ?? ((def?.ty ?? 40) * TILE + 8);
    this.enterWorld(px, py);
    this.px = px;
    this.py = py;
    this.riftHold = `${Math.floor(px / TILE)},${Math.floor(py / TILE)}`;
    this.fx.portal(px, py - 4);
    this.seenBiome = biomeName(Math.floor(px / TILE), Math.floor(py / TILE));
    this.logLine = "The ash lets you go. You stand where you stepped in.";
  }

  openPortal() {
    if (this.mode === "battle" || this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "dead") return;
    if (this.mapId === "town") {
      this.logLine = "You are already in town.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.escortHolds()) return;
    if (this.mapId === "dungeon" && dungeonById(this.dungeon)?.rift) {
      this.logLine = "The rift will not hold a gate. Its own way out is the only one.";
      return;
    }
    this.portal = { mapId: this.mapId, px: this.px, py: this.py, dungeon: this.dungeon, floor: this.floor, theme: this.theme, inside: this.inside || undefined };
    this.audio?.spell();
    const home = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? this.bringOut() : "";
    this.enterTown();
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = home ? `A gate stays on the square. ${home}` : "A gate stays on the square. It remembers one place.";
  }

  takePortal() {
    const p = this.portal;
    if (!p) return;
    this.portal = null;
    this.audio?.spell();
    if (p.mapId === "inside" && p.inside) {
      this.enterInside(p.inside, true);
      this.px = p.px;
      this.py = p.py;
    } else if (p.mapId === "dungeon") {
      this.dungeon = p.dungeon;
      this.floor = p.floor;
      this.loadFloor("start");
      this.px = p.px;
      this.py = p.py;
      this.theme = p.theme;
    } else {
      this.enterWorld(p.px, p.py);
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = "The gate folds. It will not wait a second time.";
  }

  // ---- Grave digging (item 8). Numbers in graves.ts DIG. State rides in the opened list: dug:<night>:<x>:<y>. ----

  private hasSpade() {
    return this.inv.some((i) => i.special === "Spade");
  }

  /** True while this town grave lies turned tonight (drawn as fresh earth). */
  graveDug(x: number, y: number) {
    return this.mapId === "town" && this.phase === "night" && this.opened.has(digKey(nightOf(this.worldMs), x, y));
  }

  /** Dig one town grave: once a night, after dark, with a spade. Loot from the world table; sometimes a zombie. */
  private digGrave(x: number, y: number) {
    if (this.phase !== "night") {
      this.logLine = "The yard is watched by day. Turn the earth after dark.";
      return;
    }
    if (!this.hasSpade()) {
      this.logLine = "Bare hands will not open a grave. The undertaker sells a spade.";
      return;
    }
    const night = nightOf(this.worldMs);
    const key = digKey(night, x, y);
    if (this.opened.has(key)) {
      this.logLine = "Already turned tonight. Let it settle.";
      return;
    }
    for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("dug:") && !k.startsWith(`dug:${night}:`)) this.opened.delete(k);
    this.opened.add(key);
    for (let i = 0; i < DIG.drops; i++) this.dropItem(this.px + 10 + i * 12, this.py + 8, this.makeDrop("world", false));
    this.fx.death(x * TILE + 8, y * TILE + 12, "trash", false);
    this.audio?.door();
    if (digWakes(night, x, y)) {
      // The zombie family at the town gate's zone level: the wild rule for the tile the town stands on.
      const s = scaleMonster(monsterById("zombie"), zoneLevel(GATE.x, GATE.y, this.level));
      this.openBattle([{ ...s, id: "zombie", name: s.name, hp: s.hp, max: s.hp }], { guard: false, goblin: false, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      this.logLine = "The dirt heaves. A zombie claws up out of the grave!";
      return;
    }
    this.logLine = "You turn the grave. The coffin gives something up.";
  }

  // ---- The Midnight Derby (item 9). Numbers in derby.ts DERBY. State rides in the opened list:
  //   derby:<day>:in          entered tonight
  //   derby:<day>:best:<in>   your biggest witchlit fish weighed tonight
  //   trophy:derby            the trophy hangs in your croft
  //   derby:wins:<n>          how many derbies you have won

  /** True through the night of a derby day. */
  derbyTonight() {
    return this.phase === "night" && derbyDay(dayOf(this.worldMs));
  }

  private derbyIn() {
    return this.derbyTonight() && this.opened.has(`derby:${dayOf(this.worldMs)}:in`);
  }

  /** Your best weighed witchlit fish tonight, in inches, or 0. */
  derbyBest(day = dayOf(this.worldMs)) {
    const k = [...this.opened].find((x) => x.startsWith(`derby:${day}:best:`));
    return k ? Number(k.split(":")[3]) || 0 : 0;
  }

  hasDerbyTrophy() {
    return this.opened.has("trophy:derby");
  }

  derbyWins() {
    const k = [...this.opened].find((x) => x.startsWith("derby:wins:"));
    return k ? Number(k.split(":")[2]) || 0 : 0;
  }

  private witchlit() {
    return this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "fish" && (i.size ?? 0) > 0).sort((a, b) => (b.size ?? 0) - (a.size ?? 0));
  }

  /** One HUD line for the derby, or "". */
  eventLine() {
    const fest = this.festivalToday();
    if (fest && this.mapId !== "dungeon") {
      if (this.phase === "day") return fest === "harvest" ? "Harvest Moon at nightfall · the plaza" : fest === "bloom" ? "Drowned Bloom at nightfall · the swamp" : fest === "ashen" ? "Ashen Fair at nightfall · the plaza" : "Krampusnacht at nightfall · stay lit";
      const mins = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((CYCLE_MS - (this.worldMs % CYCLE_MS)) / 60000));
      if (fest === "harvest") {
        const run = this.lanternRun;
        if (run) return `Lanterns ${run.lit.length}/${HARVEST.lanterns.length} · ${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(run.left))} s left`;
        return `Harvest Moon · ${mins} min to dawn`;
      }
      if (fest === "bloom") {
        const court = this.courtTonight();
        return `Drowned Bloom · ${mins} min · court ${court.filter((n) => this.packCaught(n.id)).length}/${court.length}`;
      }
      if (fest === "ashen") {
        const run = this.danceRun;
        if (run && run.show > 0) return `Watch the braziers · ${ASHEN_FAIR.steps} flares`;
        if (run) return `Dance ${run.step}/${ASHEN_FAIR.steps} · ${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(run.left))} s left`;
        const show = this.sideshowTonight();
        return `Ashen Fair · ${mins} min · sideshow ${show.filter((n) => this.packCaught(n.id)).length}/${show.length}`;
      }
      const list = this.naughtyTonight();
      return `Krampusnacht · ${mins} min · list ${list.filter((n) => this.naughtyCaught(n.id)).length}/${list.length}`;
    }
    const day = dayOf(this.worldMs);
    if (!derbyDay(day) || this.mapId === "dungeon") return "";
    // Kept to one HUD row (about 38 characters).
    if (this.phase === "day") return "Derby at nightfall · the Drowned Hook";
    const left = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((CYCLE_MS - (this.worldMs % CYCLE_MS)) / 60000));
    if (!this.derbyIn()) return `Derby tonight · ${left} min to dawn`;
    const best = this.derbyBest(day);
    return `Derby · ${left} min · best ${best || "none"} · mark ${derbyMark(day)} in`;
  }

  private enterDerby() {
    if (!this.derbyTonight()) {
      this.logLine = "No derby tonight.";
      return;
    }
    this.opened.add(`derby:${dayOf(this.worldMs)}:in`);
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = `Entered. Witchlit fish only, weighed here before dawn. ${derbyRival(dayOf(this.worldMs))} holds the board at ${derbyMark(dayOf(this.worldMs))} in.`;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
  }

  /** Weigh your biggest witchlit fish. The shack keeps it and marks its points, like a trade. */
  private weighIn() {
    const fish = this.witchlit()[0];
    if (!this.derbyIn() || !fish) {
      this.logLine = this.derbyIn() ? "No witchlit fish to weigh." : "The derby is not running for you.";
      return;
    }
    const day = dayOf(this.worldMs);
    const size = fish.size ?? 0;
    fish.stack = (fish.stack ?? 1) - 1;
    if ((fish.stack ?? 0) <= 0) this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i !== fish);
    this.fishPoints += fish.bonus ?? 1;
    const best = this.derbyBest(day);
    if (size > best) {
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith(`derby:${day}:best:`)) this.opened.delete(k);
      this.opened.add(`derby:${day}:best:${size}`);
    }
    const top = Math.max(size, best);
    const mark = derbyMark(day);
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = `${size} in on the scale. Your best ${top} in; ${derbyRival(day)}'s mark ${mark} in. ${top > mark ? "You lead. Hold it till dawn." : "Not enough yet."}`;
    this.mode = "play";
    this.talk = null;
  }

  /** At dawn after a derby night: judge it once, hang the trophy, and forget the night's keys. */
  private settleDerby() {
    const now = dayOf(this.worldMs);
    const days = new Set<number>();
    for (const k of this.opened) if (k.startsWith("derby:") && !k.startsWith("derby:wins:")) days.add(Number(k.split(":")[1]));
    for (const d of days) {
      // A derby runs through the night of its own day number; it is over once the day count moves on.
      if (!Number.isFinite(d) || d >= now) continue;
      const best = this.derbyBest(d);
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith(`derby:${d}:`)) this.opened.delete(k);
      if (!best) continue;
      const mark = derbyMark(d);
      const rival = derbyRival(d);
      if (best <= mark) {
        this.logLine = `Dawn at the Drowned Hook: ${rival}'s ${mark} in holds the board over your ${best} in.`;
        continue;
      }
      const wins = this.derbyWins();
      for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("derby:wins:")) this.opened.delete(k);
      this.opened.add(`derby:wins:${wins + 1}`);
      this.audio?.coin();
      if (!this.hasDerbyTrophy()) {
        this.opened.add("trophy:derby");
        this.logLine = `Dawn at the Drowned Hook: your ${best} in beats ${rival}'s ${mark} in. The derby trophy ${this.ownedHome ? "hangs on your croft wall" : "waits for a wall: the bank sells the croft deed"}.`;
      } else {
        this.fishPoints += DERBY.repeatPoints;
        this.logLine = `Dawn at the Drowned Hook: your ${best} in wins again. The trophy is already yours; the shack marks ${DERBY.repeatPoints} points.`;
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- Home decorating (item 10). Numbers and spots in decor.ts DECOR. ----

  /** True when a bought piece of furniture stands on this croft tile. */
  decorAt(tx: number, ty: number) {
    return DECOR.pieces.some((p) => p.x === tx && p.y === ty && this.opened.has(decorKey(p.id)));
  }

  /** Furniture standing in the croft, for the draw. */
  decorPieces() {
    return DECOR.pieces.filter((p) => this.opened.has(decorKey(p.id)));
  }

  /** Boss trophies on the croft's back wall: slot x, boss id, and its family (for the sprite). */
  bossTrophies() {
    const out: { slot: number; boss: string; family: string; name: string }[] = [];
    for (const k of this.opened) {
      const t = readTrophy(k);
      if (!t) continue;
      const def = BOSSES.find((b) => b.id === t.boss);
      if (def) out.push({ slot: t.slot, boss: t.boss, family: def.family, name: def.name });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Bosses you have felled at least once (the existing kill record), in the roster's order. */
  slainBosses() {
    return BOSSES.filter((b) => b.boss && this.bossDead[b.id] != null);
  }

  /** What the Felt's points shelf offers for the croft right now. */
  decorOffers() {
    const out: { id: string; label: string; price: number }[] = [];
    for (const p of DECOR.pieces) if (!this.opened.has(decorKey(p.id))) out.push({ id: `piece:${p.id}`, label: `${p.name} · ${p.price} points`, price: p.price });
    const hung = new Set(this.bossTrophies().map((t) => t.boss));
    for (const b of this.slainBosses()) if (!hung.has(b.id)) out.push({ id: `trophy:${b.id}`, label: `${b.name} trophy · ${DECOR.trophyPrice} points`, price: DECOR.trophyPrice });
    return out;
  }

  /** Buy one croft piece or boss trophy with points. It goes straight to the croft, not the pack. */
  buyDecor(id: string) {
    if (!this.ownedHome) {
      this.logLine = "No wall of your own to put it on. The bank sells the croft deed.";
      return;
    }
    const offer = this.decorOffers().find((o) => o.id === id);
    if (!offer) {
      this.logLine = "That is already in the croft.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.points < offer.price) {
      this.logLine = `That wants ${offer.price} points. You hold ${this.points}.`;
      return;
    }
    if (id.startsWith("trophy:")) {
      const used = new Set(this.bossTrophies().map((t) => t.slot));
      const slot = DECOR.trophySlots.find((x) => !used.has(x));
      if (slot == null) {
        this.logLine = "The croft wall is full of heads already.";
        return;
      }
      const boss = id.slice(7);
      this.points -= offer.price;
      this.opened.add(trophyKey(slot, boss));
      this.logLine = `Up on your croft wall: ${BOSSES.find((b) => b.id === boss)?.name ?? "a boss"}. ${this.points} points left.`;
    } else {
      const piece = DECOR.pieces.find((p) => `piece:${p.id}` === id);
      if (!piece) return;
      this.points -= offer.price;
      this.opened.add(decorKey(piece.id));
      this.logLine = `The ${piece.name.toLowerCase()} is carried to your croft. ${this.points} points left.`;
    }
    this.audio?.coin();
  }

  // ---- Companion bond (item 11). Numbers and lines in bond.ts BOND. ----

  /** Bond with this companion (by id), and the half-cycle of the last talk that counted. */
  bondOf(id = this.companion?.id ?? "") {
    for (const k of this.opened) {
      const b = readBond(k);
      if (b && b.id === id) return b;
    }
    return { id, bond: 0, stamp: -1 };
  }

  /** True when the companion at your side has reached the tier for this passive. */
  bondHas(p: BondPassive) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return false;
    return bondTier(this.bondOf(c.id).bond) > BOND.passives.indexOf(p);
  }

  /** One short meter line for the HUD and the camp talk, or "". */
  bondLine() {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return "";
    const b = this.bondOf(c.id).bond;
    const tier = bondTier(b);
    const next = BOND.tiers[tier];
    const on = BOND.passives.slice(0, tier).map((p) => BOND.names[p]);
    return `Bond ${b}/${BOND.max}${on.length ? ` · ${on.join(", ")}` : ""}${next != null ? ` · next at ${next}` : ""}`;
  }

  private bondVoice(): [string, string, string] {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return BOND_LINES.thrall;
    if (this.cls !== "vampire") return BOND_LINES[c.id] ?? BOND_LINES.shade;
    return RELIC_FOR[c.id] ? BOND_LINES.pact : BOND_LINES.thrall;
  }

  /** The camp talk. One talk a day and one a night raise the bond by one; a new tier tells its line. */
  private campTalk() {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return "";
    const now = bondStamp(this.worldMs);
    const was = this.bondOf(c.id);
    if (was.stamp === now || was.bond >= BOND.max) {
      return was.bond >= BOND.max
        ? `"The fire is enough." ${c.name} sits close. The bond is as deep as it goes.`
        : `"The fire is enough. Talk to me again after the next dawn or dusk."`;
    }
    const bond = was.bond + 1;
    for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (readBond(k)?.id === c.id) this.opened.delete(k);
    this.opened.add(bondKey(c.id, bond, now));
    const tier = BOND.tiers.indexOf(bond);
    if (tier < 0) return `You sit by the fire a while and talk about nothing much. (Bond ${bond}/${BOND.max})`;
    const p = BOND.passives[tier];
    this.audio?.spell();
    return `${this.bondVoice()[tier].replace("{name}", c.name)} (Bond ${bond}/${BOND.max}. ${BOND.names[p]}: ${c.name} ${BOND.blurbs[p]}.)`;
  }

  /** Item 11 tier 1 (Watchful): name a floor trap near you, once each. Words only; the trap is unchanged. */
  private callTraps() {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !c || c.hp <= 0 || this.mode !== "play" || !this.bondHas("watchful")) return;
    const traps = this.feats?.traps ?? [];
    const hx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const hy = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    for (const t of traps) {
      if (Math.max(Math.abs(t.x - hx), Math.abs(t.y - hy)) > BOND.callTiles) continue;
      const key = `${this.dungeon}:${this.floor}:${t.x},${t.y}`;
      if (this.trapsCalled.has(key)) continue;
      this.trapsCalled.add(key);
      const dir = Math.abs(t.x - hx) >= Math.abs(t.y - hy) ? (t.x > hx ? "east" : t.x < hx ? "west" : "here") : t.y > hy ? "south" : "north";
      this.logLine = `${c.name}: "${t.kind === "spike" ? "Spikes" : "A plate"}, ${dir === "here" ? "right under you" : `to the ${dir}`}."`;
      this.nums.push({ x: t.x * TILE + 8, y: t.y * TILE + 4, text: "!", life: 1.2 });
      return;
    }
  }

  private nearFish() {
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    for (let y = ty - 1; y <= ty + 1; y++) {
      for (let x = tx - 1; x <= tx + 1; x++) {
        if (this.wet(this.tileUnder(x * TILE + 8, y * TILE + 8))) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  private hasPole() {
    return this.inv.some((i) => i.special === "Pole") || this.equip.main?.special === "Pole" || this.equip.off?.special === "Pole";
  }

  /** Spend one bait and wait. The catch is rolled in reel, not here. */
  castLine() {
    if (this.mode !== "play") return;
    if (!this.nearFish()) {
      this.logLine = "No water in reach.";
      return;
    }
    if (!this.hasPole()) {
      this.logLine = "You need a pole. The Drowned Hook sells a rib pole.";
      return;
    }
    const bait = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "bait" && (i.stack ?? 1) > 0);
    if (!bait) {
      this.logLine = "Bare hook. Nightcrawlers, or something worse, from the fish shack.";
      return;
    }
    bait.stack = (bait.stack ?? 1) - 1;
    this.fishBait = bait.gift || "nightcrawlers";
    if ((bait.stack ?? 0) <= 0) this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i !== bait);
    this.fishZone = 12 + Math.random() * 62;
    this.fishMark = 0;
    this.fishDir = 1;
    const hole = this.tileUnder(this.px, this.py) === T.ice ? "You cut a dark hole. " : "";
    this.fishLine = `${hole}${bait.name} on the hook. Reel when the mark sits in the dark band.`;
    this.mode = "fish";
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  private tickFish(dt: number) {
    this.fishMark += this.fishDir * 46 * dt;
    if (this.fishMark >= 100) {
      this.fishMark = 100;
      this.fishDir = -1;
    } else if (this.fishMark <= 0) {
      this.fishMark = 0;
      this.fishDir = 1;
    }
  }

  reel() {
    if (this.mode !== "fish") return;
    const hit = this.fishMark >= this.fishZone && this.fishMark <= this.fishZone + 22;
    this.mode = "play";
    if (!hit) {
      this.logLine = "The line goes slack. The bait is gone.";
      this.audio?.hurt();
      return;
    }
    this.catchFish();
  }

  private ownsWaterwalk() {
    return this.hasWaterWalk() || this.inv.some((i) => i.special === "Waterwalk");
  }

  /** Mostly a named fish. A thin chance of a gem, a piece of gear, or nothing but weeds. */
  private catchFish() {
    const bait = this.fishBait;
    const gemP = 0.03 + (bait === "grub" ? 0.04 : 0) + (bait === "newt" ? 0.02 : 0);
    const gearP = 0.02 + (bait === "leech" ? 0.02 : 0) + (bait === "newt" ? 0.03 : 0);
    const bootP = 0.008 + (bait === "newt" ? 0.01 : 0);
    let got: Item;
    if (Math.random() < bootP && !this.ownsWaterwalk()) {
      got = makeItem({ name: "Bog-step boots", kind: "armor", rank: 4, slot: "feet", ac: 3, special: "Waterwalk" });
    } else if (Math.random() < gemP) {
      got = this.makeGem(Math.random, "rare", this.level);
    } else if (Math.random() < gearP) {
      const rank = Math.random() < 0.15 ? 4 : 3;
      const armor = CLASSES[this.cls].armor!;
      got =
        Math.random() < 0.5
          ? makeItem({ name: "Drowned edge", kind: "weapon", rank: asRank(rank), slot: "main", atk: 4 + rank * 2 + Math.floor(this.level / 5) })
          : makeItem({ name: "Drowned coat", kind: "armor", rank: asRank(rank), slot: "chest", armor, ac: rank + 1 });
    } else {
      const bias = bait === "newt" ? 2 : bait === "moth" || bait === "leech" ? 1 : 0;
      const span = Math.max(1, CATCHES.length - bias);
      const idx = Math.min(CATCHES.length - 1, bias + Math.floor(Math.random() * span));
      const fish = CATCHES[idx];
      if (this.derbyIn() && Math.random() < DERBY.magicChance) {
        // Item 9: a witchlit fish, sized. The size is in the name so two sizes never share a stack.
        const size = derbySize(idx, Math.random());
        got = makeItem({ name: `Witchlit ${fish.name.toLowerCase()} (${size} in)`, kind: "fish", rank: 1, bonus: fish.points, stack: 1, size });
      } else if (Math.random() < SEASON.fishChance) {
        // Item 13: the season's own catch, traded at the shack like any fish.
        const sf = SEASON.fish[this.season()];
        got = makeItem({ name: sf.name, kind: "fish", rank: 1, bonus: sf.points, stack: 1 });
      } else got = makeItem({ name: fish.name, kind: "fish", rank: 1, bonus: fish.points, stack: 1 });
    }
    if (!this.gainItem(got)) {
      this.logLine = "It slips back. The pack is full.";
      return;
    }
    if (got.kind === "fish") {
      if (this.fishStep < 1) this.fishStep = 1;
      this.logLine = got.size ? `${got.name}! It glows on the line. Weigh it at the Drowned Hook before dawn.` : `${got.name}. Worth ${got.bonus} points at the shack.`;
      this.audio?.coin();
    } else {
      this.logLine = `The water gives up ${got.name}.`;
      this.audio?.spell();
    }
  }

  /** Turn fish points in at the Drowned Hook. The rare boots are bought with those points. */
  tradeFish() {
    // On a derby night you are entered in, witchlit fish stay in the pack for the scale (item 9).
    const keep = (i: Item) => this.derbyIn() && (i.size ?? 0) > 0;
    const fish = this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "fish" && !keep(i));
    if (!fish.length) {
      this.logLine = "No catch to trade.";
      return;
    }
    let pts = 0;
    for (const f of fish) pts += (f.bonus ?? 1) * (f.stack ?? 1);
    this.fishPoints += pts;
    if (this.fishStep < 2) this.fishStep = 2;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind !== "fish" || keep(i));
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = `The shack marks ${pts} points. You hold ${this.fishPoints}.`;
  }

  buyFishReward(id: string) {
    const cost = id === "boots" ? 40 : id === "charm" ? 24 : 18;
    if (this.fishPoints < cost) {
      this.logLine = `That costs ${cost} fish points.`;
      return;
    }
    if (id === "boots" && this.ownsWaterwalk()) {
      this.logLine = "You already have boots that walk the water.";
      return;
    }
    const it =
      id === "boots"
        ? makeItem({ name: "Bog-step boots", kind: "armor", rank: 3, slot: "feet", ac: 2, special: "Waterwalk" })
        : id === "charm"
          ? makeItem({ name: "Gill charm", kind: "jewel", rank: 3, slot: "neck", ac: 1, aura: "Ward", bonus: 1 })
          : makeItem({ name: "Hooked ring", kind: "jewel", rank: 2, slot: "ring", bonus: 2 });
    if (!this.gainItem(it)) return;
    this.fishPoints -= cost;
    if (id === "boots") this.fishStep = 3;
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = `${it.name} is yours. ${this.fishPoints} points left.`;
  }

  buildStock(role: string): Item[] {
    const day = Math.floor(this.worldMs / CYCLE_MS);
    const rng = mulberry(day * 9176 + role.length * 131 + 9);
    const lv = Math.max(5, Math.min(80, this.level));
    const potion = (name: string, bonus: number) => makeItem({ name, kind: "potion", rank: 1, bonus });
    if (role.startsWith("stall:")) return this.stallStock(role.slice(6), lv, rng, potion);
    if (role === "fisher") {
      const list = [makeItem({ name: "Rib pole", kind: "tackle", rank: 1, bonus: 15, special: "Pole", stack: 1 })];
      for (const b of BAITS) list.push(makeItem({ name: b.name, kind: "bait", rank: 1, bonus: b.price, stack: b.stack, gift: b.id }));
      return list;
    }
    if (role === "shop" || role === "merchant" || role === "alchemist" || role === "mystic") {
      const list = [potion("Health draught", 1), potion("Spirit draught", 2), ...this.rescueStock(role, lv)];
      if (role === "shop" || role === "merchant") {
        list.push(makeItem({ name: "Road sword", kind: "weapon", rank: 1, slot: "main", atk: 4 + Math.floor(lv / 2) }));
        for (const g of GIFTS) list.push(makeItem({ name: g.name, kind: "gift", rank: 1, gift: g.gift, bonus: g.price }));
      } else if (role === "mystic") {
        list.push(
          makeItem({ name: "Moon Lantern", kind: "weapon", rank: 2, slot: "off", hands: 1, atk: 2, active: "Lantern Flare", bonus: 1 }),
          makeItem({ name: "Star Orb", kind: "weapon", rank: 3, slot: "off", hands: 1, atk: 3, active: "Star Bolt" }),
          makeItem({ name: "Grave Skull", kind: "weapon", rank: 2, slot: "off", hands: 1, atk: 2, active: "Skull Hex" }),
          makeItem({ name: "Spell Tome", kind: "weapon", rank: 3, slot: "off", hands: 1, atk: 1, bonus: 2, active: "Page Bolt" }),
          makeItem({ name: "Caster's ring", kind: "jewel", rank: 2, slot: "ring", bonus: 2 }),
          makeItem({ name: "Moon locket", kind: "jewel", rank: 3, slot: "neck", bonus: 1, aura: "Ward", ac: 1 }),
        );
      } else if (rng() > 0.4) {
        list.push(this.makeGem(rng, "rare", lv));
      }
      return list;
    }
    // Item 8: the undertaker sells the grave spade, priced like the fish shack's pole (tackle price = bonus).
    const spade = makeItem({ name: "Grave spade", kind: "tackle", rank: 1, bonus: DIG.spadePrice, special: "Spade", stack: 1 });
    if (role === "undertaker" && this.cls !== "vampire") {
      return [potion("Cheap draught", 1), makeItem({ name: "Coffin nails", kind: "junk", rank: 1, stack: 3 }), spade];
    }
    const list: Item[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const roll = rng();
      const rank = roll > 0.92 ? 5 : roll > 0.75 ? 4 : roll > 0.4 ? 3 : 2;
      const armor = this.cls === "vampire" || role === "undertaker" ? "chain" : CLASSES[this.cls].armor;
      if (i === 5 && rng() < 0.45) {
        list.push(this.makeGem(rng, "rare", lv));
        continue;
      }
      if (i % 2 === 0) {
        list.push(
          makeItem({
            name: rank === 5 ? "Named nightblade" : "Cabinet edge",
            kind: "weapon",
            rank: asRank(rank),
            slot: "main",
            atk: 4 + rank * 2 + Math.floor(lv / 4),
            special: rank === 5 ? "Grave spark" : undefined,
          }),
        );
      } else {
        list.push(
          makeItem({
            name: rank >= 4 ? "Cabinet coat" : "Mended armor",
            kind: "armor",
            rank: asRank(rank),
            slot: "chest",
            armor,
            ac: rank + 1,
          }),
        );
      }
    }
    // After the six rolls, so the cabinet's seeded stock is the same as before a rescue.
    return [...list, ...this.rescueStock(role, lv), ...(role === "undertaker" ? [spade] : [])];
  }

  priceOf(it: Item) {
    if (it.kind === "gift") return it.bonus || 12;
    if (it.kind === "bait" || it.kind === "tackle") return it.bonus || 4;
    if (it.kind === "fish") return 0;
    if (it.kind === "potion") return 6;
    if (it.active) return 18 + it.rank * 8;
    return 6 + it.rank * (this.shopRole === "shop" || this.shopRole === "merchant" ? 8 : 14);
  }

  buy(it: Item) {
    const price = this.priceOf(it);
    if (it.kind === "fish") {
      this.logLine = "The shack trades catch for points.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.kind === "tackle" && it.special === "Pole" && this.hasPole()) {
      this.logLine = "You already carry a pole.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.kind === "tackle" && it.special === "Spade" && this.hasSpade()) {
      this.logLine = "You already carry a spade.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.coin < price) {
      this.logLine = "Too dear.";
      return;
    }
    const taken =
      it.kind === "gift" || it.kind === "bait" || it.kind === "tackle"
        ? makeItem({
            name: it.name,
            kind: it.kind,
            rank: 1,
            gift: it.gift,
            bonus: it.bonus,
            stack: it.stack,
            special: it.special,
          })
        : it;
    if (!this.gainItem(taken)) return;
    this.coin -= price;
    if (it.kind !== "potion" && it.kind !== "gift" && it.kind !== "bait" && it.kind !== "tackle") this.stock = this.stock.filter((s) => s.uid !== it.uid);
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = `Bought ${it.name}.`;
  }

  sell(it: Item) {
    if (it.kind === "relic") {
      this.logLine = "That relic is not for sale.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.kind === "fish") {
      this.logLine = "The fish shack trades catch for points, not silver.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.kind === "tackle") {
      this.logLine = it.special === "Spade" ? "The spade stays with you." : "The pole stays with you.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.kind === "potion") return;
    const pay = it.kind === "junk" ? 2 * (it.stack ?? 1) : it.kind === "bait" ? it.stack ?? 1 : it.kind === "gift" ? 4 : 3 * it.rank;
    this.coin += pay;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== it.uid);
    this.audio?.coin();
    if (it.kind === "junk") this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 2);
  }

  sellJunk() {
    const junk = this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "junk");
    if (!junk.length) {
      this.logLine = "No junk to sell.";
      return;
    }
    for (const j of junk) this.sell(j);
    this.logLine = "The counter takes the scraps.";
  }

  equipItem(it: Item) {
    if (!it.slot) {
      this.logLine = "That does not equip.";
      return;
    }
    if (it.armor && it.armor !== CLASSES[this.cls].armor) {
      this.logLine = "Wrong armor for your class.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.dungeon === "grave" && it.kind === "armor" && it.armor !== "chain") {
      this.logLine = "The grave only keeps chain.";
      return;
    }
    const prev = this.equip[it.slot];
    if (it.hands === 2 && this.equip.off) {
      if (this.inv.length >= 20) {
        this.logLine = "The pack is full.";
        return;
      }
      this.inv.push(this.equip.off);
      delete this.equip.off;
    }
    this.equip[it.slot] = it;
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== it.uid);
    if (prev) this.inv.push(prev);
    this.logLine = `Equipped ${it.name}.`;
  }

  levelOptions(): { name: string; sharpen: boolean }[] {
    const all = CLASSES[this.cls].specials;
    const canNew = this.specials.length < 4 && this.level >= 5 + this.specials.length * 5;
    if (canNew) return all.filter((s) => !this.specials.includes(s)).map((name) => ({ name, sharpen: false }));
    if (this.specials.length && this.level >= 5) return this.specials.map((name) => ({ name, sharpen: true }));
    return [];
  }

  learn(name: string) {
    const known = this.specials.includes(name);
    const need = 5 + this.specials.length * 5;
    if (known) {
      this.ranks[name] = (this.ranks[name] ?? 0) + 1;
      this.logLine = `${name} sharpened.`;
    } else if (this.specials.length < 4 && this.level >= need) {
      this.specials.push(name);
      this.logLine = `Learned ${name}.`;
      this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 3);
    } else {
      this.logLine = `Not yet. The next art opens at level ${need}.`;
      return;
    }
    if (this.mode === "level") this.dismissLevel();
    else if (this.mode !== "shop") this.mode = "play";
  }

  dismissLevel() {
    this.mode = "play";
    this.checkLevel();
  }

  private checkLevel() {
    while (this.xp >= this.xpNeed() && this.level < 100) {
      this.xp -= this.xpNeed();
      this.level += 1;
      this.hp = this.maxHp;
      this.energy = this.maxEnergy;
      if (this.companion) this.companion.hp = this.companionMax();
      if (this.levelOptions().length) {
        this.mode = "level";
        this.logLine = `Level ${this.level}. Choose an art.`;
        return;
      }
      this.logLine = this.level === 10 ? "Level 10. The chapel, the cauldron, or the moon shop will walk with you. A cottage still wants the gift they asked for." : `Level ${this.level}.`;
    }
    if (this.mode !== "level" && this.mode !== "dead") this.mode = "play";
  }

  engage(kind: "cleave" | "ranged") {
    if (kind === "cleave") this.whirl();
    else this.smite();
  }

  /** A learned class art. Mana, or blood for a vampire. The picture matches the tree. */
  castKnown(name: string) {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0 || !name) return;
    const look = HERO_SPELLS[name];
    if (!look || !CLASSES[this.cls].specials.includes(name)) return;
    if (!this.specials.includes(name)) {
      this.logLine = "You have not learned that.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.spellCool > 0) return;
    this.castCombos = 0;
    this.castNames = [];
    const self = name === "Loadout Stance" || name === "War Cry" || name === "Vanish";
    const aoe = name === "Earthshatter" || name === "Grave Nova" || name === "Summon Shade";
    const reach = name === "Ambush" ? 30 : 100;
    const foe = this.roamers.find((r) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) < reach);
    const prop = !self && !aoe && !foe ? this.propInReach(reach) : null; // playtest1n: no foe, but a prop to aim at
    if (!self && !aoe && !foe && !prop) {
      this.logLine = "Nothing in reach.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.cls === "vampire") {
      if (this.blood < 4) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough blood.";
        return;
      }
      this.blood -= 4;
    } else if (this.energy < 6) {
      this.logLine = "Not enough mana.";
      return;
    } else this.energy -= 6;
    this.spellCool = 1.3;
    this.actPose = "cast";
    this.actFor = 0.34;
    const power = this.blow() + (this.ranks[name] ?? 0) * 2;
    const paint = (tx: number, ty: number) => this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 12, tx, ty, look.shape, look.color);
    if (name === "Loadout Stance" || name === "War Cry") {
      this.cryUntil = this.worldMs + (name === "War Cry" ? 6000 : 8000);
      this.cryMul = name === "War Cry" ? 1.4 : 1.25;
      paint(this.px, this.py);
      this.logLine = name === "War Cry" ? "The cry hangs on your next blows." : "You set your feet.";
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (name === "Vanish") {
      this.iframe = 1.2;
      this.darkUntil = this.worldMs + 900;
      paint(this.px, this.py);
      this.logLine = "You slip out of sight.";
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    // playtest1k: the spell's element (its 1h fx strip) reacts with the foe's statuses once the hit lands.
    const el = SPELL_ELEMENT[name] ?? null;
    const hit = (r: Roamer, dealt: number) => {
      const d = Math.max(1, dealt - Math.floor((r.ac ?? 0) / 2));
      this.hurtFoe(r, d, "smite");
      this.applyElement(r, el, d, false);
    };
    if (aoe) {
      const rad = name === "Earthshatter" ? 40 : name === "Summon Shade" ? 56 : 72;
      const mul = name === "Summon Shade" ? 0.7 : 1;
      let n = 0;
      for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
        if (!this.liveFoe(r) || Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) > rad) continue;
        hit(r, Math.round(power * mul));
        n += 1;
      }
      n += this.roomArea(this.px, this.py, rad, el); // playtest1n: props and oil in the area
      paint(this.px, this.py);
      this.logLine = n ? `${name}.${this.castNames.map((c) => ` ${c}!`).join("")}` : `${name} finds nothing.`; // playtest1k: and the combos it set off
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (!foe) {
      if (!prop) return;
      // playtest1n: the spell flies at the prop; fire lights it, anything else strikes it.
      const c = centre(prop, TILE);
      paint(c.x, c.y - 8);
      this.hitProp(prop, c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py, el);
      this.logLine = `${name}. ${this.logLine}`;
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    let dealt = power;
    if (name === "Deathbolt") dealt = Math.round(power * 1.45);
    if (name === "Ambush") dealt = Math.round(power * 1.7);
    if (name === "Execution" && (foe.hp ?? 0) < (foe.max ?? 1) * 0.4) dealt = power * 2;
    if (name === "Tripwire") foe.cool = (foe.cool ?? 0) + 2;
    hit(foe, dealt);
    if (el === "fire") this.roomIgnite(foe.x, foe.y, ROOM.igniteR, foe.x - this.px, foe.y - this.py); // playtest1n: fire at the impact catches
    paint(foe.x, foe.y - 10);
    this.logLine = `${name}. ${this.logLine}`;
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  /** The companion's own tree. The picture follows the art, not the hero's. */
  private castAlly(c: Companion, art: Art, foe?: Roamer) {
    const self = art.kind === "heal" || art.kind === "cleanse" || art.kind === "buff" || art.kind === "ward";
    c.act = self ? "cast" : "swing";
    c.actFor = 0.3;
    const color = art.kind === "heal" || art.kind === "cleanse" ? "#d0e4ff" : art.kind === "curse" ? "#6a8a48" : art.kind === "drain" ? "#6a3a8a" : "#f4e27a";
    const shape = art.kind === "heal" || art.kind === "cleanse" ? "mend" : art.aoe ? "nova" : art.kind === "ward" || art.kind === "buff" ? "ring" : "bolt";
    if (art.kind === "heal" || art.kind === "cleanse") {
      const n = art.power + Math.floor(this.level / 3) + this.companionBonus();
      this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + n);
      c.hp = Math.min(this.companionMax(), c.hp + Math.ceil(n / 3));
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y - 10, this.px, this.py - 10, "mend", color);
      this.logLine = `${c.name} casts ${art.name} for ${n}.`;
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (art.kind === "buff") {
      this.cryUntil = Math.max(this.cryUntil, this.worldMs + 6000);
      this.cryMul = Math.max(this.cryMul, 1.2);
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y, this.px, this.py, "ring", color);
      this.logLine = `${c.name} casts ${art.name}.`;
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (art.kind === "ward") {
      this.wardUntil = this.worldMs + 6000;
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y, this.px, this.py, "ring", color);
      this.logLine = `${c.name} casts ${art.name}.`;
      this.audio?.spell();
      return;
    }
    if (!foe) return;
    const dmg = art.power + Math.floor(this.level / 4) + Math.floor(this.companionAtk() / 2);
    const victims = art.aoe ? this.roamers.filter((r) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - c.x, r.y - c.y) < 40) : [foe];
    this.castCombos = 0;
    this.castNames = [];
    for (const r of [...victims]) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r)) continue;
      const d = Math.max(1, dmg - Math.floor((r.ac ?? 0) / 2));
      this.hurtFoe(r, d, "slash", true);
      this.applyElement(r, artElement(art.kind), d, true); // playtest1k: venom poisons, a gold taunt art is lightning
      if (art.kind === "drain") {
        const heal = Math.ceil(dmg * 0.4);
        this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + heal);
        c.hp = Math.min(this.companionMax(), c.hp + Math.floor(heal / 2));
      }
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y - 8, r.x, r.y - 8, shape, color);
    }
    this.logLine = `${c.name} casts ${art.name}.`;
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  /** Layout Two. Tap is Slash when a foe is in the cone. Otherwise it is the context verb. */
  slash() {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0) return;
    this.actPose = "swing";
    this.actFor = 0.22;
    const hit = this.strikeCone((20 * Math.PI) / 180, 36, this.blow());
    if (!hit) this.interact();
  }

  /** SEC. 18 stamina. Hits everyone at your feet. */
  whirl() {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0) return;
    if (!this.spend("whirl")) return;
    this.actPose = "swing";
    this.actFor = 0.22;
    this.fx.whirl(this.px, this.py);
    const n = this.strikeCone(Math.PI, 28, this.blow(), "whirl");
    this.logLine = n ? "Whirl." : "Whirl cuts only air.";
    this.whirlCrack();
  }

  /** A socketed spell on the rail. Costs 2 mana, or 2 blood. */
  castRail(name: string) {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0 || !name) return;
    const owned = Object.values(this.equip).some((it) => it?.active === name);
    if (!owned) return;
    if (this.cls === "vampire") {
      if (this.blood < 2) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough blood.";
        return;
      }
      this.blood -= 2;
    } else {
      if (this.energy < 2) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough mana.";
        return;
      }
      this.energy -= 2;
    }
    const foe = this.roamers.find((r) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) < 96);
    this.actPose = "cast";
    this.actFor = 0.28;
    if (!foe) {
      this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 14, this.px, this.py - 14, "ring"); // playtest1: a fizzle you can see
      this.logLine = `${name} finds nothing.`;
      return;
    }
    this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 12, foe.x, foe.y - 10, "bolt");
    this.hurtFoe(foe, 4 + Math.floor(this.level / 3), "smite");
    this.logLine = `${name}. ${this.logLine}`;
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  /** 3RD. 12 mana, or 12 blood. Breaks a telegraphed big move, then locks for 12s. */
  smite() {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0) return;
    if (!this.spend("smite")) return;
    this.actPose = "cast";
    this.actFor = 0.28;
    let n = 0;
    for (const r of this.roamers) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r)) continue;
      if (Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) > 96) continue;
      // playtest1: a wizard's Smite is the Deathbolt's shadow purple, so it reads as his; damage is unchanged.
      this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 12, r.x, r.y - 10, "bolt", this.cls === "wizard" ? "#6a3a8a" : "#f4e27a");
      if ((r.tell ?? 0) > 0 && r.casting === "big") {
        if (this.worldMs < this.smiteLock) this.logLine = "Smite is spent.";
        else {
          r.tell = 0;
          r.casting = "";
          this.smiteLock = this.worldMs + 12000;
          this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} loses the cast.`;
        }
      }
      this.fx.smite(r.x, r.y - 10);
      this.hurtFoe(r, this.blow() + 2, "smite");
      n += 1;
      break;
    }
    if (!n && this.smiteSaint()) n = 1;
    const prop = n ? null : this.propInReach(96); // playtest1n: no foe, but a prop to strike
    if (prop) {
      const c = centre(prop, TILE);
      this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 12, c.x, c.y - 8, "bolt", this.cls === "wizard" ? "#6a3a8a" : "#f4e27a");
      this.hitProp(prop, c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py, null);
      n = 1;
    }
    if (!n) {
      this.bolt(this.px, this.py - 14, this.px, this.py - 14, "ring"); // playtest1: a fizzle you can see
      this.logLine = "Smite finds nothing.";
    }
    this.audio?.spell();
  }

  private spend(kind: "whirl" | "smite") {
    if (this.cls === "vampire") {
      const cost = kind === "whirl" ? 18 : 12;
      if (this.blood < cost) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough blood.";
        return false;
      }
      this.blood -= cost;
      return true;
    }
    if (kind === "whirl") {
      if (this.stam < 18) {
        this.logLine = "Not enough stamina.";
        return false;
      }
      this.stam -= 18;
      return true;
    }
    if (this.energy < 12) {
      this.logLine = "Not enough mana.";
      return false;
    }
    this.energy -= 12;
    return true;
  }

  private blow() {
    const crit = Math.random() < 0.01 + this.agi * 0.002;
    const dealt = Math.max(1, this.atk);
    const swung = this.cryUntil > this.worldMs ? Math.round(dealt * this.cryMul) : dealt;
    return crit ? swung * 2 : swung;
  }

  private strikeCone(half: number, reach: number, dmg: number, verb: "slash" | "whirl" | "smite" = "slash") {
    const aim = this.facing === "e" ? 0 : this.facing === "s" ? Math.PI / 2 : this.facing === "w" ? Math.PI : -Math.PI / 2;
    let n = 0;
    for (const r of this.roamers) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r)) continue;
      const dx = r.x - this.px;
      const dy = r.y - this.py;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (dist > reach || dist < 0.01) continue;
      let diff = Math.abs(Math.atan2(dy, dx) - aim);
      if (diff > Math.PI) diff = Math.PI * 2 - diff;
      if (diff > half) continue;
      const crit = dmg > this.atk;
      if (verb === "slash") this.fx.slash(r.x, r.y - 10, aim, boneKin(r), crit);
      this.hurtFoe(r, Math.max(1, dmg - (r.ac ?? 0)), verb);
      if (verb !== "smite") this.shove(r, dx / dist, dy / dist); // playtest1n: the blow carries the foe
      if (crit) this.logLine = `Critical. ${this.logLine}`;
      n += 1;
    }
    n += this.strikeProps(aim, half, reach, n > 0); // playtest1n: a lantern, barrel or pillar in the swing
    if (n) this.audio?.hit();
    return n;
  }

  private liveFoe(r: Roamer) {
    return r.aggro && (r.hp ?? 0) > 0;
  }

  // ---- playtest1k [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03: elemental combos, companion commands] ----

  /** Statuses a foe picks up where it stands: wet in water, a pool, the swamp or open-air rain; chilled on snow, on ice,
   * in open-air snow, or rimed by nature (the lich). Both linger STATUS.linger s. Poison runs down. No dice: nothing here
   * draws a random number, so a fight without spells plays exactly as before. */
  private tickStatus(r: Roamer, dt: number, weather: string) {
    const st = r.st ?? (r.st = {});
    st.poison = Math.max(0, (st.poison ?? 0) - dt);
    st.comboCool = Math.max(0, (st.comboCool ?? 0) - dt);
    const t = this.tileUnder(r.x, r.y);
    const open = this.mapId === "world" || this.mapId === "camp";
    const wet = t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.swamp || (open && WET_WEATHER.has(weather));
    const cold = t === T.snow || t === T.ice || (open && COLD_WEATHER.has(weather)) || RIMED_FAMILIES.has(r.family);
    st.wet = wet ? STATUS.linger : Math.max(0, (st.wet ?? 0) - dt);
    st.chill = cold ? STATUS.linger : Math.max(0, (st.chill ?? 0) - dt);
  }

  /** Is this foe oiled (wax, pitch, straw or resin)? */
  oiled(r: Roamer) {
    return OILED_FAMILIES.has(r.family) || (r.oilT ?? 0) > 0; // playtest1n: or soaked in a barrel's oil
  }

  /** An element hit has landed on r for dealt: set off its reaction (at most two per cast), then lay the element's
   * own status (venom poisons). Bonus, splash and arcs go through hurtFoe like any hit (shells, family slivers). */
  private applyElement(r: Roamer, el: Element | null, dealt: number, ally: boolean) {
    if (!el) return;
    const st = r.st ?? (r.st = {});
    let id: ComboId | null = this.castCombos < 2 ? reaction(el, st, this.oiled(r)) : null;
    const x = r.x;
    const y = r.y;
    const others = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r && this.liveFoe(o));
    const wetFirst = (o: Roamer) => ((o.st?.wet ?? 0) > 0 ? 0 : 1);
    const arcs = id === "chain" ? others.filter((o) => Math.hypot(o.x - x, o.y - y) <= COMBO.chainR).sort((a, b) => wetFirst(a) - wetFirst(b) || Math.hypot(a.x - x, a.y - y) - Math.hypot(b.x - x, b.y - y)).slice(0, COMBO.chainMax) : [];
    if (id === "chain" && !arcs.length) id = null;
    if (el === "venom") st.poison = STATUS.poison;
    if (!id) return;
    this.castCombos += 1;
    if (!this.castNames.includes(COMBO_NAME[id])) this.castNames.push(COMBO_NAME[id]);
    st.comboCool = STATUS.comboCool;
    if (id === "blast") st.poison = 0;
    if (id === "shatter") st.chill = 0;
    const bonus = comboBonus(id, dealt);
    if (bonus && this.liveFoe(r)) this.hurtFoe(r, bonus, "smite", ally);
    const color = COMBO_COLOR[id];
    if (id === "blast" || id === "wildfire") {
      this.bolt(x, y - 8, x, y - 8, "nova", color);
      for (const o of others) if (this.liveFoe(o) && Math.hypot(o.x - x, o.y - y) <= COMBO.blastR) this.hurtFoe(o, comboSpread(id, dealt), "smite", ally);
    } else if (id === "shatter") {
      this.bolt(x, y - 8, x, y - 8, "ring", color);
      for (const [sx, sy] of [[-6, -12], [6, -12], [-5, -2], [5, -2]]) this.spark(x + sx, y + sy, color);
    } else {
      let fx = x;
      let fy = y;
      for (const o of arcs) {
        this.bolt(fx, fy - 10, o.x, o.y - 10, "bolt", color);
        fx = o.x;
        fy = o.y;
        if (this.liveFoe(o)) this.hurtFoe(o, comboSpread(id, dealt), "smite", ally);
      }
    }
    this.pushPop(x, y - 28, COMBO_NAME[id], COMBO_ROW[id]);
    this.logLine = `${COMBO_NAME[id]}! ${this.logLine}`;
    this.audio?.hit();
  }

  private pushPop(x: number, y: number, text: string, row: "red" | "blue" | "gold" | "violet") {
    this.pops.push({ x, y, text, row, life: COMBO.popLife });
    if (this.pops.length > COMBO.popMax) this.pops.shift();
  }

  private allyUp() {
    return !!this.companion && this.companion.hp > 0;
  }

  /** The companion, while r is taunted by it. */
  private tauntOf(r: Roamer) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c || c.hp <= 0 || (r.taunt ?? 0) <= this.worldMs) return null;
    return c;
  }

  /** Where a foe aims: the hero, or a taunting companion. */
  private aimOf(r: Roamer): { x: number; y: number; ally: boolean } {
    const c = this.tauntOf(r);
    return c ? { x: c.x, y: c.y, ally: true } : { x: this.px, y: this.py, ally: false };
  }

  /** A hit on the taunting companion. Field companions do not fall: under ORDER.tauntFloor of its HP it falls back and
   * every taunt ends. */
  private hurtAlly(dealt: number, label: string) {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c) return;
    c.hp = Math.max(1, c.hp - dealt);
    this.nums.push({ x: c.x, y: c.y - 18, text: String(dealt), life: 0.55 });
    this.spark(c.x, c.y - 12, "#f4e27a");
    this.logLine = label;
    if (c.hp < this.companionMax() * ORDER.tauntFloor) {
      for (const r of this.roamers) r.taunt = 0;
      this.logLine = `${label} ${c.name} falls back.`;
    }
  }

  /** The focused foe while the focus holds. */
  private focusFoe() {
    const f = this.allyFocus;
    if (!f || this.focusUntil <= this.worldMs || !this.liveFoe(f) || !this.roamers.includes(f)) {
      this.allyFocus = null;
      return null;
    }
    return f;
  }

  resetOrders() {
    this.allyStay = false;
    this.allyStayAt = "";
    this.allyFocus = null;
    this.focusUntil = 0;
    this.guardUntil = 0;
    this.orderCool = { taunt: 0, guard: 0, focus: 0, stay: 0 };
    this.pops = [];
    this.lastHit = null;
  }

  /** Does the companion know a heal (then "Heal or guard me" heals; without one it guards)? */
  allyHeals() {
    const c = this.companion;
    return !!c && KITS[c.kit].tree.some((a) => (a.kind === "heal" || a.kind === "cleanse") && this.level >= a.need);
  }

  /** The seconds left on an order's cooldown (the touch strip dims while it waits). */
  orderWait(o: Order) {
    return Math.max(0, (this.orderCool[o] - this.worldMs) / 1000);
  }

  /** One-tap orders to the companion: the touch strip, keys Z X R V, pad RT LT R3 L3 (all rebindable). */
  order(o: Order) {
    if (this.mode !== "play" || this.fallen > 0) return;
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c || c.hp <= 0) {
      this.logLine = "No one walks with you.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.orderCool[o] > this.worldMs) {
      this.logLine = `${ORDER_LABEL[o].long}: not yet.`;
      return;
    }
    let pop = ORDER_LABEL[o].short.toUpperCase();
    if (o === "focus") {
      const reach = (r: Roamer) => this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - this.px, r.y - this.py) <= ORDER.focusR;
      const last = this.lastHit && this.roamers.includes(this.lastHit) && reach(this.lastHit) ? this.lastHit : null;
      const t = last ?? this.roamers.filter(reach).sort((a, b) => Math.hypot(a.x - this.px, a.y - this.py) - Math.hypot(b.x - this.px, b.y - this.py))[0];
      if (!t) {
        this.logLine = "No target to focus.";
        return;
      }
      this.allyFocus = t;
      this.focusUntil = this.worldMs + ORDER.focus * 1000;
      this.logLine = `${c.name} goes for ${t.name ?? "it"}.`;
    } else if (o === "taunt") {
      const known = KITS[c.kit].tree.some((a) => a.kind === "aggro" && this.level >= a.need);
      const secs = known ? ORDER.tauntArt : ORDER.taunt;
      let n = 0;
      for (const r of this.roamers) {
        if (!this.liveFoe(r) || Math.hypot(r.x - c.x, r.y - c.y) > ORDER.tauntR) continue;
        r.taunt = this.worldMs + (r.boss ? secs / 2 : secs) * 1000;
        n += 1;
      }
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y - 8, c.x, c.y - 8, "ring", "#f4e27a");
      this.logLine = n ? `${c.name} taunts ${n === 1 ? "the foe" : `${n} foes`}.` : `${c.name} taunts the empty air.`;
    } else if (o === "guard") {
      const known = KITS[c.kit].tree.filter((a) => this.level >= a.need);
      const mend = [...known].reverse().find((a) => a.kind === "heal" || a.kind === "cleanse");
      if (mend) {
        this.castAlly(c, mend);
        c.artCool = 4.5;
        pop = "HEAL";
      } else {
        // The fallback for a companion without a heal: it stands at your side and takes the edge off every hit.
        this.guardUntil = this.worldMs + ORDER.guard * 1000;
        this.allyStay = false;
        this.bolt(c.x, c.y, this.px, this.py, "ring", "#f4e27a");
        this.logLine = `${c.name} guards you.`;
        this.audio?.spell();
        pop = "GUARD";
      }
    } else {
      this.allyStay = !this.allyStay;
      this.allyStayAt = `${this.mapId}:${this.inside}`;
      pop = this.allyStay ? "STAY" : "FOLLOW";
      this.logLine = this.allyStay ? `${c.name} holds here.` : `${c.name} follows.`;
    }
    this.orderCool[o] = this.worldMs + ORDER.cool[o] * 1000;
    c.act = "cast";
    c.actFor = 0.3;
    this.pushPop(c.x, c.y - 30, pop, "violet");
  }

  private hurtFoe(r: Roamer, dealt: number, verb: "slash" | "whirl" | "smite" = "slash", ally = false) {
    if (!ally) this.lastHit = r; // playtest1k: "Focus my target" reads the foe you last hit
    dealt = this.tuned(r, dealt, verb);
    if ((r.shell ?? 0) > 0) {
      const bite = verb === "smite" ? dealt * 2 : dealt;
      r.shell = (r.shell ?? 0) - bite;
      if ((r.shell ?? 0) > 0) {
        this.logLine = `The shell takes ${bite}.`;
        r.flash = 0.08;
        return;
      }
      dealt = Math.max(1, -(r.shell ?? 0));
      r.shell = 0;
    }
    r.hp = Math.max(0, (r.hp ?? 0) - dealt);
    if (r.affix === "linked" && r.link != null) {
      for (const o of this.roamers) if (o !== r && o.link === r.link && o.affix === "linked") o.hp = r.hp;
    }
    r.flash = 0.08;
    const dx = r.x - this.px;
    const dy = r.y - this.py;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    this.tryBody(r, (dx / dist) * 3, (dy / dist) * 3);
    r.aggro = true;
    this.nums.push({ x: r.x, y: r.y - 16, text: String(dealt), life: 0.55 });
    this.spark(r.x, r.y - 10, "#f4e27a");
    this.logLine = `${dealt} on ${r.name ?? "it"}.`;
    if ((r.hp ?? 0) <= 0) {
      if (r.affix === "linked" && r.link != null) {
        for (const o of [...this.roamers]) if (o.link === r.link && o.affix === "linked") this.fellFoe(o);
        return;
      }
      this.fellFoe(r);
    }
  }

  /** Family slivers. Never zero. Whirl cracks bone. Smite bites ghosts. */
  private tuned(r: Roamer, dealt: number, verb: "slash" | "whirl" | "smite") {
    let m = 1;
    const bone = r.family === "skeleton" || r.family === "lich" || r.def === "lich" || r.def === "saint" || r.def === "abbot";
    if (bone && verb === "whirl") m = 1.2;
    if ((r.family === "ghost" || r.def === "shade" || r.def === "bride" || r.def === "tzar") && verb === "smite") m = 1.2;
    const court = r.family === "vampire" || r.def === "dracula" || r.def === "queen" || r.def === "leech" || r.def === "vamp";
    if (court && verb === "smite" && r.casting === "big") m = 1.25;
    if ((r.family === "mummy" || r.family === "tree" || r.def === "mummyboss" || r.def === "wrapped" || r.def === "rootwidow") && verb === "whirl") m = 1.15;
    return Math.max(1, Math.round(dealt * m));
  }

  private fellFoe(r: Roamer) {
    this.fx.death(r.x, r.y - 8, r.boss ? "boss" : r.mini || r.rare ? "elite" : "trash", boneKin(r));
    if (r.guard && r.rare) this.opened.add(r.guard);
    if (r.boss && r.def) {
      this.bossDead[r.def] = this.worldMs + 2 * CYCLE_MS;
      if (MINI_NAME[r.def]) this.remnantId = r.def;
    }
    if (r.hunter) {
      this.hunterGoneUntil = this.phase === "day" ? nextNight(this.worldMs) : nextDay(this.worldMs);
      this.hunterBanUntil = nextNight(this.worldMs);
      this.barred.inn = this.hunterBanUntil;
      this.barred.casino = this.hunterBanUntil;
    }
    if (!r.helper) {
      this.xp += r.xp ?? 0;
      if (r.family && !this.bestiary.includes(r.family)) this.bestiary.push(r.family);
      const silver = r.silver ?? 0;
      if (silver > 0) this.dropSilver(r.x - 8, r.y + 6, silver);
      const source = r.boss ? "boss" : r.mini ? "rare" : "common";
      if (!r.mimic && (r.boss || r.mini || Math.random() > 0.35)) this.dropItem(r.x + 8, r.y + 4, this.makeDrop(source, !!r.boss));
      if (r.mimic) this.mimicLoot(r);
      if (r.boss || r.mini) this.maybeRelic(!!r.boss, r.x, r.y - 8);
      this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} falls. ${r.xp ?? 0} experience. The loot is on the ground.`;
      if (r.mini && r.def && r.def === this.remnantId) {
        this.coin += 20;
        this.remnantId = "";
        this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "The remnant"} falls. The chapel pays 20 silver.`;
      }
      // A cursed floor pays extra: a second pile beside the first, a set share of the foe's silver.
      if (silver > 0 && this.curseOn() && this.curse) {
        const extra = Math.max(1, Math.round((silver * RUNS.bonus[this.curse]) / 100));
        this.dropSilver(r.x + 4, r.y + 12, extra);
        this.logLine = `${this.logLine} The curse pays ${extra} more.`;
      }
      if (this.mapId === "world") {
        const zone = biomeName(Math.floor(this.px / TILE), Math.floor(this.py / TILE));
        if (!this.zones.includes(zone)) this.zones.push(zone);
        const board: Record<string, number> = { "Decayed vale": 8, "Winter hollow": 12, "Dry waste": 16, Cinder: 20, "Scourge swamp": 28 };
        const pay = board[zone];
        if (pay && !this.paidZones.includes(zone)) {
          this.paidZones.push(zone);
          this.coin += pay;
          this.logLine = `${zone} is marked. The board pays ${pay} silver.`;
        }
      }
      this.payBounty(r);
      this.festivalFell(r);
      this.countErrandKill();
    }
    // Ashen: the floor, not the foe, bursts the body. A plate-style mark, then the plate's circle test.
    if (this.curseOn() && this.curse === "ash") {
      this.ashes.push({ x: r.x, y: r.y, tell: RUNS.ashTell });
      this.logLine = `${this.logLine} Its ash gathers. Step out of the mark.`;
    }
    this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
    this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 1);
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.checkLevel();
  }

  /** Trash swings. A boss paints its named big move. Walk out or take it. */
  private tickField(dt: number) {
    if (this.iframe > 0) this.iframe = Math.max(0, this.iframe - dt);
    this.nums = this.nums.filter((n) => (n.life -= dt) > 0);
    this.fx.update(dt);
    this.riftSwirl(dt);
    this.noteBossRoom();
    this.spells = this.spells.filter((s) => (s.life -= dt) > 0);
    // playtest1k: combo and order pops rise and fade; statuses read the weather once a frame.
    this.pops = this.pops.filter((p) => (p.life -= dt) > 0);
    for (const p of this.pops) p.y -= 12 * dt;
    const weather = this.weather;
    for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r)) continue;
      this.freeBody(r);
      r.actFor = Math.max(0, (r.actFor ?? 0) - dt);
      r.flash = Math.max(0, (r.flash ?? 0) - dt);
      r.age = (r.age ?? 0) + dt;
      this.tickStatus(r, dt, weather);
      if (this.roomHold(r, dt)) continue; // playtest1n: shoved or stunned, it does nothing else this frame
      if (r.goblin && (r.age ?? 0) > 4) {
        const ox = r.x;
        const oy = r.y;
        this.tryBody(r, r.x < this.px ? -50 * dt : 50 * dt, r.y < this.py ? -30 * dt : 30 * dt);
        r.moving = Math.hypot(r.x - ox, r.y - oy) > 0.2;
        if ((r.age ?? 0) > 5.4) {
          this.logLine = "The goblin bolts.";
          this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((o) => o !== r);
        }
        continue;
      }
      // playtest1k: a taunted foe turns on the companion (it walks to it, swings at it and marks it).
      const aim = this.aimOf(r);
      const dx = aim.x - r.x;
      const dy = aim.y - r.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      const leash = (r.boss ? 40 : 24) * TILE;
      if (!r.boss && dist > leash) {
        r.aggro = false;
        r.hp = r.max;
        r.tell = 0;
        continue;
      }
      // playtest1j: a boss with a big move turns to phase two at half HP: it roars (stands and glows, no hit), then its
      // big moves come quicker and in patterns (telegraph.ts).
      if (r.boss && r.big && (r.phase ?? 1) < 2 && (r.hp ?? 1) <= (r.max ?? 1) * PHASE.at && !(r.tell ?? 0)) {
        r.phase = 2;
        r.wave = Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / PHASE.cycle2);
        r.cool = Math.max(r.cool ?? 0, PHASE.roar);
        r.act = "cast";
        r.actFor = PHASE.roar;
        r.flash = PHASE.roar;
        this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} roars. Its second phase begins.`;
      }
      if ((r.tell ?? 0) > 0) {
        r.moving = false;
        r.tell = (r.tell ?? 0) - dt;
        if ((r.tell ?? 0) <= 0) {
          // playtest1j: an aimed phase-two big is followed by its echo, whatever the first one hit.
          const echo = r.pattern === "echo";
          this.resolveCast(r, aim.ally ? Math.hypot(this.px - r.x, this.py - r.y) || 1 : dist);
          if (echo && this.liveFoe(r)) this.beginEcho(r);
        }
        continue;
      }
      const ox = r.x;
      const oy = r.y;
      const esc = r.atEscort && this.escort?.state === "follow" ? this.escort : null;
      if (esc) {
        // Walking over to the captive for its one swing (owner-approved 2026-09-30); a brawler's pace.
        const ex = esc.x - r.x;
        const ey = esc.y - r.y;
        const ed = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1;
        if (ed > 16) this.tryBody(r, (ex / ed) * 36 * (r.affix === "fast" ? 1.25 : 1) * dt, (ey / ed) * 36 * (r.affix === "fast" ? 1.25 : 1) * dt);
      } else this.stepFoe(r, dx / dist, dy / dist, dist, dt);
      r.moving = Math.hypot(r.x - ox, r.y - oy) > 0.2;
      r.cool = (r.cool ?? 0) - dt;
      if ((r.cool ?? 0) > 0) continue;
