      if (r.boss && r.big && this.wantBig(r)) this.beginCast(r, "big", r.big, r.bigTag ?? "ring", 1);
      else if ((r.boss || r.mini) && r.mid && (r.age ?? 0) > 3.2) this.beginCast(r, "mid", r.mid, r.midTag ?? "ring", 0.5);
      // A mimic only bites from inside its own mark's reach; it walks in first. Other rares are unchanged.
      else if (r.rare && (!r.mimic || dist < 40)) this.beginCast(r, "spam", r.spam ?? "swipe", r.spamTag ?? "melee", 0.35);
      else if (this.swingAtEscort(r, dist)) {
        // One swing in ESCORT.escortEvery with the captive in reach went to them.
      } else if (this.inReach(r, dist)) this.landSpam(r, dist);
      if (r.affix === "horde" && (r.age ?? 0) > 8 && Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / 8) !== (r.wave ?? 0)) {
        r.wave = Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / 8);
        this.summonPack(r, 2);
      }
      if (r.affix === "vortex" && (r.age ?? 0) > 10 && Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / 10) !== (r.hits ?? 0)) {
        r.hits = Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / 10);
        this.beginCast(r, "mid", "vortex", "pull", 0.35);
      }
    }
  }

  private wantBig(r: Roamer) {
    if ((r.age ?? 0) < 6) return false;
    if (r.personality === "tyrant" && (r.hp ?? 1) > (r.max ?? 1) * 0.4 && (r.age ?? 0) < 12) return false;
    return Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / this.bigCycle(r)) !== (r.wave ?? 0);
  }

  /** playtest1j: seconds between big moves (8 in phase one, the old cycle; 6 in phase two). */
  private bigCycle(r: Roamer) {
    return (r.phase ?? 1) >= 2 ? PHASE.cycle2 : PHASE.cycle1;
  }

  private inReach(r: Roamer, dist: number) {
    return (r.spamTag === "bolt" ? dist < 120 : dist < 22);
  }

  private stepFoe(r: Roamer, ux: number, uy: number, dist: number, dt: number) {
    const fast = r.affix === "fast" ? 1.25 : 1;
    const p = r.personality ?? "brawler";
    const step = (sx: number, sy: number) => this.roomStep(r, sx, sy); // playtest1n: a wary foe steps round a live hazard
    if (p === "anchor" && r.homeX != null && r.homeY != null) {
      if (Math.hypot(r.x - r.homeX, r.y - r.homeY) > 12) step(Math.sign(r.homeX - r.x) * 20 * dt, Math.sign(r.homeY - r.y) * 20 * dt);
      return;
    }
    if (p === "kiter" || p === "warden") {
      if (p === "kiter" && dist < 96) step(-ux * 36 * fast * dt, -uy * 36 * fast * dt);
      else if (dist > 140) step(ux * 28 * fast * dt, uy * 28 * fast * dt);
      return;
    }
    if (p === "skirmisher") {
      const out = (r.hits ?? 0) >= 2;
      const pace = (out ? -48 : 70) * fast * dt;
      step(ux * pace, uy * pace);
      if (out && dist > 80) r.hits = 0;
      return;
    }
    if (dist > 16) step(ux * (p === "zealot" ? 46 : 36) * fast * dt, uy * (p === "zealot" ? 46 : 36) * fast * dt);
  }

  private beginCast(r: Roamer, slot: "mid" | "big" | "spam", name: string, tag: string, seconds: number) {
    r.casting = slot;
    const aim = this.aimOf(r);
    r.markX = aim.x;
    r.markY = aim.y;
    r.markR = markRadius(tag);
    r.pattern = "";
    // playtest1j: a big move's pattern (phase one aims; phase two alternates a nova round the boss and an aimed mark
    // with an echo), and every mid and big mark long enough to walk out of on a touch stick (telegraph.ts tellFor).
    if (slot === "big") {
      const pat = bigPattern(r.phase ?? 1, r.bigs ?? 0, tag);
      r.bigs = (r.bigs ?? 0) + 1;
      if ((r.phase ?? 1) >= 2) seconds = TELL.bigPhase2;
      if (pat === "nova") {
        r.markX = r.x;
        r.markY = r.y;
        r.markR = PHASE.novaR;
        tag = "ring";
      }
      r.pattern = pat === "aim" ? "" : pat;
    }
    r.tell = tellFor(slot, tag, seconds, r.markR);
    r.tellMax = r.tell;
    r.cool = slot === "big" ? 2.2 : 1.3;
    r.act = "cast";
    r.actFor = r.tell;
    if (slot === "big") r.wave = Math.floor((r.age ?? 0) / this.bigCycle(r));
    this.logLine = r.pattern === "nova" ? `${r.name ?? "It"} gathers ${name} around itself.` : `${r.name ?? "It"} marks ${name}.`;
  }

  /** playtest1j: the echo, a second mark on the spot the hero stepped to, right after an aimed phase-two big move. */
  private beginEcho(r: Roamer) {
    r.casting = "mid";
    const aim = this.aimOf(r);
    r.markX = aim.x;
    r.markY = aim.y;
    r.markR = markRadius("ring");
    r.pattern = "echoed";
    r.tell = tellFor("mid", "ring", TELL.echo, r.markR);
    r.tellMax = r.tell;
    r.cool = 1.3;
    r.act = "cast";
    r.actFor = r.tell;
    this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} marks ${r.big ?? "it"} again.`;
  }

  private resolveCast(r: Roamer, dist: number) {
    const pattern = r.pattern ?? "";
    const name = pattern === "echoed" ? `${r.big ?? "it"}'s echo` : r.casting === "big" ? r.big : r.casting === "spam" ? r.spam : r.mid;
    const tag = pattern === "nova" || pattern === "echoed" ? "ring" : r.casting === "big" ? r.bigTag : r.casting === "spam" ? r.spamTag : r.midTag;
    const slot = r.casting;
    r.pattern = "";
    const mx = r.markX ?? this.px;
    const my = r.markY ?? this.py;
    const inside = this.inMark(mx, my, r.markR ?? 40);
    r.casting = "";
    r.tell = 0;
    // The line is swept from where the foe stood when the tell ended, so a lunge cannot shorten it.
    const ox = r.x;
    const oy = r.y;
    if (r.mimic && slot === "spam") {
      // The bite lunge: the chest jumps at its own mark. The hit test below is the existing line shape.
      const lx = mx - r.x;
      const ly = my - r.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(lx, ly);
      const reach = Math.min(MIMICS.lunge, Math.max(0, d - 8));
      if (d > 0 && reach > 0) this.tryBody(r, (lx / d) * reach, (ly / d) * reach);
      r.act = "swing";
      r.actFor = 0.28;
    }
    this.bolt(r.x, r.y - 14, mx, my, tag === "ring" ? "ring" : "bolt");
    this.audio?.spell();
    if (tag === "shield") {
      r.ac = (r.ac ?? 0) + 2;
      this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} raises ${name}.`;
      return;
    }
    if (tag === "blink") {
      const spot = this.openNear(this.px - 18, this.py);
      r.x = spot.x;
      r.y = spot.y;
      this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} steps through you.`;
      return;
    }
    if (tag === "summon") {
      this.summonPack(r, 1);
      this.logLine = `${r.name ?? "It"} calls with ${name}.`;
      return;
    }
    if (tag === "dark") this.darkUntil = this.worldMs + 1000;
    if (tag === "pull" && inside) {
      const dx = r.x - this.px;
      const dy = r.y - this.py;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      this.try((dx / d) * 36, (dy / d) * 36);
    }
    const hit =
      tag === "line"
        ? this.nearLine(ox, oy, mx, my)
        : tag === "cone"
          ? dist < 80 && inside
          : inside;
    // playtest1k: a mark aimed at a taunting companion lands on it if it stands there (it does not dodge).
    const ally = this.tauntOf(r);
    if (ally && Math.hypot(ally.x - mx, ally.y - my) < (r.markR ?? 40)) {
      const took = Math.max(1, (r.atk ?? 2) + (slot === "big" ? 4 : 1));
      this.hurtAlly(took, `${r.name ?? "It"} lands ${name} on ${ally.name} for ${took}.`);
      if (!hit) return;
    }
    if (!hit) {
      this.logLine = `You step clear of ${name}.`;
      return;
    }
    const dealt = Math.max(1, (r.atk ?? 2) + (slot === "big" ? 4 : 1) - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
    this.bite(dealt, `${r.name ?? "It"} lands ${name} for ${dealt}.`);
  }

  // ---- playtest1n [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-03 15:41 ET: use-the-room combat] ----
  // Bill (2026-10-03 15:41 ET): knock foes into spikes and hazards, drop lanterns into fire, set off oil and powder
  // barrels, break pillars; foes a little wary; fair (every hazard is marked before it lands); saves untouched.

  /** This floor's props, from its seed (room.ts placeRoom), the same whichever stair you came by: the arrival kept clear
   * is the up stair's (the down stair's is inside the stair gap). Hidden secret rooms count as rock, so nothing stands there. */
  private placeFloorRoom(seedKey: string) {
    const tiles = Uint8Array.from(this.tiles);
    for (const i of this.hidden) if (tiles[i] !== T.chest) tiles[i] = T.wall; // a secret room is rock; its chest still keeps props off
    const up = findTile(this.tiles, this.w, T.stairU) ?? findTile(this.tiles, this.w, T.floor) ?? { x: 2, y: 2 };
    this.room = placeRoom(tiles, this.w, this.h, seedKey, this.floor, featSpots(this.feats), stepOff(this.tiles, this.w, this.h, up.x, up.y));
  }

  /** The nearest prop in reach that can still be struck, on explored floor. */
  private propInReach(reach: number): RoomProp | null {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon") return null;
    let best: RoomProp | null = null;
    let bd = reach;
    for (const p of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(p) || (this.fog && this.fog[p.y * this.w + p.x] === 0)) continue;
      const c = centre(p, TILE);
      const d = Math.hypot(c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py);
      if (d < bd) {
        best = p;
        bd = d;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  /** The hero's swing on the props in its arc (aim, half-angle, reach as strikeCone). The log keeps a foe's line. */
  private strikeProps(aim: number, half: number, reach: number, foeHit: boolean) {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon") return 0;
    let n = 0;
    const log = this.logLine;
    for (const p of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(p) || !inSwing(p, this.px, this.py, aim, half, reach, TILE)) continue;
      const c = centre(p, TILE);
      this.hitProp(p, c.x - this.px, c.y - this.py, null);
      n += 1;
    }
    if (n && foeHit) this.logLine = `${log} ${this.logLine}`;
    return n;
  }

  /** A slash or whirl shoves the foe it hits (below ground: that is where the hazards are). Minis and rares less, bosses not. */
  private shove(r: Roamer, ux: number, uy: number) {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || r.boss || !this.liveFoe(r)) return;
    const k = r.mini || r.rare ? ROOM.shoveMini : 1;
    r.shoveX = ux * ROOM.shove * k;
    r.shoveY = uy * ROOM.shove * k;
    r.shoveT = ROOM.shoveFor;
  }

  /** The room's hold on a foe this frame: the shove carries it, a stun stands it still. True while either lasts. */
  private roomHold(r: Roamer, dt: number) {
    if (r.oilT) r.oilT = Math.max(0, r.oilT - dt);
    if ((r.shoveT ?? 0) > 0) {
      const t = Math.min(dt, r.shoveT ?? 0);
      this.tryBody(r, (r.shoveX ?? 0) * t, (r.shoveY ?? 0) * t);
      r.shoveT = (r.shoveT ?? 0) - dt;
      r.moving = true;
      if ((r.shoveT ?? 0) <= 0) this.shoveLands(r);
      return true;
    }
    if ((r.stun ?? 0) > 0) {
      r.stun = Math.max(0, (r.stun ?? 0) - dt);
      r.moving = false;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /** A shoved foe lands. On a spike trap it springs the spikes at once, whatever their cycle (that is the point of the
   * shove), and the foe reels ROOM.spikeStun s; a spike bites a foe once per rise either way. */
  private shoveLands(r: Roamer) {
    const sec = this.worldMs / 1000;
    const tx = Math.floor(r.x / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(r.y / TILE);
    const t = this.feats?.traps?.find((k) => k.kind === "spike" && k.x === tx && k.y === ty);
    if (!t || !this.liveFoe(r) || sec - (r.spikeAt ?? -99) < TRAPS.spikeUp + 0.05) return;
    r.spikeAt = sec;
    this.bolt(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, "ring", "#b07aff");
    this.roomHurt(r, trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel()) * ROOM.foeSpike, "Thrown on the spikes:", false);
    this.roomStun(r, ROOM.spikeStun);
  }

  /** A foe's step. A wary one (room.ts wary: two in three, never a boss) will not step into a live hazard: it slides
   * round it, or waits. Bold ones walk on. Only below ground, and only while something there is live. */
  private roomStep(r: Roamer, sx: number, sy: number) {
    const traps = this.feats?.traps;
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon" || !wary(r.id, !!r.boss) || (!roomBusy(this.room) && !traps?.some((t) => t.kind === "spike"))) {
      this.tryBody(r, sx, sy);
      return;
    }
    const sec = this.worldMs / 1000;
    const bad = (x: number, y: number) => hazardAt(this.room, traps, sec, x, y, TILE);
    const len = Math.hypot(sx, sy) || 1;
    const ahead = (ax: number, ay: number) => bad(r.x + (ax / len) * 10, r.y + (ay / len) * 10);
    if (bad(r.x, r.y) || !ahead(sx, sy)) {
      this.tryBody(r, sx, sy);
      return;
    }
    if (!ahead(-sy, sx)) this.tryBody(r, -sy, sx);
    else if (!ahead(sy, -sx)) this.tryBody(r, sy, -sx);
  }

  /** Strike a prop (from the direction dx, dy). Fire sets it alight instead; stone does not burn. */
  private hitProp(p: RoomProp, dx: number, dy: number, el: Element | null) {
    if (!propLive(p)) return;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const ux = dx / d;
    const uy = dy / d;
    const c = centre(p, TILE);
    this.spark(c.x, c.y - 10, p.kind === "pillar" ? "#767488" : "#8a6848");
    if (el === "fire" && p.kind !== "pillar") {
      this.lightProp(p, ux, uy);
      return;
    }
    p.hp -= 1;
    if (p.kind === "pillar") {
      if (p.hp > 0) {
        this.logLine = p.hp === 1 ? "The pillar groans. One more." : "The pillar cracks.";
        return;
      }
      this.topple(p, ux, uy);
    } else if (p.kind === "lantern") this.dropLantern(p, ux, uy);
    else if (p.kind === "oil") this.burstOil(p, false);
    else this.lightFuse(p);
  }

  /** Fire on a prop: a lantern drops, an oil barrel goes up in a big fire, a powder barrel's fuse catches. */
  private lightProp(p: RoomProp, ux: number, uy: number) {
    if (!propLive(p)) return;
    if (p.kind === "lantern") this.dropLantern(p, ux, uy);
    else if (p.kind === "oil") this.burstOil(p, true);
    else if (p.kind === "powder") this.lightFuse(p);
  }

  private propIndex(p: RoomProp) {
    return p.y * this.w + p.x;
  }

  /** The lantern swings off its hook away from the blow; its red mark fills where it will land (room.ts dropTell). */
  private dropLantern(p: RoomProp, ux: number, uy: number) {
    p.done = true;
    const c = centre(p, TILE);
    let x = c.x + ux * ROOM.dropOut;
    let y = c.y + uy * ROOM.dropOut + 2;
    if (this.solidAt(x, y) || this.blockedProp(x, y)) {
      x = c.x + ux * 4;
      y = c.y + 6;
    }
    this.room.drops.push({ x, y, tell: ROOM.dropTell, max: ROOM.dropTell });
    this.logLine = "The lantern swings off its hook.";
  }

  /** An oil barrel bursts: a slick (foes in it are oiled), or a big fire if it was lit. */
  private burstOil(p: RoomProp, lit: boolean) {
    p.done = true;
    this.room.solid.delete(this.propIndex(p));
    const c = centre(p, TILE);
    if (lit) {
      addPatch(this.room, "fire", c.x, c.y, ROOM.fireBigR);
      this.bolt(c.x, c.y - 6, c.x, c.y - 6, "nova", "#e04a2a");
      this.logLine = "The oil barrel goes up.";
    } else {
      addPatch(this.room, "oil", c.x, c.y + 2, ROOM.oilR);
      this.logLine = "The oil barrel bursts. Anything that walks the slick will burn.";
    }
    this.audio?.hit();
  }

  /** A powder barrel's fuse: its red blast mark fills over ROOM.fuse, then it goes off. */
  private lightFuse(p: RoomProp) {
    if (p.fuse > 0 || p.done) return;
    p.fuse = ROOM.fuse;
    this.logLine = "The powder barrel's fuse catches. Get clear.";
  }

  /** A pillar on its last crack topples away from the blow: its violet lane fills, then it lands. */
  private topple(p: RoomProp, ux: number, uy: number) {
    p.done = true;
    this.room.solid.delete(this.propIndex(p));
    const c = centre(p, TILE);
    this.room.falls.push({ x: c.x, y: c.y, dx: ux, dy: uy, tell: ROOM.fallTell, max: ROOM.fallTell, k: this.room.props.indexOf(p) });
    this.logLine = "The pillar topples.";
  }

  /** Fire at (x, y) within rad: props there catch, oil there goes up. (dx, dy) is the way the fire came from. */
  private roomIgnite(x: number, y: number, rad: number, dx = 0, dy = 1) {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon") return;
    for (const p of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(p) || p.kind === "pillar") continue;
      const c = centre(p, TILE);
      if (Math.hypot(c.x - x, c.y - y) <= rad) this.lightProp(p, c.x - x || dx, c.y - y || dy);
    }
    for (const o of this.room.patches) {
      if (o.kind !== "oil" || o.life <= 0 || Math.hypot(o.x - x, o.y - y) > rad + o.r) continue;
      o.kind = "fire";
      o.r = ROOM.fireBigR;
      o.life = ROOM.fireLife;
      o.max = ROOM.fireLife;
      o.tick = 0;
      this.logLine = "The oil catches.";
    }
  }

  /** An area spell on the room: every live prop in rad is struck (fire lights it), and fire lights the oil there. */
  private roomArea(x: number, y: number, rad: number, el: Element | null) {
    if (this.mapId !== "dungeon") return 0;
    let n = 0;
    for (const p of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(p)) continue;
      const c = centre(p, TILE);
      if (Math.hypot(c.x - x, c.y - y) > rad) continue;
      this.hitProp(p, c.x - x, c.y - y, el);
      n += 1;
    }
    if (el === "fire") this.roomIgnite(x, y, rad);
    return n;
  }

  /** The room hurts a foe: its armour halves it, fire is a fire hit (1k: poisoned is VENOM BLAST, oiled is WILDFIRE).
   * Room damage is the companion's kind of hit: it does not move "Focus my target". */
  private roomHurt(r: Roamer, dealt: number, label: string, fire: boolean) {
    if (!this.liveFoe(r)) return;
    const d = Math.max(1, Math.round(dealt) - Math.floor((r.ac ?? 0) / 2));
    this.castCombos = 0;
    this.castNames = [];
    this.hurtFoe(r, d, "slash", true);
    if (fire) this.applyElement(r, "fire", d, true);
    this.logLine = `${label} ${this.logLine}`;
  }

  /** The room hurts the hero: lightly, through armour, iframes and the ward like any hit. */
  private roomBite(share: number, label: string) {
    const dealt = Math.max(1, Math.round(trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel()) * share) - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
    this.bite(dealt, `${label} ${dealt}.`);
  }

  private roomStun(r: Roamer, s: number) {
    if (!this.liveFoe(r) || r.boss) return;
    r.stun = Math.max(r.stun ?? 0, r.mini || r.rare ? s / 2 : s);
  }

  /** A powder barrel goes off: foes in the blast take it hard and reel, the hero lightly; props in it catch. */
  private blast(p: RoomProp) {
    p.fuse = 0;
    p.done = true;
    this.room.solid.delete(this.propIndex(p));
    const c = centre(p, TILE);
    const atk = trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel());
    this.bolt(c.x, c.y - 6, c.x, c.y - 6, "nova", "#e04a2a");
    for (const [sx, sy] of [[-10, -12], [10, -12], [-12, 2], [12, 2], [0, -18]]) this.spark(c.x + sx, c.y + sy, "#e04a2a");
    this.audio?.hit();
    let log = "The powder barrel blows.";
    for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r) || Math.hypot(r.x - c.x, r.y - c.y) > ROOM.blastR) continue;
      this.roomHurt(r, atk * ROOM.foeBlast, "The blast:", true);
      log = this.logLine;
      this.roomStun(r, ROOM.blastStun);
    }
    if (Math.hypot(this.px - c.x, this.py - c.y) <= ROOM.blastR) this.roomBite(ROOM.heroBlast, "The blast catches you for");
    else this.logLine = log;
    for (const o of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(o)) continue;
      const oc = centre(o, TILE);
      if (Math.hypot(oc.x - c.x, oc.y - c.y) > ROOM.blastR + ROOM.igniteR / 2) continue;
      if (o.kind === "pillar") this.hitProp(o, oc.x - c.x, oc.y - c.y, null);
      else this.lightProp(o, oc.x - c.x, oc.y - c.y);
    }
    this.roomIgnite(c.x, c.y, ROOM.blastR);
  }

  /** A pillar lands: foes in its lane take it and are stunned (a mini or rare half as long, a boss not), the hero lightly. */
  private landFall(f: Fall) {
    const atk = trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel());
    const ex = f.x + f.dx * ROOM.fallLen;
    const ey = f.y + f.dy * ROOM.fallLen;
    this.bolt(ex, ey - 4, ex, ey - 4, "ring", "#767488");
    for (let t = 8; t <= ROOM.fallLen; t += 12) this.spark(f.x + f.dx * t, f.y + f.dy * t - 4, "#767488");
    this.audio?.hit();
    let log = "The pillar comes down.";
    for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
      if (!this.liveFoe(r) || !inLane(f, r.x, r.y)) continue;
      this.roomHurt(r, atk * ROOM.foePillar, "The pillar falls:", false);
      log = this.logLine;
      this.roomStun(r, ROOM.stun);
    }
    if (inLane(f, this.px, this.py)) this.roomBite(ROOM.heroBlast, "The pillar clips you for");
    else this.logLine = log;
    for (const o of this.room.props) {
      if (!propLive(o)) continue;
      const oc = centre(o, TILE);
      if (inLane(f, oc.x, oc.y)) this.hitProp(o, f.dx, f.dy, null);
    }
  }

  /**
   * The room, once a frame below ground: spikes now bite foes too (once per rise; a shove is how they get there), a
   * dropped lantern lands and lights, fuses burn down, pillars land, fire patches burn (every ROOM.fireTick: foes as a
   * fire hit, the hero lightly, oil and props in reach catch) and oil soaks the foes that walk it.
   */
  private tickRoom(dt: number) {
    const room = this.room;
    const traps = this.feats?.traps ?? [];
    if (!roomBusy(room) && !traps.length) return;
    const atk = trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel());
    const sec = this.worldMs / 1000;
    for (const t of traps) {
      if (t.kind !== "spike" || spikeStage(t, sec) !== 2) continue;
      for (const r of [...this.roamers]) {
        if (!this.liveFoe(r) || Math.floor(r.x / TILE) !== t.x || Math.floor(r.y / TILE) !== t.y) continue;
        if (sec - (r.spikeAt ?? -99) < TRAPS.spikeUp + 0.05) continue;
        r.spikeAt = sec;
        this.roomHurt(r, atk * ROOM.foeSpike, "Spikes:", false);
      }
    }
    if (!roomBusy(room)) return;
    for (const d of room.drops) {
      d.tell -= dt;
      if (d.tell > 0) continue;
      addPatch(room, "fire", d.x, d.y, ROOM.fireR);
      this.bolt(d.x, d.y - 4, d.x, d.y - 4, "ring", "#e04a2a");
      this.audio?.hit();
      this.roomIgnite(d.x, d.y, ROOM.fireR);
    }
    room.drops = room.drops.filter((d) => d.tell > 0);
    for (const p of room.props) {
      if (p.fuse <= 0) continue;
      p.fuse -= dt;
      if (p.fuse <= 0) this.blast(p);
    }
    for (const f of room.falls) {
      f.tell -= dt;
      if (f.tell <= 0) this.landFall(f);
    }
    room.falls = room.falls.filter((f) => f.tell > 0);
    for (const pa of [...room.patches]) {
      pa.life -= dt;
      if (pa.life <= 0) continue;
      if (pa.kind === "oil") {
        for (const r of this.roamers) if (this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - pa.x, r.y - pa.y) < pa.r) r.oilT = ROOM.oilSoak;
        continue;
      }
      pa.tick -= dt;
      if (pa.tick > 0) continue;
      pa.tick = ROOM.fireTick;
      for (const r of [...this.roamers]) if (this.liveFoe(r) && Math.hypot(r.x - pa.x, r.y - pa.y) < pa.r) this.roomHurt(r, atk * ROOM.foeFire, "Fire:", true);
      if (patchAt(room, this.px, this.py, "fire") === pa) this.roomBite(ROOM.heroFire, "The fire burns you for");
      this.roomIgnite(pa.x, pa.y, pa.r + 4);
    }
    room.patches = room.patches.filter((pa) => pa.life > 0);
  }

  /** The telegraph hit test, shared by foe marks and plate traps: a circle on the mark center. */
  private inMark(mx: number, my: number, rad: number) {
    return Math.hypot(this.px - mx, this.py - my) < rad;
  }

  /**
   * Floor traps hurt the hero only. Foes and companions walk over them. Spikes hurt while up;
   * a plate stepped on marks a circle on itself for 0.5 s. Walk out of the mark or take the hit.
   */
  private tickTraps(dt: number) {
    const traps = this.feats?.traps;
    if (!traps?.length) return;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const dealt = Math.max(1, trapAtk(this.dungeonLevel()) - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
    traps.forEach((t, i) => {
      const on = t.x === tx && t.y === ty;
      if (t.kind === "spike") {
        if (on && spikeStage(t, this.worldMs / 1000) === 2) this.bite(dealt, `Floor spikes for ${dealt}.`);
        return;
      }
      const p = this.plates[i] ?? (this.plates[i] = { tell: 0, cool: 0 });
      if (p.tell > 0) {
        p.tell = Math.max(0, p.tell - dt);
        if (p.tell > 0) return;
        const mx = t.x * TILE + 8;
        const my = t.y * TILE + 8;
        p.cool = TRAPS.plateRearm;
        this.bolt(mx, my - 14, mx, my, "ring");
        this.audio?.spell();
        if (this.inMark(mx, my, TRAPS.plateRadius)) this.bite(dealt, `The plate's trap lands for ${dealt}.`);
        else this.logLine = "You step clear of the plate.";
        return;
      }
      if (p.cool > 0) {
        p.cool = Math.max(0, p.cool - dt);
        return;
      }
      if (on && this.fallen <= 0) {
        p.tell = TRAPS.plateTell;
        this.logLine = "A plate clicks underfoot.";
      }
    });
  }

  private landSpam(r: Roamer, dist: number) {
    if (!this.inReach(r, dist)) return;
    const dealt = Math.max(1, (r.atk ?? 2) - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
    r.cool = r.boss ? 1.15 : 0.85;
    r.hits = (r.hits ?? 0) + 1;
    r.act = r.spamTag === "bolt" ? "cast" : "swing";
    r.actFor = 0.28;
    const ally = this.tauntOf(r);
    if (ally) {
      // playtest1k: the taunted foe's swing goes to the companion.
      if (r.spamTag === "bolt") this.bolt(r.x, r.y - 12, ally.x, ally.y - 10, "bolt");
      const took = Math.max(1, r.atk ?? 2);
      this.hurtAlly(took, `${r.name ?? "It"} uses ${r.spam ?? "a swing"} on ${ally.name} for ${took}.`);
      return;
    }
    if (r.spamTag === "bolt") this.bolt(r.x, r.y - 12, this.px, this.py - 10, "bolt");
    this.bite(dealt, `${r.name ?? "It"} uses ${r.spam ?? "a swing"} for ${dealt}.`);
  }

  private nearLine(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number) {
    const dx = x1 - x0;
    const dy = y1 - y0;
    const len = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((this.px - x0) * dx + (this.py - y0) * dy) / len));
    return Math.hypot(this.px - (x0 + dx * t), this.py - (y0 + dy * t)) < 16;
  }

  private summonPack(r: Roamer, n: number) {
    const live = this.roamers.filter((o) => o.add && (o.hp ?? 0) > 0).length;
    const room = Math.max(0, 6 - live);
    const kit = BOSS_KITS[r.def];
    const help = FAMILIES.find((f) => f.id === kit?.helper) ?? FAMILIES.find((f) => f.family === r.family) ?? FAMILIES[0];
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(n, room); i++) {
      const h = scaleMonster(help, Math.max(1, Math.round((r.level || this.level) * 0.55)));
      h.hp = Math.round(h.hp * 0.45);
      const spot = this.openNear(r.x + 16, r.y + 8);
      this.roamers.push({
        id: `add-${this.worldMs}-${i}`,
        x: spot.x,
        y: spot.y,
        family: help.family,
        tint: help.tint,
        def: help.id,
        level: r.level,
        ang: 0,
        hp: h.hp,
        max: h.hp,
        atk: h.atk,
        ac: h.ac,
        name: help.name,
        aggro: true,
        helper: true,
        add: true,
        spam: FAMILY_KITS[help.id]?.spam ?? "swipe",
        spamTag: FAMILY_KITS[help.id]?.spamTag ?? "melee",
        personality: FAMILY_KITS[help.id]?.personality ?? "brawler",
        cool: 0.6,
      });
      if (r.festival || r.naughty) this.roamers[this.roamers.length - 1].spawnAt = this.worldMs; // fade2 (OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-01 21:20 ET): spawn-time tag, read only by draw.ts
    }
  }

  private bolt(x: number, y: number, tx: number, ty: number, kind: "bolt" | "ring" | "nova" | "mend" | "cone" = "bolt", color = "#f4e27a") {
    if (this.spells.length >= 12) this.spells.shift();
    // playtest1 (owner-reported 2026-10-01, spells did not show): 0.6 s on screen, flight then impact (was 0.32 s).
    this.spells.push({ x, y, tx, ty, life: SPELL_LIFE, max: SPELL_LIFE, kind, color });
  }

  /** Four chips around a point, through the pool (combat channel, cap 24, oldest dies first). */
  private spark(x: number, y: number, color: string) {
    this.fx.spark(x, y, paletteIndex(color));
  }

  private bite(dealt: number, label: string) {
    if (this.fallen > 0 || this.iframe > 0) return;
    if (this.wardUntil > this.worldMs) dealt = Math.max(1, dealt - 2);
    // playtest1k: "Guard me" (a companion without a heal): it stands at your side and every hit is cut.
    if (this.guardUntil > this.worldMs && this.allyUp()) dealt = Math.max(1, Math.round(dealt * ORDER.guardCut));
    this.hp -= dealt;
    this.iframe = 0.7;
    this.nums.push({ x: this.px, y: this.py - 18, text: String(dealt), life: 0.55 });
    this.spark(this.px, this.py - 12, "#f4e27a");
    this.logLine = label;
    this.audio?.hurt();
    if (this.hp <= 0) this.fall();
  }

  /** Fallen overlay. Slot 1 reloads. No gold is taken. */
  private fall() {
    if (this.fallen > 0) return;
    this.fallen = 1.3;
    this.hp = Math.max(1, this.hp);
    this.roamers = this.roamers.filter((r) => !r.aggro);
    this.logLine = "You drop. The ash is patient.";
  }

  /**
   * A fight stays on the field. The old battle screen is not used.
   */
  openBattle(foes: Foe[], flags: { guard: boolean; goblin: boolean; hunter: boolean; fromRole: string }) {
    if (this.mode === "shop" || this.mode === "talk" || this.mode === "title") return;
    this.goal = null;
    this.audio?.hit();
    const link = this.worldMs;
    foes.forEach((f, i) => {
      const ang = (i / Math.max(1, foes.length)) * Math.PI * 2;
      const spot = this.openNear(this.px + Math.cos(ang) * 28, this.py + Math.sin(ang) * 18);
      const kit = (f.boss || f.mini) && BOSS_KITS[f.id] ? BOSS_KITS[f.id] : FAMILY_KITS[f.family] ?? FAMILY_KITS[f.id] ?? FAMILY_KITS.zombie;
      const bossKit = !!(f.boss || f.mini) && !!BOSS_KITS[f.id];
      this.roamers.push({
        id: f.id || `f${i}-${this.worldMs}`,
        x: spot.x,
        y: spot.y,
        family: f.family,
        tint: f.tint,
        boss: !!f.boss,
        mini: !!f.mini,
        rare: !!f.rare,
        def: f.id || f.family,
        level: this.level,
        ang,
        hp: f.hp,
        max: f.max,
        atk: f.atk,
        ac: f.ac,
        xp: f.xp,
        silver: f.silver,
        name: f.name,
        aggro: true,
        cool: 0.45,
        helper: !!f.helper,
        goblin: flags.goblin && i === 0,
        hunter: flags.hunter && i === 0,
        age: 0,
        homeX: spot.x,
        homeY: spot.y,
        spam: kit.spam,
        spamTag: kit.spamTag,
        mid: bossKit ? kit.mid : undefined,
        midTag: bossKit ? kit.midTag : undefined,
        big: f.boss && bossKit ? kit.big : undefined,
        bigTag: f.boss && bossKit ? kit.bigTag : undefined,
        personality: kit.personality,
        affix: f.affix,
        mimic: f.mimic || undefined,
        bounty: f.bounty || undefined,
        festival: f.festival || undefined,
        naughty: f.naughty || undefined,
        guard: f.guard || undefined,
        link: f.affix === "linked" ? link : undefined,
        shell: f.affix === "shielded" ? Math.round(f.max * 0.2) : 0,
      });
      if (i > 0 && (foes[0].festival || foes[0].naughty)) this.roamers[this.roamers.length - 1].spawnAt = this.worldMs; // fade2 (OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-01 21:20 ET): spawn-time tag, read only by draw.ts
    });
    for (let i = 0; i < this.roamers.length; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < i; j++) {
        const a = this.roamers[i];
        const b = this.roamers[j];
        if (!a.aggro || !b.aggro) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) >= 14) continue;
        a.x += 16;
        a.homeX = a.x;
      }
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = `${foes.map((f) => f.name).join(", ")} turn to face you.`;
  }

  /**
   * Choose one action. It plays out, the blow lands, then the next actor goes.
   * A buff still spends the turn and does not also swing.
   */
  command(kind: string, live = false) {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b || this.mode !== "battle") return;
    if (!live) {
      if (b.turn === "ally") {
        if (kind !== "strike" && kind !== "guard" && kind !== "cleave" && kind !== "ranged" && !this.artNamed(kind)) {
          this.pushLog("They do not know that.");
          return;
        }
        this.stage("ally", kind);
        return;
      }
      if (b.turn !== "you") return;
      if (!this.afford(kind)) return;
      this.stage("you", kind);
      return;
    }
    if (b.playerStun) {
      b.playerStun = false;
      this.pushLog("You are held still.");
      this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "Phylactery") {
      if (b.phylactery !== "whole") return;
      b.phylactery = "broken";
      this.pushLog("The phylactery cracks. He will not rise from it.");
      this.audio?.spell();
      this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "drive" && b.goblin) {
      this.loseSomething();
      this.pushLog("You drive it off. It takes a scrap with it.");
      this.endBattle(false);
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "rummage" && b.goblin) {
      const lost = this.loseUnequipped();
      this.pushLog(lost ? `It rummages and takes ${lost}.` : "It finds nothing loose.");
      this.endBattle(false);
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "flee") {
      const chance = Math.min(0.85, 0.28 + this.agi * 0.035) * (b.foes.some((f) => f.boss) ? 0.65 : 1);
      if (Math.random() < chance) {
        if (b.goblin) this.loseUnequipped();
        this.pushLog(b.goblin ? "You flee. The goblin still snags something." : "You break away.");
        this.returnPending();
        this.endBattle(false);
        return;
      }
      this.pushLog("The way back closes.");
      this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "Vanish" && this.specials.includes("Vanish")) {
      this.pushLog("You vanish. The bag stays yours.");
      this.returnPending();
      this.audio?.spell();
      this.endBattle(false);
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "potion") {
      const hp = this.potHp;
      const mana = this.potMana;
      this.usePotion(true);
      if (this.potHp === hp && this.potMana === mana) return;
      this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    const focus = this.equip.off?.active;
    if (focus && kind === focus) {
      if (this.cls === "vampire") this.hp = Math.max(1, this.hp - 2);
      else {
        if (this.energy < 2) {
          this.pushLog("Not enough mana.");
          return;
        }
        this.energy -= 2;
      }
      const living = b.foes.filter((f) => f.hp > 0);
      if (!living.length) {
        this.winBattle();
        return;
      }
      let dmg = 4 + Math.floor(this.level / 3) + (this.equip.off?.atk ?? 0) + (this.equip.off?.bonus ?? 0);
      const aoe = kind === "Lantern Flare";
      if (kind === "Star Bolt") dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.45);
      if (kind === "Skull Hex") b.poison = Math.max(b.poison, 4);
      if (kind === "Page Bolt" && this.cls !== "vampire") this.energy = Math.min(this.maxEnergy, this.energy + 2);
      const victims = aoe ? b.foes : living.slice(0, 1);
      for (const f of victims) {
        if (f.hp <= 0) continue;
        f.hp -= dmg;
        this.pushLog(`${kind} hits ${f.name} for ${dmg}.`);
      }
      this.audio?.spell();
      if (b.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) this.winBattle();
      else this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    const specials = new Set(CLASSES[this.cls].specials);
    const isSpecial = specials.has(kind);
    if (isSpecial && !this.specials.includes(kind)) {
      this.pushLog("You have not learned that.");
      return;
    }
    if (isSpecial && this.cls !== "vampire") {
      const cost = 3;
      if (this.energy < cost) {
        this.pushLog("Not enough mana.");
        return;
      }
      this.energy -= cost;
    }
    if (isSpecial && this.cls === "vampire") this.hp = Math.max(1, this.hp - 2);
    /** Hold the buff and leave the turn. Damage would stack a swing on top of the shout. */
    if (kind === "Loadout Stance" || kind === "War Cry") {
      if (kind === "Loadout Stance") b.stance = 2 + (this.ranks[kind] ?? 0);
      else b.cry = 2 + (this.ranks[kind] ?? 0);
      this.pushLog(kind === "War Cry" ? "The cry hangs on your next blows." : "You set your feet. The stance holds.");
      this.audio?.spell();
      this.afterHero();
      return;
    }
    const living = b.foes.filter((f) => f.hp > 0);
    if (!living.length) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    const target = living[0];
    let dmg = this.atk + (this.ranks[kind] ?? 0);
    const aoe = kind === "cleave" || kind === "Earthshatter" || kind === "Grave Nova";
    if (kind === "ranged") dmg = Math.round(dmg * 0.85);
    if (kind === "Deathbolt") dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.45);
    if (kind === "Ambush") dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.7);
    if (kind === "Execution" && target.hp < target.max * 0.4) dmg = Math.round(dmg * 2);
    if (kind === "Summon Shade") dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.25);
    const spell = kind === "Deathbolt" || kind === "Grave Nova" || kind === "Curse" || kind === "Summon Shade";
    const crit = Math.random() < 0.01 + this.agi * 0.002;
    let mult = crit ? 2 : 1;
    if (crit && this.equip.main?.special === "Brutal") mult = 3;
    dmg *= mult;
    const apply = (f: Foe) => {
      const shade = f.id === "shade" || f.name === "Death Shade";
      const dealt = shade ? (spell ? dmg : Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 0.6))) : Math.max(1, dmg - f.ac);
      if (f.hp <= 0) return;
      const lethal = dealt >= f.hp;
      if (lethal && f.id === "lich" && b.phylactery === "whole") {
        f.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(f.max * 0.5));
        b.phylactery = "spent";
        this.pushLog("The phylactery pulls the Lich back to half.");
        return;
      }
      f.hp -= dealt;
      if (this.cls === "vampire") this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + Math.ceil(dealt * 0.25));
      this.pushLog(`${mult === 3 ? "Brutal. " : mult === 2 ? "Critical. " : ""}${dealt} on ${f.name}.`);
    };
    if (aoe) b.foes.forEach((f) => apply(f));
    else apply(target);
    if (kind === "Curse" || kind === "Envenom") b.poison = 4;
    if (kind === "Tripwire") b.skipEnemy = true;
    if (spell) this.audio?.spell();
    else this.audio?.hit();
    this.shake = 0.12;
    if (b.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    this.afterHero();
  }

  /** Remember who goes next. The blow's animation finishes before they start. */
  private afterHero() {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) return;
    if (b.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      this.die();
      return;
    }
    b.follow = this.companion && this.companion.hp > 0 ? "ally" : "foes";
  }

  /** Companion art. A ward or a cry refreshes; it does not add to the last one. */
  private allyCommand(kind: string) {
    const b = this.battle;
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!b || !c || c.hp <= 0) {
      this.followFoes();
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "guard") {
      b.allyGuard = 2;
      this.pushLog(`${c.name} covers you.`);
      this.followFoes();
      return;
    }
    if (kind === "cleave" || kind === "ranged") {
      const livingNow = b.foes.filter((f) => f.hp > 0);
      const dmg = (kind === "ranged" ? 2 : 3) + Math.floor(this.level / 4) + Math.floor(this.companionAtk() / 2);
      const victims = kind === "cleave" ? livingNow : livingNow.slice(0, 1);
      for (const f of victims) {
        f.hp -= dmg;
        this.pushLog(`${c.name} ${kind === "cleave" ? "sweeps" : "looses at"} ${f.name} for ${dmg}.`);
      }
      this.audio?.hit();
      if (b.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) this.winBattle();
      else this.followFoes();
      return;
    }
    const art = kind === "strike" ? undefined : this.artNamed(kind);
    if (kind !== "strike" && !art) {
      this.pushLog("They do not know that.");
      return;
    }
    const bonus = this.companionBonus();
    const living = b.foes.filter((f) => f.hp > 0);
    if (!living.length) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    if (!art || art.kind === "heal" || art.kind === "cleanse") {
      if (!art) {
        let dmg = 3 + Math.floor(this.level / 3) + this.companionAtk();
        const crit = Math.random() < 0.01 + this.agi * 0.002;
        if (crit) dmg *= 2;
        living[0].hp -= dmg;
        this.pushLog(`${crit ? "Critical. " : ""}${c.name} strikes ${living[0].name} for ${dmg}.`);
        this.audio?.hit();
      } else {
        const n = art.power + Math.floor(this.level / 3) + bonus;
        this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + n);
        c.hp = Math.min(this.companionMax(), c.hp + Math.ceil(n / 3));
        if (art.kind === "cleanse") b.poison = 0;
        this.pushLog(`${c.name} ${art.kind === "cleanse" ? "veils" : "mends"} you for ${n}.`);
        this.audio?.spell();
      }
    } else if (art.kind === "buff") {
      b.cry = Math.max(b.cry, art.power);
      this.pushLog(`${c.name} blesses your next blows.`);
      this.audio?.spell();
    } else if (art.kind === "ward") {
      b.stance = Math.max(b.stance, art.power);
      this.pushLog(`${c.name} raises a ward.`);
      this.audio?.spell();
    } else if (art.kind === "aggro") {
      b.taunt = art.power;
      const nick = 2 + Math.floor(this.companionAtk() / 2);
      living[0].hp -= nick;
      this.pushLog(`${c.name} pulls every eye and rakes for ${nick}.`);
      this.audio?.spell();
    } else {
      const dmg = art.power + Math.floor(this.level / 4) + Math.floor(bonus / 2) + Math.floor(this.companionAtk() / 2);
      const victims = art.aoe ? living : living.slice(0, 1);
      for (const f of victims) {
        f.hp -= dmg;
        this.pushLog(`${c.name} marks ${f.name} for ${dmg}.`);
        if (art.kind === "drain") {
          const heal = Math.ceil(dmg * 0.5);
          this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + heal);
          c.hp = Math.min(this.companionMax(), c.hp + Math.floor(heal / 2));
        }
      }
      if (art.kind === "curse") b.poison = Math.max(b.poison, 3);
      this.audio?.spell();
    }
    if (b.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) this.winBattle();
    else this.followFoes();
  }

  private companionReact(action: "attack" | "drink") {
    const c = this.companion;
    if (!c || c.hp <= 0) return;
    if (c.focus === "heal" && (action === "drink" || this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.6)) {
      const n = 5 + Math.floor(this.level / 3) + this.companionBonus();
      this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + n);
      this.logLine = `${c.name} mends you.`;
      return;
    }
    if (action === "attack" && this.battle) {
      const f = this.battle.foes.find((foe) => foe.hp > 0);
      if (!f) return;
      const dmg = 2 + Math.floor(this.level / 4) + Math.floor(this.companionAtk() / 2);
      f.hp -= dmg;
      this.pushLog(`${c.name} follows your swing for ${dmg}.`);
      this.cue("ally", "swing", this.battle.foes.indexOf(f), "foes");
    }
  }

  private pushLog(line: string) {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) {
      this.logLine = line;
      return;
    }
    b.log.unshift(line);
    b.log.length = Math.min(6, b.log.length);
    this.logLine = line;
  }

  /** Queue one pose. Later poses in the same turn wait their turn, so a swing is not hidden by the reply. */
  private cue(who: "you" | "ally" | "foe", kind: "swing" | "spell" | "shot" | "heal" | "buff", foe = 0, aim: "you" | "ally" | "foes" = who === "foe" ? "you" : "foes") {
    const span = 420;
    let at = this.worldMs;
    for (const fx of this.battleFx) at = Math.max(at, fx.at + span);
    this.battleFx.push({ who, kind, foe, aim, at });
    this.battleFx = this.battleFx.filter((fx) => fx.at + span > this.worldMs).slice(-8);
  }

  /** Refuse a command that cannot be spent, before the turn starts playing. */
  private afford(kind: string) {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) return false;
    if (b.playerStun) return true;
    if (kind === "Phylactery") return b.phylactery === "whole";
    if (kind === "potion" && this.potHp <= 0 && this.potMana <= 0) {
      this.pushLog("No draughts.");
      return false;
    }
    const focus = this.equip.off?.active;
    if (focus && kind === focus && this.cls !== "vampire" && this.energy < 2) {
      this.pushLog("Not enough mana.");
      return false;
    }
    const specials = new Set(CLASSES[this.cls].specials);
    if (specials.has(kind) && !this.specials.includes(kind)) {
      this.pushLog("You have not learned that.");
      return false;
    }
    if (specials.has(kind) && this.cls !== "vampire" && this.energy < 3) {
      this.pushLog("Not enough mana.");
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** Start the pose now. The numbers wait until the blow connects. */
  private stage(who: "you" | "ally" | "foe", kind: string, foe = 0) {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) return;
    const at = this.worldMs;
    const c = this.companion;
    const aimAlly = who === "foe" && !!c && c.hp > 0 && (b.taunt > 0 || Math.random() < 0.28);
    b.turn = "act";
    b.act = { who, kind, foe, aimAlly, hit: at + 180, until: at + 460, done: false };
    this.cuePose(who, kind, foe, aimAlly);
  }

  private cuePose(who: "you" | "ally" | "foe", kind: string, foe: number, aimAlly: boolean) {
    if (who === "foe") {
      const act = (this.battle?.round ?? 0) % 4;
      const pose = act === 0 ? "swing" : act === 1 ? "shot" : "spell";
      this.cue("foe", pose, foe, aimAlly ? "ally" : "you");
      return;
    }
    if (who === "ally") {
      const art = kind === "strike" ? undefined : this.artNamed(kind);
      if (kind === "guard" || art?.kind === "buff" || art?.kind === "ward") this.cue("ally", "buff", -1, "you");
      else if (!art && kind !== "cleave" && kind !== "ranged") this.cue("ally", "swing", 0, "foes");
      else if (kind === "ranged") this.cue("ally", "shot", 0, "foes");
      else if (kind === "cleave") this.cue("ally", "swing", -1, "foes");
      else if (art?.kind === "heal" || art?.kind === "cleanse") this.cue("ally", "heal", -1, "you");
      else this.cue("ally", "spell", art?.aoe ? -1 : 0, "foes");
      return;
    }
    const focus = this.equip.off?.active;
    const spell = kind === focus || kind === "Deathbolt" || kind === "Grave Nova" || kind === "Curse" || kind === "Summon Shade" || kind === "Tripwire" || kind === "Phylactery";
    const pose = kind === "potion" ? "heal" : kind === "War Cry" || kind === "Loadout Stance" ? "buff" : spell ? "spell" : kind === "ranged" ? "shot" : "swing";
    const aoe = kind === "cleave" || kind === "Earthshatter" || kind === "Grave Nova" || kind === "Lantern Flare";
    this.cue("you", pose, aoe ? -1 : 0, pose === "heal" || pose === "buff" ? "you" : "foes");
  }

  /** Play the current action, then hand the battle to the next actor. */
  private tickBattle() {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b?.act) return;
    if (!b.act.done && this.worldMs >= b.act.hit) {
      b.act.done = true;
      const { who, kind, foe } = b.act;
      if (who === "foe") this.foeStrike(foe);
      else if (who === "ally") this.allyCommand(kind);
      else this.command(kind, true);
    }
    if (!this.battle?.act?.done || this.worldMs < this.battle.act.until) return;
    const who = this.battle.act.who;
    const follow = this.battle.follow;
    this.battle.act = null;
    this.battle.follow = "";
    if (!this.battle) return;
    if (who === "foe") {
      if (this.battle.foeQueue.length) {
        this.stage("foe", "strike", this.battle.foeQueue.shift() ?? 0);
        return;
      }
      this.battle.poison = Math.max(0, this.battle.poison - 1);
      if (this.battle.taunt > 0) this.battle.taunt -= 1;
      if (this.battle.allyGuard > 0) this.battle.allyGuard -= 1;
      if (this.battle.foes.every((f) => f.hp <= 0)) {
        this.winBattle();
        return;
      }
      if (this.hp <= 0) {
        this.die();
        return;
      }
      this.battle.turn = "you";
      return;
    }
    if (follow === "ally" && this.companion && this.companion.hp > 0) {
      this.battle.turn = "ally";
      this.pushLog(`${this.companion.name}'s turn.`);
      return;
    }
    if (follow === "foes") {
      this.startFoeRound();
      return;
    }
    this.battle.turn = "you";
  }

  private followFoes() {
    if (this.battle) this.battle.follow = "foes";
  }

  /** Line up every living foe. Each one plays alone. */
  private startFoeRound() {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) return;
    b.round += 1;
    if (b.skipEnemy) {
      b.skipEnemy = false;
      this.pushLog("They stumble on the wire.");
      b.turn = "you";
      return;
    }
    const living = b.foes.map((f, i) => (f.hp > 0 ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
    if (!living.length) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    b.foeQueue = living.slice(1);
    this.stage("foe", "strike", living[0]);
  }

  /** One foe's action. A cracked phylactery is what keeps the lich down. */
  private foeStrike(index: number) {
    const b = this.battle;
    const f = b?.foes[index];
    if (!b || !f || f.hp <= 0) return;
    if (b.poison > 0 && !f.helper) f.hp -= 2 + Math.floor(this.level / 10);
    if (f.hp <= 0) return;
    if (b.goblin && f.id === "goblin") {
      const chance = 0.2 + 0.15 * b.scrambleFails;
      if (Math.random() < chance) {
        const lost = this.loseUnequipped();
        this.pushLog(lost ? `The goblin scrambles off with ${lost}.` : "The goblin scrambles off with nothing loose.");
        this.endBattle(false);
        return;
      }
      b.scrambleFails += 1;
      this.pushLog("The goblin fails to scramble.");
    }
    const act = b.round % 4;
    const incorporeal = f.id === "shade";
    let raw = f.atk;
    if (act === 1) raw = Math.round(f.atk * 0.9);
    if (act === 2) raw = Math.round(f.atk * 0.8);
    if (f.id === "horseman" && b.round === 1) raw += 4;
    if (f.id === "queen" && act === 2) b.playerStun = true;
    const dealt0 = incorporeal ? raw : Math.max(1, raw - Math.floor(this.ac / 2));
    const c = this.companion;
    const aimAlly = b.act?.aimAlly && !!c && c.hp > 0;
    const label = act === 0 ? "strikes" : act === 1 ? "looses a shot" : act === 2 ? "breaks the ground" : "calls";
    if (aimAlly && c) {
      const dealt = b.allyGuard > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dealt0 * 0.6)) : dealt0;
      c.hp -= dealt;
      this.pushLog(`${f.name} ${label} ${c.name} for ${dealt}.`);
      if (c.hp <= 0) this.pushLog(`${c.name} drops.`);
    } else {
      this.hp -= dealt0;
      this.pushLog(`${f.name} ${label} for ${dealt0}.`);
    }
    this.audio?.hurt();
    if (act === 3 && b.foes.length < 6 && f.boss) {
      const h = scaleMonster(FAMILIES[0], Math.max(1, this.level));
      h.hp = Math.round(h.hp * 0.4);
      h.name = "Risen help";
      b.foes.push({ ...h, hp: h.hp, max: h.hp, helper: true });
      b.foeQueue.push(b.foes.length - 1);
      this.pushLog(`${f.name} pulls something up.`);
    }
  }

  /** Grant xp, silver, and one drop, then check for a level and a boss relic. */
  private winBattle() {
    const b = this.battle;
    if (!b) return;
    let xp = 0;
    let silver = 0;
    for (const f of b.foes) {
      if (f.helper) continue;
      xp += f.xp;
      silver += f.silver;
      if (f.boss && f.id) this.bossDead[f.id] = this.worldMs + 2 * CYCLE_MS;
      if (!this.bestiary.includes(f.family)) this.bestiary.push(f.family);
    }
    if (b.hunter) {
      this.hunterGoneUntil = this.phase === "day" ? nextNight(this.worldMs) : nextDay(this.worldMs);
      this.hunterBanUntil = nextNight(this.worldMs);
      this.barred.inn = this.hunterBanUntil;
      this.barred.casino = this.hunterBanUntil;
    }
    this.xp += xp;
    this.quest = Math.max(this.quest, 1);
    if (silver > 0) this.dropSilver(this.px - 8, this.py + 8, silver);
    if (this.mapId === "world") {
      const zone = biomeName(Math.floor(this.px / TILE), Math.floor(this.py / TILE));
      if (!this.zones.includes(zone)) this.zones.push(zone);
    }
    const drop = this.rollDrop(b);
    if (drop) this.dropItem(this.px + 10, this.py + 6, drop);
    if (b.foes.some((f) => f.boss || f.mini)) this.maybeRelic(b.foes.some((f) => f.boss), this.px, this.py - 8);
    if (b.goblin || b.foes.some((f) => f.boss)) {
      const gem = this.makeGem(Math.random, b.goblin || b.foes.some((f) => f.boss) ? "boss" : "rare", this.level);
      this.dropItem(this.px + 18, this.py + 4, gem);
    }
    this.pending = null;
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.battle = null;
    this.calm = 2.4;
    if (this.mapId === "world") this.placeBosses();
    this.logLine = `Victory. ${xp} experience. The silver is on the ground.`;
    this.checkLevel();
  }

  private endBattle(victory: boolean) {
    if (victory) {
      this.winBattle();
      return;
    }
    this.battle = null;
    this.calm = 2.2;
    this.mode = "play";
  }

  private returnPending() {
    if (!this.pending || this.mapId === "town") {
      this.pending = null;
      return;
    }
    const spot = this.openNear(this.px + 40, this.py + 8);
    this.pending.x = spot.x;
    this.pending.y = spot.y;
    this.roamers.push(this.pending);
    this.pending = null;
  }

  private rollDrop(b: Battle): Item | null {
    if (b.goblin) {
      const rank = Math.random() < 0.25 ? 5 : 4;
      return makeItem({
        name: rank === 5 ? "Goblin's red prize" : "Goblin's gold prize",
        kind: "weapon",
        rank,
        slot: "main",
        atk: 8 + Math.floor(this.level / 2),
        special: rank === 5 ? "Grave spark" : undefined,
      });
    }
    const boss = b.foes.some((f) => f.boss);
    const mini = b.foes.some((f) => f.mini);
    const source = boss ? "boss" : mini ? "rare" : "common";
    if (!boss && Math.random() < 0.35) return makeItem({ name: "Tattered rag", kind: "junk", rank: 1, stack: 1 });
    return this.makeDrop(source, boss);
  }

  private makeDrop(source: string, boss: boolean): Item {
    let rank = 1;
    if (source === "boss") rank = Math.random() < 0.12 ? 5 : Math.random() < 0.4 ? 4 : 3;
    else if (source === "rare") rank = Math.random() < 0.22 ? 3 : 2;
    else if (source === "chest") rank = Math.random() < 0.45 ? 3 : 2;
    else if (source === "world") rank = Math.random() < 0.2 ? 2 : 1;
    else rank = Math.random() < 0.12 ? 2 : 1;
    const lv = this.mapId === "dungeon" ? this.dungeonLevel() : this.level;
    const armor = this.dungeon === "grave" ? "chain" : CLASSES[this.cls].armor!;
    if (!boss && Math.random() < 0.25) return this.makeGem(Math.random, source === "rare" || source === "boss" ? "rare" : "normal", lv);
    if (!boss && Math.random() < 0.18) {
      const mana = Math.random() < 0.5;
      return makeItem({ name: mana ? "Spirit draught" : "Health draught", kind: "potion", rank: 1, bonus: mana ? 2 : 1 });
    }
    const r = asRank(rank);
    const asArmor = this.dungeon === "hearth" ? Math.random() < 0.6 : Math.random() < 0.5;
    if (asArmor) {
      const slots = ["head", "chest", "legs", "feet"] as const;
      const slot = slots[Math.floor(Math.random() * slots.length)];
      const set = rank === 4 ? SET_NAME[armor] : undefined;
      return makeItem({
        name: set ? `${set} ${slot}` : `${armor} ${slot}`,
        kind: "armor",
        rank: r,
        slot,
        armor,
        ac: rank + 1 + Math.floor(lv / 8),
        set,
        special: rank >= 4 ? "Socket" : undefined,
      });
    }
    return makeItem({
      name: boss ? "Boss relic" : "Found blade",
      kind: "weapon",
      rank: r,
      slot: "main",
      atk: 3 + rank * 2 + Math.floor(lv / 4),
      special: rank === 5 ? "Grave spark" : rank >= 4 ? "Socket" : undefined,
    });
  }

  private makeGem(rng: () => number, source: "normal" | "rare" | "boss", level: number): Item {
    const stones = ["Diamond", "Emerald", "Ruby", "Sapphire", "Amethyst", "Amber"];
    const grades = ["Rough", "Cut", "Polished", "Pristine", "Perfect"];
    const pool = source === "normal" ? [0, 1, 2, 4] : [0, 1, 2, 3, 4];
    const g = pool[Math.floor(rng() * pool.length)];
    const band = 1 + Math.floor(level / 5);
    return makeItem({
      name: `${grades[g]} ${stones[Math.floor(rng() * stones.length)]}`,
      kind: "gem",
      rank: asRank(Math.min(5, 1 + Math.floor(g / 1.2))),
      bonus: band,
      special: grades[g],
    });
  }

  private gainItem(it: Item): boolean {
    if (it.kind === "potion") {
      const pool = this.cls === "vampire" ? "hp" : (it.bonus ?? 1) === 2 ? "mana" : "hp";
      const low = pool === "mana" ? this.energy < this.maxEnergy * 0.5 : this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.5;
      if (low && this.mode === "battle" && !this.potionsCorked()) {
        if (pool === "mana") this.energy = Math.min(this.maxEnergy, this.energy + Math.round(this.maxEnergy * 0.35));
        else this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + Math.round(this.maxHp * 0.35));
        this.logLine = "You drink the draught on the spot.";
        return true;
      }
      if ((it.bonus ?? 1) === 2 && this.cls !== "vampire") this.potMana += 1;
      else this.potHp += 1;
      return true;
    }
    if (it.kind === "junk" || it.kind === "bait" || it.kind === "fish") {
      const stack = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === it.kind && i.name === it.name);
      if (stack) {
        stack.stack = (stack.stack ?? 1) + (it.stack ?? 1);
        return true;
      }
    }
    if (this.inv.length >= 20) {
      this.logLine = "The pack is full.";
      return false;
    }
    this.inv.push(it);
    return true;
  }

  private loseUnequipped(): string | null {
    const pool = this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind !== "potion");
    if (!pool.length) {
      const n = Math.min(this.coin, 6);
      this.coin -= n;
      return n ? `${n} silver` : null;
    }
    const it = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
    if (it.kind === "junk" && (it.stack ?? 1) > 1) {
      it.stack = (it.stack ?? 1) - 1;
      return it.name;
    }
    this.inv = this.inv.filter((i) => i.uid !== it.uid);
    return it.name;
  }

  private loseSomething() {
    const junk = this.inv.find((i) => i.kind === "junk");
    if (junk) this.loseUnequipped();
    else this.coin = Math.max(0, this.coin - 6);
  }

  usePotion(inBattle = false) {
    if (this.potionsCorked()) {
      this.logLine = "Corked. The curse holds every flask shut on this floor.";
      return;
    }
    const needMana = this.cls !== "vampire" && this.energy < this.maxEnergy * 0.5 && this.potMana > 0 && this.hp >= this.maxHp * 0.5;
    if (needMana) {
      this.potMana -= 1;
      this.energy = Math.min(this.maxEnergy, this.energy + Math.round(this.maxEnergy * 0.4));
      this.logLine = "The spirit draught settles.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.potHp <= 0) {
      this.logLine = "No draughts.";
      return;
    }
    if (!inBattle && this.hp >= this.maxHp * 0.5 && this.mode === "play") {
      this.logLine = "You are not hurt enough. The draught stays corked.";
      return;
    }
    this.potHp -= 1;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + Math.round(this.maxHp * 0.4));
    this.fx.potion(this.px, this.py - 14);
    this.logLine = "The draught burns, then helps.";
    if (!inBattle) this.companionReact("drink");
  }

  spendPoints() {
    if (this.points < 20) {
      this.logLine = "The back shelf wants 20 points.";
      return;
    }
    this.points -= 20;
    const roll = Math.random();
    const rank = roll < 0.08 ? 5 : roll < 0.23 ? 4 : 3;
    this.gainItem(
      makeItem({
        name: "House prize",
        kind: "weapon",
        rank: asRank(rank),
        slot: "main",
        atk: 6 + rank * 2 + Math.floor(this.level / 3),
        special: rank === 5 ? "Grave spark" : undefined,
      }),
    );
    this.logLine = "The points merchant slides a prize across.";
  }

  private die() {
    this.returnPending();
    this.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(this.maxHp / 3));
    this.battle = null;
    this.mode = "dead";
    this.logLine = this.cls === "vampire" ? "You wake among the graves. Nothing was taken." : "You wake under the church bell. Nothing was taken.";
  }

  revive() {
    if (this.cls === "vampire") this.enterTown(14 * TILE + 8, 19 * TILE);
    else this.enterTown(5 * TILE + 8, 19 * TILE);
    this.mode = "play";
    this.hp = Math.max(this.hp, Math.round(this.maxHp / 3));
  }

  camp() {
    if (this.mapId === "camp") {
      // playtest1: breaking camp asks first.
      this.askLeaveCamp();
      return;
    }
    if (this.mapId !== "world" || this.mode === "battle" || this.mode === "dead" || this.fallen > 0) {
      this.logLine = "Camp only in the wild, and not in a fight.";
      return;
    }
    // Item 11 tier 3 (Fireside): half instead of a third. Out of a fight only; no combat number moves.
    const share = this.bondHas("fireside") ? BOND.campShare : 1 / 3;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + Math.round(this.maxHp * share));
    this.energy = Math.min(this.maxEnergy, this.energy + Math.round(this.maxEnergy * share));
    if (this.companion) this.companion.hp = this.companionMax();
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const chance = goblinChance(tx, ty);
    if (Math.random() < chance) {
      const g = scaleMonster(monsterById("goblin"), Math.max(3, this.level));
      this.openBattle([{ ...g, id: "goblin", hp: g.hp, max: g.hp }], { guard: false, goblin: true, hunter: false, fromRole: "" });
      this.logLine = "Something is in the packs.";
      return;
    }
    this.enterCamp();
  }

  meditate() {
    if (this.mode !== "crypt" && this.mapId !== "camp") return;
    this.leaveCamp();
  }

  /** A small clearing. The south mark breaks camp and puts you back where you were. */
  enterCamp(keep = false) {
    if (!keep) {
      this.campX = this.px;
      this.campY = this.py;
    }
    const built = buildCamp();
    this.mapId = "camp";
    this.inside = "";
    this.theme = "camp";
    this.fog = null;
    this.w = built.w;
    this.h = built.h;
    this.tiles = built.tiles;
    this.npcs = [];
    this.roamers = [];
    this.entrances = {};
    this.critters = [];
    this.px = 8 * TILE + 8;
    this.py = 8 * TILE + 8;
    this.facing = "s";
    this.doorCool = 0.45;
    if (this.companion) {
      this.companion.x = 11 * TILE + 8;
      this.companion.y = 8 * TILE + 8;
      this.campGoal = null;
      this.campWalk = 0;
    }
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = this.companion
      ? "The tent is up. The fire holds. Talk to them, then take the south mark when you mean to leave."
      : "The tent is up. The fire holds. The south mark leads back to the road.";
  }

  /** playtest1b: feet on the camp's south mark, or below its row within a tile of it (the trees push you there). */
  private onCampMark(): boolean {
    const i = this.tiles.indexOf(T.door);
    if (i < 0) return false;
    const mx = i % this.w;
    const my = (i - mx) / this.w;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    return (tx === mx && ty === my) || (ty > my && Math.abs(tx - mx) <= 1);
  }

  /** playtest1b: the HUD's camp button is lit where it works: in the wild out of a fight, or in camp to break it. */
  canCamp(): boolean {
    if (this.mapId === "camp") return true;
    return this.mapId === "world" && this.mode !== "battle" && this.mode !== "dead" && this.fallen <= 0; // camp()'s own test
  }

  /** playtest1 (owner-reported 2026-10-01): breaking camp, or walking onto its south mark, asks "Leave camp?". */
  askLeaveCamp() {
    if (this.mapId !== "camp" || this.mode !== "play") return;
    this.askLeave = true;
    this.held.clear();
    this.logLine = "Leave camp?";
  }

  /** Yes breaks camp as before. No keeps you by the fire, a step back from the south mark. */
  answerLeave(yes: boolean) {
    if (!this.askLeave) return;
    this.askLeave = false;
    if (yes) {
      this.leaveCamp();
      return;
    }
    const tx = Math.floor(this.px / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor(this.py / TILE);
    if (this.tiles[ty * this.w + tx] === T.door) {
      this.py = (ty - 1) * TILE + 8;
      this.facing = "n";
    } else if (this.onCampMark()) {
      // playtest1b: slipped past the mark into the trees: back to the clearing above it
      const i = this.tiles.indexOf(T.door);
      this.px = (i % this.w) * TILE + 8;
      this.py = (Math.floor(i / this.w) - 1) * TILE + 8;
      this.facing = "n";
    }
    this.doorCool = 0.45;
    this.logLine = "You stay by the fire.";
  }

  leaveCamp() {
    const x = this.campX || GATE.x * TILE + 8;
    const y = this.campY || GATE.y * TILE + 8;
    this.enterWorld(x, y);
    this.mode = "play";
    this.logLine = "You break camp. The road is where you left it.";
  }

  turnInPage() {
    if (this.bestiary.length < 20) {
      this.logLine = "The page wants 20 kinds.";
      return;
    }
    if (this.pagePaid) {
      this.logLine = "The guild already paid for that page.";
      return;
    }
    this.pagePaid = true;
    this.coin += 40;
    this.audio?.coin();
    this.logLine = "The guild pays 40 silver for the page.";
  }

  private maybeRelic(boss: boolean, x: number, y: number) {
    if (Math.random() > (boss ? 0.22 : 0.1)) return;
    const relics = Object.values(RELIC_FOR);
    const owned = new Set(this.inv.filter((i) => i.kind === "relic").map((i) => i.name));
    if (this.companion) {
      const held = Object.values(RELIC_FOR).find((r) => r.who === this.companion?.name || r.kit === this.companion?.kit);
      if (held) owned.add(held.name);
    }
    const left = relics.filter((r) => !owned.has(r.name));
    if (!left.length) return;
    const relic = left[Math.floor(Math.random() * left.length)];
    this.dropItem(x, y, makeItem({ name: relic.name, kind: "relic", rank: 4, special: relic.kit }));
    this.logLine = `${relic.name} is on the ground.`;
  }

  private dropHere(x: number, y: number) {
    let px = x;
    let py = y;
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const piled = this.drops.some((d) => this.sameMap(d) && Math.hypot(d.x - px, d.y - py) < 10);
      if (!piled && !this.solidAt(px, py)) return { x: px, y: py };
      px += 12;
      py += i % 2 ? 8 : -8;
    }
    return { x, y };
  }

  private sameMap(d: { mapId: string; dungeon: string; floor: number; inside: string }) {
    return d.mapId === this.mapId && d.dungeon === this.dungeon && d.floor === this.floor && d.inside === (this.inside || "");
  }

  private dropSilver(x: number, y: number, n: number) {
    if (n <= 0) return;
    const spot = this.dropHere(x, y);
    this.drops.push({ ...spot, mapId: this.mapId, dungeon: this.dungeon, floor: this.floor, inside: this.inside || "", age: 0, silver: n, item: null, told: false });
  }

  private dropItem(x: number, y: number, it: Item) {
    const spot = this.dropHere(x, y);
    this.drops.push({ ...spot, mapId: this.mapId, dungeon: this.dungeon, floor: this.floor, inside: this.inside || "", age: 0, silver: 0, item: it, told: false });
  }

  /** Pick up what you are standing on. Fresh drops wait a moment so the icon can be seen. */
  private tickDrops(dt: number) {
    for (const d of [...this.drops]) {
      if (!this.sameMap(d)) continue;
      d.age += dt;
      if (d.age < 0.35) continue;
      // Item 11 tier 2 (Forager): the companion's feet pick up too, with the same reach as yours.
      const c = this.companion;
      const foraged = !!c && c.hp > 0 && this.bondHas("forager") && Math.hypot(d.x - c.x, d.y - c.y) <= BOND.forageReach;
      if (Math.hypot(d.x - this.px, d.y - this.py) > 12 && !foraged) continue;
      if (d.silver > 0) {
        this.coin += d.silver;
        this.drops = this.drops.filter((o) => o !== d);
        this.logLine = `${d.silver} silver.`;
        this.audio?.coin();
        continue;
      }
      if (!d.item) continue;
      if (this.gainItem(d.item)) {
        this.drops = this.drops.filter((o) => o !== d);
        this.logLine = d.item.name;
        this.audio?.coin();
      } else if (!d.told) {
        d.told = true;
        this.logLine = "The pack is full. It stays on the ground.";
      }
    }
  }

  /** One roulette spin. Green is the long shot. The house does not track a streak. */
  bet(color: "red" | "black" | "green", stake: number) {
    const bet = Math.floor(stake);
    if (bet < 1 || this.coin < bet) {
      this.logLine = "Not enough silver for that stake.";
      return;
    }
    this.coin -= bet;
    const roll = Math.random();
    const landed = roll < 0.473 ? "red" : roll < 0.946 ? "black" : "green";
    if (landed === color) {
      this.coin += color === "green" ? bet * 15 : bet * 2;
      this.points += Math.max(1, Math.ceil(bet * 0.1));
      this.logLine = color === "green" ? `${landed}. Fourteen to one.` : `${landed}. Even money.`;
    } else {
      this.points += Math.max(1, Math.ceil(bet * 0.05));
      this.logLine = `${landed}. The house keeps the stake.`;
    }
    this.audio?.coin();
  }

  /** Write this life into one of the three local slots. */
  saveSlot(slot: number) {
    const all = this.readSaves();
    all[slot] = this.saveRecord();
    localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(all));
    this.logLine = `Saved in slot ${slot + 1}.`;
  }

  /**
   * playtest1 (owner-reported 2026-10-01, the Android edge swipe lost progress): this life as one save record, the
   * same SaveBlob a slot holds (one shape, so an autosave and a slot load through the same loadRecord).
   */
  saveRecord(): SaveBlob {
    return {
      cls: this.cls,
      path: this.path,
      name: this.name,
      level: this.level,
      xp: this.xp,
      str: this.str,
      dex: this.dex,
      int: this.int,
      sta: this.sta,
      agi: this.agi,
      fort: this.fort,
      hp: this.hp,
      energy: this.energy,
      coin: this.coin,
      bank: this.bank,
      points: this.points,
      potHp: this.potHp,
      potMana: this.potMana,
      specials: [...this.specials],
      ranks: { ...this.ranks },
      worldMs: this.worldMs,
      // Map writer: a save inside a rift zone is written at the rift's return spot (no new save field).
      mapId: this.riftSaveSpot()?.mapId ?? this.mapId,
      px: this.riftSaveSpot()?.px ?? this.px,
      py: this.riftSaveSpot()?.py ?? this.py,
      quest: this.quest,
      bossDead: { ...this.bossDead },
      dungeon: this.riftSaveSpot() ? "" : this.dungeon,
      floor: this.riftSaveSpot() ? 0 : this.floor,
      inv: this.inv.map((i) => ({ ...i })),
      equip: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(this.equip).map(([k, v]) => [k, v ? { ...v } : v])),
      visited: [...this.visited],
      bestiary: [...this.bestiary],
      portal: this.portal ? { ...this.portal } : null,
      rentedUntil: this.rentedUntil,
      hunterBanUntil: this.hunterBanUntil,
      hunterGoneUntil: this.hunterGoneUntil,
      barred: { ...this.barred },
      blessing: this.blessing,
      fed: { ...this.fed },
      opened: [...this.opened],
      entryLevel: this.entryLevel,
      companion: this.companion
        ? { ...this.companion, equip: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(this.companion.equip).map(([k, v]) => [k, v ? { ...v } : v])) }
        : null,
      favor: { ...this.favor },
      trickDay: { ...this.trickDay },
      fishPoints: this.fishPoints,
      inside: this.inside,
      vault: this.vault.map((i) => ({ ...i })),
      ownedHome: this.ownedHome,
      zones: [...this.zones],
      paidZones: [...this.paidZones],
      fishStep: this.fishStep,
      remnantId: this.remnantId,
      pagePaid: this.pagePaid,
      campX: this.campX,
      campY: this.campY,
      worldV: WORLD.scale,
      drops: this.drops.map((d) => ({ ...d, item: d.item ? { ...d.item } : null })),
    };
  }

  /**
   * playtest1: the autosave, its own key beside the three slots (the slots are never touched by it). live marks a
   * page that went away mid-play (hidden, swiped off, closed), so the next load resumes it at once. Never written
   * on the title, at death or while fallen. Returns whether it wrote.
   */
  autosave(live: boolean): boolean {
    if (this.mode === "title" || this.mode === "dead" || this.fallen > 0) return false;
    try {
      const auto: AutoSave = { v: 1, at: Date.now(), live, rec: this.saveRecord() };
      localStorage.setItem(AUTO_KEY, JSON.stringify(auto));
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /** playtest1: the autosave, if there is a readable one. */
  readAuto(): AutoSave | null {
    try {
      const a = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(AUTO_KEY) || "null") as AutoSave | null;
      return a && a.v === 1 && a.rec && typeof a.rec.cls === "string" ? a : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  autoBrief() {
    const a = this.readAuto();
    return a ? `${a.rec.name} · ${CLASSES[a.rec.cls]?.label ?? a.rec.cls} · Lv ${a.rec.level}` : "";
  }

  /** playtest1: pick up exactly where the autosave left off (the same restore a slot uses). */
  resumeAuto(): boolean {
    const a = this.readAuto();
    if (!a) return false;
    this.loadRecord(a.rec);
    this.logLine = "Back where you left off.";
    return true;
  }

  /** Restore a slot and rebuild the map under the saved pixel. */
  loadSlot(slot: number) {
    const raw = this.readSaves()[slot];
    if (!raw) return;
    this.loadRecord(raw);
  }

  /** Restore one save record (a slot's, or the autosave's) and rebuild the map under the saved pixel. */
  loadRecord(record: SaveBlob) {
    this.resetOrders(); // playtest1k: orders and combo pops are never saved; a loaded game follows
    const raw = migrateWorldSave(record);
    this.cls = raw.cls;
    this.path = raw.path;
    this.name = raw.name;
    this.level = raw.level;
    this.xp = raw.xp;
    this.str = raw.str;
    this.dex = raw.dex;
    this.int = raw.int;
    this.sta = raw.sta;
    this.agi = raw.agi;
    this.fort = raw.fort;
    this.coin = raw.coin;
    this.bank = raw.bank ?? 0;
    this.points = raw.points ?? 0;
    this.fishPoints = raw.fishPoints ?? 0;
    this.vault = raw.vault ?? [];
    this.ownedHome = !!raw.ownedHome;
    this.zones = raw.zones ?? [];
    this.paidZones = raw.paidZones ?? [];
    this.fishStep = raw.fishStep ?? 0;
    this.remnantId = raw.remnantId ?? "";
    this.pagePaid = !!raw.pagePaid;
    this.campX = raw.campX ?? 0;
    this.campY = raw.campY ?? 0;
    this.potHp = raw.potHp;
    this.potMana = raw.potMana;
    this.specials = raw.specials ?? [];
    this.ranks = raw.ranks ?? {};
    this.worldMs = raw.worldMs;
    this.quest = raw.quest ?? 0;
    this.bossDead = raw.bossDead ?? {};
    this.inv = raw.inv ?? [];
    this.equip = raw.equip ?? {};
    this.visited = raw.visited ?? [];
    this.bestiary = raw.bestiary ?? [];
    this.portal = raw.portal ?? null;
    this.rentedUntil = raw.rentedUntil ?? 0;
    this.hunterBanUntil = raw.hunterBanUntil ?? 0;
    this.hunterGoneUntil = raw.hunterGoneUntil ?? 0;
    this.barred = raw.barred ?? {};
    this.blessing = raw.blessing ?? 0;
    this.fed = raw.fed ?? {};
    this.opened = new Set(raw.opened ?? []);
    if (raw.mapId !== "dungeon") for (const k of [...this.opened]) if (k.startsWith("escort:")) this.opened.delete(k);
    this.entryLevel = raw.entryLevel ?? raw.level;
    this.companion = raw.companion ?? null;
    this.favor = raw.favor ?? {};
    this.trickDay = raw.trickDay ?? {};
    this.folkKey = "";
    this.floors = {};
    this.riftReturn = null;
    this.gen = null;
    this.battle = null;
    this.talk = null;
    this.travel = null; // playtest1f: a journey is never saved, and a load never lands mid-swirl
    this.wayHold = "";
    this.hp = Math.min(raw.hp, this.maxHp);
    this.energy = this.cls === "vampire" ? 0 : Math.min(raw.energy, this.maxEnergy);
    if (raw.mapId === "world") {
      this.enterWorld(raw.px, raw.py);
      if (raw !== record) this.landMigrated();
      // A save made inside a rift sits on its mouth: hold the mouth until you step off, as on the way out.
      this.riftHold = `${Math.floor(raw.px / TILE)},${Math.floor(raw.py / TILE)}`;
    } else if (raw.mapId === "camp") this.enterCamp(true);
    else if (raw.mapId === "inside" && raw.inside) {
      this.enterInside(raw.inside, true);
      this.px = raw.px;
      this.py = raw.py;
    } else if (raw.mapId === "dungeon" && raw.dungeon) {
      this.dungeon = raw.dungeon;
      this.floor = raw.floor || 1;
      this.loadFloor("start");
      this.px = raw.px;
      this.py = raw.py;
      this.unstick(); // playtest1n: never inside a room prop
    } else this.enterTown(raw.px, raw.py);
    this.mode = "play";
    this.drops = raw.drops ?? [];
    this.logLine = "The night remembers you.";
  }

  private readSaves(): Array<SaveBlob | null> {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY) || "[]") as Array<SaveBlob | null>;
      return [parsed[0] ?? null, parsed[1] ?? null, parsed[2] ?? null];
    } catch {
      return emptySaves();
    }
  }

  hasSave(slot: number) {
    return !!this.readSaves()[slot];
  }
}

/**
 * playtest1e [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1e bigger world]: a save from before the twice-size vale keeps its
 * place. Every world pixel it holds (the hero on the vale, the camp's return spot, a town portal's anchor, drops lying
 * on the vale) is doubled, the way playtest1a moved town saves onto the new town. loadRecord then steps a hero who
 * lands in a tree, rock, water or wall to the nearest open tile (landMigrated). Pure: the record passed in is not changed.
 */
export function migrateWorldSave(raw: SaveBlob): SaveBlob {
  if ((raw.worldV ?? 1) >= WORLD.scale) return raw;
  const k = WORLD.scale;
  const fit = (v: number, n: number) => Math.max(TILE + 8, Math.min(n * TILE - TILE - 8, Math.round(v * k)));
  const out: SaveBlob = { ...raw, worldV: k };
  if (raw.mapId === "world") {
    out.px = fit(raw.px, WORLD.w);
    out.py = fit(raw.py, WORLD.h);
  }
  if (raw.campX) out.campX = fit(raw.campX, WORLD.w);
  if (raw.campY) out.campY = fit(raw.campY, WORLD.h);
  if (raw.portal && raw.portal.mapId === "world") out.portal = { ...raw.portal, px: fit(raw.portal.px, WORLD.w), py: fit(raw.portal.py, WORLD.h) };
  if (raw.drops) out.drops = raw.drops.map((d) => (d.mapId === "world" ? { ...d, x: fit(d.x, WORLD.w), y: fit(d.y, WORLD.h) } : d));
  return out;
}

/** World bosses and where they stand. A slain one leaves its remnant here for two day-night cycles. */
const WORLD_BOSSES = [
  { id: "bride", tx: 32, ty: 84, lv: 14 },
  { id: "shade", tx: 28, ty: 72, lv: 18 },
  { id: "saint", tx: 120, ty: 44, lv: 28 },
  { id: "mummyboss", tx: 116, ty: 60, lv: 36 },
  { id: "ashmare", tx: 96, ty: 104, lv: 38 },
  { id: "wolfman", tx: 64, ty: 12, lv: 52 },
  { id: "queen", tx: 24, ty: 42, lv: 58 },
  { id: "tzar", tx: 76, ty: 64, lv: 72 },
  { id: "leech", tx: 54, ty: 60, lv: 78 },
];

/** Loot Goblin odds by biome: the camp's numbers, shared with a rift's possible goblin. */
// playtest1e [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1e bigger world]: the zone rule is content.ts worldBiome (the old
// rectangles at twice the size); distances from the town gate count in old tiles (WORLD.scale), so the level bands
// cover the same share of the bigger vale.
function goblinChance(tx: number, ty: number) {
  const z = worldBiome(tx, ty);
  return z === "swamp" ? 0.15 : z === "vale" ? 0.08 : 0.12;
}

function zoneLevel(tx: number, ty: number, player: number) {
  const dist = Math.hypot(tx - GATE.x, ty - GATE.y) / WORLD.scale;
  const band = dist < 8 ? 0 : dist < 16 ? 2 : dist < 26 ? 5 : 8;
  const biome = { snow: 4, sand: 3, ash: 3, swamp: 6, vale: 1 }[worldBiome(tx, ty)];
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(player * 0.35 + band + biome));
}

function biomeName(tx: number, ty: number) {
  return { snow: "Winter hollow", sand: "Dry waste", ash: "Cinder", swamp: "Scourge swamp", vale: "Decayed vale" }[worldBiome(tx, ty)];
}

function familiesFor(tx: number, ty: number, mapId: string): MonsterDef[] {
  if (mapId === "dungeon") return FAMILIES;
  // The camp and the town keep the old rule on their own small grids.
  const z = mapId === "world" ? worldBiome(tx, ty) : planBiome(tx, ty);
  if (z === "snow") return FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id === "wolf" || m.id === "ghost" || m.id === "bat");
  if (z === "sand") return FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id === "mummy" || m.id === "skeleton" || m.id === "ghoul");
  if (z === "ash") return FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id === "pumpkin" || m.id === "scare" || m.id === "witch");
  if (z === "swamp") return FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id === "witch" || m.id === "tree" || m.id === "zombie");
  return FAMILIES.filter((m) => m.id === "zombie" || m.id === "skeleton" || m.id === "ghost" || m.id === "bat");
}
