function findTile(tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, w: number, tile: number) {
  for (let i = 0; i < tiles.length; i++) if (tiles[i] === tile) return { x: i % w, y: Math.floor(i / w) };
  return null;
}

function findOpen(tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, w: number, h: number, preferX: number, skip?: number[]) {
  let best = { x: 2, y: 2, score: -1 };
  const off = new Set(skip ?? []);
  for (let y = 1; y < h - 1; y++) {
    for (let x = 1; x < w - 1; x++) {
      if (tiles[y * w + x] !== T.floor && tiles[y * w + x] !== T.road) continue;
      if (off.has(y * w + x)) continue;
      const score = x >= preferX ? x : x / 10;
      if (score > best.score) best = { x, y, score };
    }
  }
  return best;
}

function stepOff(tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, w: number, h: number, x: number, y: number) {
  const options = [
    [x, y + 1],
    [x, y - 1],
    [x + 1, y],
    [x - 1, y],
  ];
  for (const [sx, sy] of options) {
    if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= w || sy >= h) continue;
    const t = tiles[sy * w + sx];
    if (t !== T.wall && t !== T.water && t !== T.pool && t !== T.stairD && t !== T.stairU && !secretTile(t)) return { x: sx, y: sy };
  }
  return { x, y };
}

/**
 * Stamp the town. Roads first, then houses, furnishings, the pond, and the people.
 * A person standing on a wall tile is a bug: feet need a full tile of floor or cobble.
 */
type RoomDef = {

  name: string;
  kind: string;
  doorX: number;
  doorY: number;
  outX: number;
  outY: number;
  home?: string;
  who?: string;
};

  { id: "alchemy", name: "The Cauldron", kind: "alchemy", doorX: 27, doorY: 26, outX: 27, outY: 27 },
  { id: "mystic", name: "The Moon and Star", kind: "mystic", doorX: 34, doorY: 26, outX: 34, outY: 27 },
];

function roomByDoor(x: number, y: number) {
  return ROOMS.find((r) => r.doorX === x && r.doorY === y);
}

/** playtest1b: the room behind a town door (its trade and its name), for the signs. Picture only. */
/**
 * [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02 Bill] mana change (playtest1b): max MP x2.5 and MP regen x3 for every mana class, so a
 * caster gets about 6-8 casts from full and refills noticeably faster. Spell costs and damage are not touched.
 */
export const MANA = { pool: 2.5, regen: 3 } as const;

/** playtest1b: the log line on stepping into water, and the one that replaces it on climbing out. */
export const WADE_LINE = "You sink in. Swimming is half speed.";
export const DRY_LINE = "You climb out onto dry ground.";

export function townRoomAt(x: number, y: number): { id: string; name: string; kind: string } | undefined {
  const r = roomByDoor(x, y);
  return r ? { id: r.id, name: r.name, kind: r.kind } : undefined;
}

function buildRoom(kind: string) {
  const w = 14;
  const h = 11;
  const tiles = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  tiles.fill(T.wall);
  for (let y = 1; y < h - 1; y++) for (let x = 1; x < w - 1; x++) tiles[y * w + x] = T.floor;
  tiles[(h - 1) * w + 7] = T.door;
  const set = (x: number, y: number, t: number) => {
    tiles[y * w + x] = t;
  };
  if (kind === "shop" || kind === "guild" || kind === "bank" || kind === "tailor") {
    set(2, 2, T.shelf);
    set(3, 2, T.shelf);
    set(10, 2, T.shelf);
    set(11, 2, T.shelf);
  }
  if (kind === "chapel") set(7, 2, T.hearth);
  if (kind === "alchemy") {
    set(7, 3, T.cauldron);
    set(2, 2, T.shelf);
    set(11, 2, T.shelf);
  }
  if (kind === "mystic") {
    set(7, 3, T.moon);
    set(2, 2, T.shelf);
    set(11, 2, T.shelf);
  }
  if (kind === "fisher") {
    set(3, 3, T.nets);
    set(4, 3, T.nets);
    set(10, 3, T.nets);
  }
  if (kind === "croft") set(4, 6, T.chest);
  return { w, h, tiles };
}

function buildCamp() {
  const w = 16;
  const h = 12;
  const tiles = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  tiles.fill(T.grass);
  const set = (x: number, y: number, t: number) => {
    if (x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < w && y < h) tiles[y * w + x] = t;
  };
  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
    set(x, 0, T.tree);
    set(x, h - 1, T.tree);
  }
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    set(0, y, T.tree);
    set(w - 1, y, T.tree);
  }
  for (let y = 5; y <= 9; y++) for (let x = 4; x <= 12; x++) set(x, y, T.dirt);
  for (let y = 2; y <= 5; y++) for (let x = 3; x <= 7; x++) set(x, y, T.wall);
  set(5, 5, T.dirt);
  set(11, 7, T.hearth);
  set(8, 10, T.door);
  return { w, h, tiles };
}

/** Skeleton kin throw bone chips instead of rust. Picture only. */
function boneKin(r: Roamer) {
  return r.family === "skeleton" || r.family === "lich" || r.def === "lich" || r.def === "saint" || r.def === "abbot";
}

/** playtest1: a drawn spell's time on screen, its flight then its impact. Picture only. */
export const SPELL_LIFE = 0.6;
/** playtest1: the town is 40 x 30 (two margin rows south of the south houses). */
const TOWN_H = 30;
/**
 * playtest1 (owner-reported bug 2026-10-01): the town is left only through its four road gates, the edge tiles where a
 * road meets the map edge. Everywhere else the edge is a fence. Each gate maps to a side of the vale's town door.
 */
const TOWN_GATES: [number, number][] = [
  [20, 0], [21, 0],
  [20, TOWN_H - 1], [21, TOWN_H - 1],
  [0, 12], [0, 13], [0, 21],
  [39, 12], [39, 13], [39, 21],
];
export type TownSide = "n" | "s" | "w" | "e";
/** Where a gate's road enters the town: the hero's arrival pixel and facing for each side. */
export const TOWN_ARRIVE: Record<TownSide, { x: number; y: number; facing: "n" | "s" | "e" | "w" }> = {
  s: { x: 21 * TILE, y: (TOWN_H - 2) * TILE + 8, facing: "n" },
  n: { x: 21 * TILE, y: 1 * TILE + 8, facing: "s" },
  w: { x: 1 * TILE + 8, y: 13 * TILE, facing: "e" },
  e: { x: 38 * TILE + 8, y: 13 * TILE, facing: "w" },
};
/** The side of town a gate tile is on, or "" for a fence. */
export function townGateSide(tx: number, ty: number): TownSide | "" {
  if (!TOWN_GATES.some(([x, y]) => x === tx && y === ty)) return "";
  if (ty === 0) return "n";
  if (ty === TOWN_H - 1) return "s";
  return tx === 0 ? "w" : "e";
}

function buildTown() {
  const w = 40;
  // playtest1 (owner-reported bug 2026-10-01): two margin rows below the south houses, so their front doors face open ground.
  const h = TOWN_H;
  const tiles = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  tiles.fill(T.grass);
  const set = (x: number, y: number, t: number) => {
    if (x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < w && y < h) tiles[y * w + x] = t;
  };
  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
    set(x, 0, T.exit);
    set(x, h - 1, T.exit);
  }
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    set(0, y, T.exit);
    set(w - 1, y, T.exit);
  }
  const shell = (x: number, y: number, bw: number, bh: number, doorX: number, doorY: number) => {
    for (let yy = y; yy < y + bh; yy++) for (let xx = x; xx < x + bw; xx++) set(xx, yy, T.wall);
    set(doorX, doorY, T.door);
  };
  const stoop = (x: number, y: number) => {
    if (tiles[y * w + x] === T.grass) set(x, y, T.dirt);
  };
  for (let x = 2; x < 38; x++) {
    set(x, 12, T.cobble);
    set(x, 13, T.cobble);
    set(x, 21, T.cobble);
  }
  for (let y = 1; y < 27; y++) {
    set(20, y, T.cobble);
    set(21, y, T.cobble);
  }
  // playtest1: every door is on its building's south face, where the house sprite draws it; the rest of the block is wall.
  for (let x = 20; x <= 21; x++) for (let y = 27; y < h - 1; y++) set(x, y, T.cobble);
  for (const [x, y] of TOWN_GATES) {
    set(x, y, T.cobble);
    if (x === 0) set(1, y, T.cobble);
    if (x === w - 1) set(w - 2, y, T.cobble);
  }
  shell(2, 2, 8, 5, 5, 6);
  stoop(5, 7);
  shell(12, 2, 6, 5, 14, 6);
  stoop(14, 7);
  shell(24, 2, 7, 5, 30, 6);
  stoop(30, 7);
  shell(33, 2, 5, 5, 34, 6);
  stoop(34, 7);
  shell(12, 8, 6, 4, 14, 11);
  shell(24, 8, 6, 4, 26, 11);
  shell(3, 8, 6, 4, 5, 11);
  shell(11, 22, 6, 5, 13, 26);
  shell(2, 15, 8, 5, 5, 19);
  shell(24, 15, 7, 5, 27, 19);
  shell(32, 15, 6, 5, 34, 19);
  shell(2, 22, 7, 5, 5, 26);
  shell(24, 22, 7, 5, 27, 26);
  shell(33, 22, 5, 5, 34, 26);
  shell(17, 17, 3, 4, 18, 20);
  shell(17, 23, 3, 4, 18, 26);
  stoop(34, 20);
  // The south lane in front of the south houses' doors.
  for (let x = 2; x < 38; x++) if (tiles[27 * w + x] === T.grass) set(x, 27, T.dirt);
  for (let y = 16; y <= 19; y++) for (let x = 12; x <= 16; x++) set(x, y, T.dirt);
  set(12, 17, T.grave);
  set(15, 17, T.grave);
  set(13, 19, T.grave);
  set(14, 18, T.stairD);
  for (let y = 14; y <= 16; y++) for (let x = 16; x <= 19; x++) set(x, y, T.water);
  set(16, 16, T.dirt);
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const x = 2 + ((i * 7) % 16);
    const y = 9 + (i % 2);
    if (tiles[y * w + x] === T.grass) set(x, y, T.tree);
  }
  set(11, 10, T.pump);
  set(11, 14, T.pump);
  set(9, 14, T.tree);
  for (const [tx, ty] of [
    [11, 7],
    [31, 7],
    [22, 7],
    [8, 14],
    [32, 13],
  ]) {
    if (tiles[ty * w + tx] === T.grass) set(tx, ty, T.tree);
  }
  for (let x = 34; x <= 37; x++) for (let y = 9; y <= 11; y++) set(x, y, T.cobble);
  const npcs: Npc[] = [
    { id: "undertaker", name: "Undertaker", x: 13 * TILE + 8, y: 18 * TILE + 8, coat: "#16161a", role: "undertaker" },
    { id: "zep", name: "Zeppelin Warden", x: 35 * TILE + 8, y: 10 * TILE, coat: "#4a4038", role: "zeppelin" },
    { id: "guard1", name: "Guard", x: 21 * TILE + 8, y: 18 * TILE + 8, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "guard2", name: "Bank Guard", x: 37 * TILE + 8, y: 10 * TILE + 8, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "guard3", name: "Casino Guard", x: 31 * TILE + 8, y: 21 * TILE + 8, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
  ];
  spreadProps(tiles, w, h, true);
  return { w, h, tiles, npcs };
}

/** The world boss stands and the road carts, on the vale grid (twice the old plan). */
const WORLD_CARTS: [number, number][] = [
  [64, 84],
  [40, 72],
  [104, 48],
  [80, 100],
  [60, 24],
  [68, 52],
];

/**
 * The vale. playtest1e [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1e bigger world]: 128x120, laid by the map writer
 * (tools/map-writer/gravewake_world.ts, phase 3: groves, roads, winding trails to every place, landmarks and trail caches
 * spread through each biome, a forest band round the edge). Every place sits at twice its old tile. Built once and
 * copied: the sim stamps the world's tiles (a boss's clearing) on its own copy.
 */
let worldMemo: { w: number; h: number; tiles: Uint8Array; entrances: Record<string, string>; npcs: Npc[]; landmarks: { x: number; y: number; kind: string }[]; caches: { x: number; y: number }[] } | null = null;
/** playtest1e: the vale's landmarks and caches as the map writer laid them (copies; the checks and the mini map read them). */
export function worldPlan() {
  if (!worldMemo) worldMemo = layWorld();
  const l = worldMemo;
  return { landmarks: l.landmarks.map((m) => ({ ...m })), caches: l.caches.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y })) };
}
function buildWorld() {
  if (!worldMemo) worldMemo = layWorld();
  const m = worldMemo;
  return { w: m.w, h: m.h, tiles: m.tiles.slice(), entrances: { ...m.entrances }, npcs: m.npcs.map((n) => ({ ...n })), landmarks: m.landmarks, caches: m.caches };
}

/** Tiles a wayrift's front may not walk through to reach the road net (the body rule: solidAt and blockedProp). */
const WAY_BLOCK = new Set<number>([T.tree, T.rock, T.pump, T.grave, T.wall, T.water, T.pool, T.ice, T.shelf, T.cauldron, T.moon]);
function layWorld() {
  const sites: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
  for (const d of DUNGEONS) if (!d.town) sites.push({ x: d.tx, y: d.ty });
  for (const r of RIFTS) sites.push({ x: r.tx, y: r.ty });
  for (const b of WORLD_BOSSES) sites.push({ x: b.tx, y: b.ty });
  for (const [x, y] of WORLD_CARTS) sites.push({ x, y });
  const npcs: Npc[] = [
    { id: "m1", name: "Road Merchant", x: 66 * TILE, y: 84 * TILE, coat: "#6a5040", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "m2", name: "Campfire Trader", x: 40 * TILE, y: 72 * TILE, coat: "#8a5a30", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "m3", name: "Waste Cart", x: 104 * TILE, y: 48 * TILE, coat: "#c4b48a", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "m4", name: "Ash Seller", x: 80 * TILE, y: 100 * TILE, coat: "#4a4038", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "m5", name: "Snow Peddler", x: 60 * TILE, y: 24 * TILE, coat: "#d0d8e4", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "m6", name: "Swamp Cart", x: 68 * TILE, y: 52 * TILE, coat: "#2a4030", role: "merchant" },
    { id: "g1", name: "Vale Watch", x: 68 * TILE, y: 80 * TILE, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "g2", name: "Waste Watch", x: 100 * TILE, y: 80 * TILE, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "g3", name: "Cinder Watch", x: 80 * TILE, y: 94 * TILE, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "g4", name: "Winter Watch", x: 68 * TILE, y: 16 * TILE, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
    { id: "g5", name: "Swamp Watch", x: 64 * TILE + 20, y: 60 * TILE, coat: "#2a3140", role: "guard" },
  ];
  for (const n of npcs) sites.push({ x: Math.floor(n.x / TILE), y: Math.floor(n.y / TILE) });
  sites.push({ x: WORLD_DOOR.x, y: WORLD_DOOR.y }, { x: HARVEST.lord.x, y: HARVEST.lord.y }, { x: KRAMPUSNACHT.spot.x, y: KRAMPUSNACHT.spot.y });
  const laid = gravewakeWorld(sites);
  const { w, h, tiles } = laid;
  const entrances: Record<string, string> = {};
  tiles[WORLD_DOOR.y * w + WORLD_DOOR.x] = T.door;
  for (let y = WORLD_DOOR.y + 1; y <= GATE.y; y++) tiles[y * w + WORLD_DOOR.x] = T.road;
  for (const d of DUNGEONS) {
    if (d.town) continue;
    if (d.tx < 0 || d.ty < 0 || d.tx >= w || d.ty >= h) continue;
    tiles[d.ty * w + d.tx] = T.stairD;
    entrances[`${d.tx},${d.ty}`] = d.id;
    const below = (d.ty + 1) * w + d.tx;
    if (d.ty + 1 < h && (tiles[below] === T.tree || tiles[below] === T.water || tiles[below] === T.rock)) tiles[below] = T.dirt;
  }
  spreadProps(tiles, w, h, false, 0);
  // the edge forest stays dense (spreadProps spaces props three apart); the roads run out through it
  edgeBand(tiles, w, h, 2, T.tree, (t) => t === T.road);
  for (const n of npcs) clearAround(tiles, w, h, n.x, n.y);
  // playtest1f [OWNER-APPROVED 2026-10-02: playtest1f portals]: the wayrifts stand on cleared ground, each front joined
  // to the road net (wayrifts.ts stampWayrifts: it cuts a trail only where the writer left none).
  stampWayrifts(tiles, w, h, { road: T.road, trail: T.dirt, tree: T.tree, rock: T.rock, pump: T.pump, water: T.water, ice: T.ice }, (x, y) => WORLD_DRESS[worldBiome(x, y)].ground, (t) => !WAY_BLOCK.has(t));
  return { w, h, tiles, entrances, npcs, landmarks: laid.landmarks.map((l) => ({ x: l.x, y: l.y, kind: l.kind })), caches: laid.caches };
}

function carveFloor(dungeon: string, floor: number, floors: number, pocket: boolean) {
  const w = pocket ? 18 : 28;
  const h = pocket ? 12 : 18;
  const tiles = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  tiles.fill(T.wall);
  const rng = mulberry(dungeon.length * 100 + floor * 17 + 3);
  const rooms: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];
  const count = pocket ? 3 : 6;
  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
    const rw = 4 + Math.floor(rng() * 3);
    const rh = 3 + Math.floor(rng() * 3);
    const rx = 1 + Math.floor(rng() * Math.max(1, w - rw - 2));
    const ry = 1 + Math.floor(rng() * Math.max(1, h - rh - 2));
    rooms.push({ x: rx, y: ry, w: rw, h: rh });
    for (let y = ry; y < ry + rh && y < h - 1; y++) for (let x = rx; x < rx + rw && x < w - 1; x++) tiles[y * w + x] = T.floor;
  }
  rooms.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);
  for (let i = 0; i < rooms.length - 1; i++) {
    let x = Math.min(w - 2, rooms[i].x + 1);
    let y = Math.min(h - 2, rooms[i].y + 1);
    const tx = Math.min(w - 2, rooms[i + 1].x + 1);
    const ty = Math.min(h - 2, rooms[i + 1].y + 1);
    while (x !== tx) {
      tiles[y * w + x] = T.floor;
      x += Math.sign(tx - x);
    }
    while (y !== ty) {
      tiles[y * w + x] = T.floor;
      y += Math.sign(ty - y);
    }
  }
  const left = rooms[0];
  const right = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
  const upX = Math.min(w - 2, left.x + 1);
  const upY = Math.min(h - 2, left.y + 1);
  tiles[upY * w + upX] = T.stairU;
  if (floor < floors) {
    const dx = Math.min(w - 2, right.x + 1);
    const dy = Math.min(h - 2, right.y + 1);
    tiles[dy * w + dx] = T.stairD;
  }
  const pool = rooms[Math.min(rooms.length - 1, 1)];
  const px = Math.min(w - 2, pool.x + 1);
  const py = Math.min(h - 2, pool.y + 1);
  if (tiles[py * w + px] === T.floor) tiles[py * w + px] = T.pool;
  const boneX = Math.min(w - 2, px + 1);
  if (tiles[py * w + boneX] === T.floor) tiles[py * w + boneX] = T.bone;
  const chestRoom = rooms[Math.min(rooms.length - 1, 2)];
  const cx = Math.min(w - 2, chestRoom.x + 2);
  const cy = Math.min(h - 2, chestRoom.y + 1);
  if (tiles[cy * w + cx] === T.floor) tiles[cy * w + cx] = T.chest;
  if (dungeon === "chapel") {
    const mid = Math.floor(h / 2);
    for (let x = 2; x < w - 2; x++) if (tiles[mid * w + x] !== T.stairD && tiles[mid * w + x] !== T.stairU) tiles[mid * w + x] = T.road;
  }
  // Secrets go in last, into rock only, so every room above stays where it was.
  const feats = placeFeats(tiles, w, h, dungeon, floor, floors, pocket);
  // Traps are an overlay on plain floor: tiles and foe spawns stay exactly as they were.
  const spawns = [w - 4, 6, 14].map((px) => findOpen(tiles, w, h, px, feats.hidden)).map((p) => p.y * w + p.x);
  feats.traps = placeTraps(tiles, w, h, dungeon, floor, floors, pocket, feats, spawns);
  // A captive is an overlay too: a body on a floor tile, after the traps, touching nothing.
  const captive = pocket ? null : placeCaptive(tiles, w, h, dungeon, floor, floors, feats, spawns);
  if (captive) feats.captive = captive;
  // A mimic is an overlay on the floor's plain chest: the chest tile stays until the mimic wakes.
  const mimic = mimicChest(tiles, w, dungeon, floor, floors, pocket, feats);
  if (mimic) feats.mimic = mimic;
  const fog = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  return { tiles, w, h, fog, feats };
}

/** Map writer: one generated floor, with the game's own secrets, traps, captive, and mimic placed on it. */
function carveGen(def: DungeonDef, floor: number) {
  const { map, feats } = gravewakeFloor(def.id, `${MAP_WRITER.seed}:${def.id}`, floor, def.floors, def.level);
  return { tiles: map.tiles, w: map.w, h: map.h, fog: new Uint8Array(map.w * map.h), feats, gen: map };
}

/** Map writer: a rift zone's pocket for one day-night cycle. Trash from the rift tile's own families. */
function carveRift(def: DungeonDef, cycle: number) {
  const roster = familiesFor(def.tx, def.ty, "world").map((m) => ({ id: m.id }));
  const { map, feats } = gravewakeRift(def.id, riftSeed(MAP_WRITER.seed, def.tx, def.ty, cycle), def.level, roster);
  return { tiles: map.tiles, w: map.w, h: map.h, fog: new Uint8Array(map.w * map.h), feats, gen: map };
}

function biomeTile(x: number, y: number): number {
  return WORLD_DRESS[worldBiome(x, y)].ground;
}

function clearAround(tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, w: number, h: number, px: number, py: number) {
  const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
  const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
  for (let y = ty - 2; y <= ty + 1; y++) {
    for (let x = tx - 1; x <= tx + 1; x++) {
      if (x < 1 || y < 1 || x >= w - 1 || y >= h - 1) continue;
      const i = y * w + x;
      const t = tiles[i];
      if (t !== T.tree && t !== T.rock && t !== T.pump && t !== T.grave) continue;
      tiles[i] = biomeTile(x, y);
    }
  }
}

function spreadProps(tiles: Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>, w: number, h: number, town: boolean, edge = 3) {
  const propish = (t: number) => t === T.tree || t === T.rock || t === T.pump;
  const hard = (t: number) => t === T.wall || t === T.door || t === T.water || t === T.pool || t === T.ice || t === T.stairD || t === T.stairU || t === T.chest;
  const blocksGrave = (t: number) => t === T.wall || t === T.water || t === T.pool;
  const restore = (x: number, y: number) => (town ? T.grass : biomeTile(x, y));
  // playtest1e: the vale keeps its edge forest (edge 0); the town still clears three tiles in from its fence.
  const margin = edge;
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const i = y * w + x;
      const grave = tiles[i] === T.grave;
      if (!propish(tiles[i]) && !grave) continue;
      if (x < margin || y < margin || x >= w - margin || y >= h - margin) {
        tiles[i] = grave && town ? T.dirt : restore(x, y);
        continue;
      }
      let bad = false;
      for (let yy = Math.max(0, y - 1); yy <= Math.min(h - 1, y + 1) && !bad; yy++) {
        for (let xx = Math.max(0, x - 1); xx <= Math.min(w - 1, x + 1); xx++) {
          if (xx === x && yy === y) continue;
          const other = tiles[yy * w + xx];
          if (grave ? blocksGrave(other) : hard(other)) bad = true;
        }
      }
      if (bad) {
        tiles[i] = grave && town ? T.dirt : restore(x, y);
        continue;
      }
      if (grave) {
        for (let yy = Math.max(0, y - 1); yy <= Math.min(h - 1, y + 1) && !bad; yy++) {
          for (let xx = Math.max(0, x - 1); xx <= Math.min(w - 1, x + 1); xx++) {
            if (xx === x && yy === y) continue;
            if (propish(tiles[yy * w + xx])) bad = true;
          }
        }
        if (bad) tiles[i] = town ? T.dirt : restore(x, y);
        continue;
      }
      for (let yy = Math.max(0, y - 2); yy <= y && !bad; yy++) {
        for (let xx = Math.max(0, x - 2); xx <= Math.min(w - 1, yy === y ? x - 1 : x + 2); xx++) {
          if (propish(tiles[yy * w + xx]) || tiles[yy * w + xx] === T.grave) bad = true;
        }
      }
      if (bad) tiles[i] = restore(x, y);
    }
  }
}
